`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/AbstractCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.Check;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

/**
 * 反作弊检查抽象基类 (AbstractCheck)
 * 封装通用配置读取、VL 告警触发与回退支持
 */
public abstract class AbstractCheck implements Check {

    protected final ArgusACPlugin plugin;
    protected final String name;
    protected final CheckType type;

    protected boolean enabled;
    protected int maxVl;
    protected boolean setbackEnabled;
    protected int setbackMinVl;
    protected String setbackMode;

    public AbstractCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin, String name, CheckType type) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.name = name;
        this.type = type;
    }

    @Override
    public String getName() {
        return name;
    }

    @Override
    public CheckType getType() {
        return type;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isEnabled() {
        return enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) {
        this.enabled = enabled;
        plugin.getConfig().set("checks." + name + ".enabled", enabled);
        plugin.saveConfig();
    }

    @Override
    public int getMaxVl() {
        return maxVl;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSetbackEnabled() {
        return setbackEnabled;
    }

    @Override
    public int getSetbackMinVl() {
        return setbackMinVl;
    }

    @Override
    public String getSetbackMode() {
        return setbackMode != null ? setbackMode : plugin.getSetbackManager().getDefaultMode();
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfig();
        String path = "checks." + name + ".";
        this.enabled = config.getBoolean(path + "enabled", true);
        this.maxVl = config.getInt(path + "max-vl", 15);

        // 回退模块配置覆写 (回退作为默认处罚手段，默认 min-vl 为全局 default-min-vl: 1)
        int defaultMinVl = config.getInt("setback.default-min-vl", 1);
        String setbackPath = "setback.per-check." + name + ".";
        this.setbackEnabled = config.getBoolean(setbackPath + "enabled", true);
        this.setbackMinVl = config.getInt(setbackPath + "min-vl", defaultMinVl);
        this.setbackMode = config.getString(setbackPath + "mode", plugin.getSetbackManager().getDefaultMode()).toUpperCase();
    }

    /**
     * 标记玩家作弊行为并递交违规管理器处理
     *
     * @param player 违规玩家
     * @param vl 增加的 VL 点数
     * @param details 违规指标详情
     */
    protected void flag(Player player, int vl, String details) {
        plugin.getViolationManager().handleViolation(player, this, vl, details, null);
    }

    /**
     * 标记移动作弊行为并携带移动事件 (支持 SOFT 模式原地修正终点)
     *
     * @param player 违规玩家
     * @param vl 增加的 VL 点数
     * @param details 违规指标详情
     * @param moveEvent 移动事件
     */
    protected void flag(Player player, int vl, String details, org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent moveEvent) {
        plugin.getViolationManager().handleViolation(player, this, vl, details, moveEvent);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/CheckManager.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.Check;
import com.example.argusac.check.combat.AutoClickerCheck;
import com.example.argusac.check.combat.KillAuraCheck;
import com.example.argusac.check.combat.ReachCheck;
import com.example.argusac.check.combat.VelocityCheck;
import com.example.argusac.check.movement.ElytraFlyCheck;
import com.example.argusac.check.movement.FlyCheck;
import com.example.argusac.check.movement.InventoryMoveCheck;
import com.example.argusac.check.movement.NoFallCheck;
import com.example.argusac.check.movement.SpeedCheck;
import com.example.argusac.check.packet.BadPacketsCheck;
import com.example.argusac.check.world.FastBreakCheck;
import com.example.argusac.check.world.NukerCheck;
import com.example.argusac.check.world.ScaffoldCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.*;

/**
 * 检测模块集中管理器 (CheckManager)
 * 负责各检测类的实例化注册、生命周期管理与批量热重载
 */
public class CheckManager {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;
    private final Map<String, Check> checkMap = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    private SpeedCheck speedCheck;
    private FlyCheck flyCheck;
    private NoFallCheck noFallCheck;
    private InventoryMoveCheck inventoryMoveCheck;
    private ElytraFlyCheck elytraFlyCheck;

    private ReachCheck reachCheck;
    private AutoClickerCheck autoClickerCheck;
    private KillAuraCheck killAuraCheck;
    private VelocityCheck velocityCheck;

    private FastBreakCheck fastBreakCheck;
    private NukerCheck nukerCheck;
    private ScaffoldCheck scaffoldCheck;

    private BadPacketsCheck badPacketsCheck;

    public CheckManager(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.registerAll();
    }

    private void registerAll() {
        // 移动类
        register(speedCheck = new SpeedCheck(plugin));
        register(flyCheck = new FlyCheck(plugin));
        register(noFallCheck = new NoFallCheck(plugin));
        register(inventoryMoveCheck = new InventoryMoveCheck(plugin));
        register(elytraFlyCheck = new ElytraFlyCheck(plugin));

        // 战斗类
        register(reachCheck = new ReachCheck(plugin));
        register(autoClickerCheck = new AutoClickerCheck(plugin));
        register(killAuraCheck = new KillAuraCheck(plugin));
        register(velocityCheck = new VelocityCheck(plugin));

        // 世界交互类
        register(fastBreakCheck = new FastBreakCheck(plugin));
        register(nukerCheck = new NukerCheck(plugin));
        register(scaffoldCheck = new ScaffoldCheck(plugin));

        // 数据包类
        register(badPacketsCheck = new BadPacketsCheck(plugin));

        reloadAll();
    }

    private void register(Check check) {
        checkMap.put(check.getName().toLowerCase(), check);
    }

    public Check getCheck(String name) {
        if (name == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return checkMap.get(name.toLowerCase());
    }

    public Collection<Check> getAllChecks() {
        return checkMap.values();
    }

    public void reloadAll() {
        for (Check check : checkMap.values()) {
            check.reloadConfig();
        }
    }

    // Typed Getters
    public SpeedCheck getSpeedCheck() { return speedCheck; }
    public FlyCheck getFlyCheck() { return flyCheck; }
    public NoFallCheck getNoFallCheck() { return noFallCheck; }
    public InventoryMoveCheck getInventoryMoveCheck() { return inventoryMoveCheck; }
    public ElytraFlyCheck getElytraFlyCheck() { return elytraFlyCheck; }

    public ReachCheck getReachCheck() { return reachCheck; }
    public AutoClickerCheck getAutoClickerCheck() { return autoClickerCheck; }
    public KillAuraCheck getKillAuraCheck() { return killAuraCheck; }
    public VelocityCheck getVelocityCheck() { return velocityCheck; }

    public FastBreakCheck getFastBreakCheck() { return fastBreakCheck; }
    public NukerCheck getNukerCheck() { return nukerCheck; }
    public ScaffoldCheck getScaffoldCheck() { return scaffoldCheck; }

    public BadPacketsCheck getBadPacketsCheck() { return badPacketsCheck; }

    /**
     * 集中判断玩家是否处于鞘翅滑翔状态，用于 Fly, Speed, NoFall 等移动检测模块的显式互斥
     * 当为 true 时，FlyCheck、SpeedCheck、NoFallCheck 必须直接跳过判定，统一移交 ElytraFly 模块处理
     *
     * @param player 目标玩家
     * @param data 玩家数据
     * @return 是否处于滑翔互斥状态
     */
    public boolean isGlidingExempt(Player player, PlayerData data) {
        if (player == null || data == null) {
            return false;
        }
        return com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().isGliding(player) || data.isElytraGliding();
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/combat/AutoClickerCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check.combat;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.check.AbstractCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import com.example.argusac.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Deque;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 自动连点器检测 (AutoClickerCheck)
 * 监控每秒点击数 (CPS) 以及点击间隔时间差的标准差 (Standard Deviation)
 * 精确识别人类难以达到的超高频连点与脚本宏极其均匀的无抖动点击
 */
public class AutoClickerCheck extends AbstractCheck {

    private int maxCps;
    private double minStdDeviation;

    public AutoClickerCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "AutoClicker", CheckType.COMBAT);
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        super.reloadConfig();
        String path = "checks." + name + ".";
        this.maxCps = plugin.getConfig().getInt(path + "max-cps", 18);
        this.minStdDeviation = plugin.getConfig().getDouble(path + "min-std-deviation", 4.5);
    }

    /**
     * 在玩家左键点击交互或攻击实体时记录点击频率
     */
    public void handleClick(Player player) {
        if (!enabled || player == null) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (data == null || data.isInGracePeriod()) {
            return;
        }

        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // 过滤单次物理点击由 Bukkit 同时触发 Interact 与 Damage 事件导致的短时间 (<15ms) 重复计数
        if (now - data.getLastClickTimestamp() < 15L) {
            return;
        }
        data.setLastClickTimestamp(now);

        Deque<Long> clicks = data.getClickTimestamps();
        clicks.addLast(now);

        // 清理 1000 毫秒 (1秒) 之前的过期点击
        while (!clicks.isEmpty() && now - clicks.peekFirst() > 1000L) {
            clicks.pollFirst();
        }

        int currentCps = clicks.size();

        // 1. 超过最大 CPS 极限阈值
        if (currentCps > maxCps) {
            flag(player, 1, String.format("High CPS=%d, max=%d", currentCps, maxCps));
            return;
        }

        // 2. 采样 10 次以上点击，计算点击时间间隔的一致性 (宏连点器检测)
        if (clicks.size() >= 12) {
            List<Long> timeList = new ArrayList<>(clicks);
            List<Long> intervals = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 1; i < timeList.size(); i++) {
                intervals.add(timeList.get(i) - timeList.get(i - 1));
            }

            double stdDev = MathUtil.getStandardDeviation(intervals);
            // CPS > 12 且时间间隔几乎完全机械一致 (方差过小)
            if (currentCps > 12 && stdDev < minStdDeviation) {
                flag(player, 1, String.format("Robot Click CPS=%d, stdDev=%.2f", currentCps, stdDev));
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/combat/KillAuraCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check.combat;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.check.AbstractCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import com.example.argusac.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.FluidCollisionMode;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.RayTraceResult;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * 杀戮光环检测 (KillAuraCheck)
 * 检测攻击脱离视角角度 (Angle)、快速多目标切换 (Switch) 以及隔墙打击 (RayTrace)
 */
public class KillAuraCheck extends AbstractCheck {

    private double maxAngle;
    private boolean raytraceBlocks;
    private long targetSwitchMs;

    public KillAuraCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "KillAura", CheckType.COMBAT);
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        super.reloadConfig();
        String path = "checks." + name + ".";
        this.maxAngle = plugin.getConfig().getDouble(path + "max-angle", 68.0);
        this.raytraceBlocks = plugin.getConfig().getBoolean(path + "raytrace-blocks", true);
        this.targetSwitchMs = plugin.getConfig().getLong(path + "target-switch-ms", 80L);
    }

    public void handleAttack(Player attacker, Entity victim, EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || attacker == null || !(victim instanceof LivingEntity livingVictim)) {
            return;
        }

        // 死亡状态攻击与攻击自身漏洞拦截
        if (attacker.isDead() || livingVictim.isDead()) {
            flag(attacker, 3, "Attack while dead");
            event.setCancelled(true);
            return;
        }
        if (attacker.equals(livingVictim)) {
            flag(attacker, 3, "Self damage packet exploit");
            event.setCancelled(true);
            return;
        }

        if (attacker.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || attacker.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(attacker);
        if (data == null || data.isInGracePeriod()) {
            return;
        }

        Location eyeLoc = attacker.getEyeLocation();
        Location victimEye = livingVictim.getEyeLocation();

        if (eyeLoc.getWorld() != victimEye.getWorld()) {
            return;
        }

        // 1. 视角夹角检测 (Angle Check)
        Vector eyeDirection = eyeLoc.getDirection();
        Vector toTarget = victimEye.toVector().subtract(eyeLoc.toVector());
        if (toTarget.lengthSquared() < 0.01) {
            return;
        }

        double distance = eyeLoc.distance(victimEye);
        double angle = MathUtil.getAngleBetweenVectors(eyeDirection, toTarget);

        // 仅在距离大于 1.0 格时检测极限脱节夹角 (贴脸时因碰撞箱宽大正常挥刀夹角变化剧烈)
        if (distance > 1.0 && angle > maxAngle) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(attacker, 2, String.format("Impossible Angle angle=%.1f deg, max=%.1f", angle, maxAngle));
            return;
        }

        // 2. 准星光线脱靶命中检测 (Silent Aim / Aimbot RayTrace Miss)
        int ping = Math.max(0, com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().getPing(attacker));
        double pingExpansion = Math.min(0.50, (ping / 100.0) * 0.12);
        if (distance > 1.2 && angle > 55.0) {
            com.example.argusac.compat.CompatBoundingBox expandedBox = com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter()
                    .getEntityBoundingBox(livingVictim)
                    .expand(0.40 + pingExpansion);
            if (expandedBox.distanceTo(eyeLoc) > distance + 1.0) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
                flag(attacker, 2, String.format("Aimbot Ray Miss angle=%.1f, ping=%d", angle, ping));
                return;
            }
        }

        // 3. 隔墙穿透攻击检测 (RayTrace Blocks)
        if (raytraceBlocks) {
            if (distance > 1.2) {
                RayTraceResult rayResult = eyeLoc.getWorld().rayTraceBlocks(
                        eyeLoc,
                        toTarget.clone().normalize(),
                        distance - 0.5,
                        FluidCollisionMode.NEVER,
                        true
                );
                if (rayResult != null && rayResult.getHitBlock() != null && rayResult.getHitBlock().getType().isOccluding()) {
                    event.setCancelled(true);
                    flag(attacker, 2, "Attack Through Wall: " + rayResult.getHitBlock().getType());
                    return;
                }
            }
        }

        // 4. 极速切换不同目标检测 (Multi-Target Switch)
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        UUID lastTarget = data.getLastAttackedTarget();
        long lastAttack = data.getLastAttackTime();

        if (lastTarget != null && !lastTarget.equals(victim.getUniqueId())) {
            long switchInterval = now - lastAttack;
            if (switchInterval < targetSwitchMs) {
                flag(attacker, 1, String.format("Rapid Switch interval=%dms", switchInterval));
            }
        }

        data.setLastAttackedTarget(victim.getUniqueId());
        data.setLastAttackTime(now);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/combat/ReachCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check.combat;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.check.AbstractCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;

/**
 * 攻击距离检测 (ReachCheck)
 * 检测超出合法范围的超距攻击，综合考虑玩家延迟补偿与目标碰撞箱边缘
 */
public class ReachCheck extends AbstractCheck {

    private double maxReach;
    private double maxPingReach;

    public ReachCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Reach", CheckType.COMBAT);
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        super.reloadConfig();
        String path = "checks." + name + ".";
        this.maxReach = plugin.getConfig().getDouble(path + "max-reach", 3.10);
        this.maxPingReach = plugin.getConfig().getDouble(path + "max-ping-reach", 3.50);
    }

    public void handleAttack(Player attacker, Entity victim, EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || attacker == null || !(victim instanceof LivingEntity)) {
            return;
        }

        // 创造模式合法攻击距离可达 5.0，豁免
        if (attacker.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || attacker.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(attacker);
        if (data == null || data.isInGracePeriod()) {
            return;
        }

        Location eyeLocation = attacker.getEyeLocation();
        if (eyeLocation.getWorld() != victim.getWorld()) {
            return;
        }

        // 精确计算视线起点到目标跨版本碰撞箱 (CompatBoundingBox) 最短物理距离
        com.example.argusac.compat.CompatBoundingBox box = com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().getEntityBoundingBox(victim);
        double distanceToBox = box.distanceTo(eyeLocation);

        int ping = Math.max(0, com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().getPing(attacker));
        // 延迟补偿：每 100ms 延迟放宽约 0.08 格
        double dynamicLimit = maxReach + Math.min(0.40, (ping / 100.0) * 0.08);
        dynamicLimit = Math.min(dynamicLimit, maxPingReach);

        if (distanceToBox > dynamicLimit) {
            // 超距攻击直接取消本次伤害事件生效
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(attacker, 1, String.format("dist=%.2f, limit=%.2f, ping=%d", distanceToBox, dynamicLimit, ping));
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/combat/VelocityCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check.combat;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.check.AbstractCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerVelocityEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

/**
 * 击退速度检测 (VelocityCheck / Anti-KB)
 * 检测玩家受到外部击退或爆炸作用力时，非法消除垂直或水平击退位移的作弊行为
 */
public class VelocityCheck extends AbstractCheck {

    private double verticalRatio;
    private double horizontalRatio;

    public VelocityCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Velocity", CheckType.COMBAT);
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        super.reloadConfig();
        String path = "checks." + name + ".";
        this.verticalRatio = plugin.getConfig().getDouble(path + "vertical-ratio", 0.85);
        this.horizontalRatio = plugin.getConfig().getDouble(path + "horizontal-ratio", 0.80);
    }

    /**
     * 服务端向客户端派发物理击退速度
     */
    public void handleVelocity(Player player, PlayerVelocityEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (data == null || data.isInGracePeriod()) {
            return;
        }

        Vector velocity = event.getVelocity();
        // 忽略微小的自然扰动速度
        if (velocity.lengthSquared() < 0.05) {
            return;
        }

        data.setExpectedVelocity(velocity);
    }

    /**
     * 在后续移动事件中校验玩家是否如期产生物理位移响应
     */
    public void handleMove(Player player, PlayerData data, PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null || data == null) {
            return;
        }

        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR
                || player.isInsideVehicle() || player.isFlying()) {
            data.setVelocityHandled(true);
            return;
        }

        if (data.isVelocityHandled()) {
            return;
        }

        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        // 如果距离收到速度已经超过 600ms，说明该次击退已过期
        if (now - data.getLastVelocityReceivedTime() > 600L) {
            data.setVelocityHandled(true);
            return;
        }

        Vector expected = data.getExpectedVelocity();
        if (expected == null) {
            data.setVelocityHandled(true);
            return;
        }

        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to = event.getTo();
        if (to == null) {
            return;
        }

        double deltaY = to.getY() - from.getY();

        // 校验是否头顶撞墙或被方块阻挡
        if (hasBlockAbove(from)) {
            data.setVelocityHandled(true);
            return;
        }

        // 垂直击退检测：若预期向上受到击退 (expected.getY() > 0.2)，但实际位移几乎为 0
        if (expected.getY() > 0.20) {
            double expectedY = expected.getY() * verticalRatio;
            if (deltaY < 0.01 && deltaY < expectedY) {
                flag(player, 2, String.format("Anti-KB deltaY=%.2f, expected=%.2f", deltaY, expected.getY()), event);
            }
        }

        // 水平击退检测：若预期有水平击退速度，但实际水平位移极小
        double expectedHorizSq = expected.getX() * expected.getX() + expected.getZ() * expected.getZ();
        if (expectedHorizSq > 0.04) {
            double dx = to.getX() - from.getX();
            double dz = to.getZ() - from.getZ();
            double actualHoriz = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
            double expectedHoriz = Math.sqrt(expectedHorizSq) * horizontalRatio;
            if (actualHoriz < 0.01 && actualHoriz < expectedHoriz) {
                flag(player, 1, String.format("Anti-KB(H) horiz=%.3f, expected=%.3f", actualHoriz, Math.sqrt(expectedHorizSq)), event);
            }
        }

        data.setVelocityHandled(true);
    }

    private boolean hasBlockAbove(Location loc) {
        Block head = loc.clone().add(0, 1.8, 0).getBlock();
        Block ceiling = loc.clone().add(0, 2.0, 0).getBlock();
        return head.getType().isSolid() || ceiling.getType().isSolid()
                || head.getType() == Material.COBWEB || ceiling.getType() == Material.COBWEB;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/movement/ElytraFlyCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check.movement;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.Check;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.check.AbstractCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.ElytraData;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import com.example.argusac.model.VelocitySourceType;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.block.Action;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityToggleGlideEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerVelocityEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.inventory.meta.Damageable;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

/**
 * 鞘翅滑翔与非法飞行集中检测模块 (ElytraFlyCheck)
 * <p>
 * 深度解析 Minecraft 鞘翅滑翔物理动力学，作为独立检测项完整接入 ArgusAC 架构体系。
 * 包含 8 大独立可配置子检测项：
 * 1. ElytraSpeed: 滑翔水平速度上限（综合俯冲 Pitch、烟花推进与外部推力）
 * 2. ElytraAscend: 无推进异常上升（严密区分烟花/激流/气泡柱/风弹）
 * 3. ElytraHover: 空中悬停滞空（容忍起飞缓冲、贴墙碰撞与缓降/悬浮药水）
 * 4. ElytraFirework: 烟花消耗与正向加速不匹配反向作弊判定
 * 5. ElytraGlideState: 滑翔装备与耐久一致性（防无鞘翅/破损滑翔状态伪造）
 * 6. ElytraTimer: 滑翔 Tick 速率异常（滑动窗口平均采样联动服务器 TPS）
 * 7. ElytraFlip: 高频开关滑翔状态刷初速度（GlideFlip 作弊）
 * 8. ElytraNoFall: 触地瞬间开关滑翔免摔滥用判定
 */
public class ElytraFlyCheck extends AbstractCheck {

    // 独立子检测项实例
    private final ElytraSubCheck speedSubCheck;
    private final ElytraSubCheck ascendSubCheck;
    private final ElytraSubCheck hoverSubCheck;
    private final ElytraSubCheck fireworkSubCheck;
    private final ElytraSubCheck glideStateSubCheck;
    private final ElytraSubCheck timerSubCheck;
    private final ElytraSubCheck flipSubCheck;
    private final ElytraSubCheck noFallSubCheck;

    // 全局豁免窗口与环境参数
    private long fireworkBoostDurationMs;
    private long riptideDurationMs;
    private long externalVelocityDurationMs;
    private long takeoffGraceDurationMs;
    private double minServerTps;

    // 1. ElytraSpeed 参数
    private double speedMaxUnboosted;
    private double speedMaxBoosted;
    private double speedPitchDiveMultiplier;

    // 2. ElytraAscend 参数
    private double ascendMaxVertical;
    private int ascendTicksThreshold;

    // 3. ElytraHover 参数
    private double hoverMaxHorizontalSpeed;
    private double hoverMaxVerticalSpeed;
    private int hoverTicksThreshold;

    // 4. ElytraFirework 参数
    private double fireworkMinUnpropelledAccel;
    private int fireworkContinuousHighSpeedTicks;

    // 5. ElytraGlideState 参数
    private boolean glideStateCheckDurability;

    // 6. ElytraTimer 参数
    private int timerSampleWindowSize;
    private double timerMinAverageIntervalMs;

    // 7. ElytraFlip 参数
    private long flipWindowMs;
    private int flipMaxToggles;

    // 8. ElytraNoFall 参数
    private float noFallMinFallDistance;
    private double noFallGroundProximityDistance;

    public ElytraFlyCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "ElytraFly", CheckType.MOVEMENT);

        // 初始化 8 个子检测项实体，每个子项持有独立名称、回退模式与 min-vl 状态
        this.speedSubCheck = new ElytraSubCheck(this, "ElytraSpeed");
        this.ascendSubCheck = new ElytraSubCheck(this, "ElytraAscend");
        this.hoverSubCheck = new ElytraSubCheck(this, "ElytraHover");
        this.fireworkSubCheck = new ElytraSubCheck(this, "ElytraFirework");
        this.glideStateSubCheck = new ElytraSubCheck(this, "ElytraGlideState");
        this.timerSubCheck = new ElytraSubCheck(this, "ElytraTimer");
        this.flipSubCheck = new ElytraSubCheck(this, "ElytraFlip");
        this.noFallSubCheck = new ElytraSubCheck(this, "ElytraNoFall");
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        super.reloadConfig();
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfig();
        String basePath = "checks.ElytraFly.";

        // 全局豁免与环境配置
        this.fireworkBoostDurationMs = config.getLong(basePath + "exemptions.firework-boost-duration-ms", 1500L);
        this.riptideDurationMs = config.getLong(basePath + "exemptions.riptide-duration-ms", 2000L);
        this.externalVelocityDurationMs = config.getLong(basePath + "exemptions.external-velocity-duration-ms", 1200L);
        this.takeoffGraceDurationMs = config.getLong(basePath + "exemptions.takeoff-grace-duration-ms", 1000L);
        this.minServerTps = config.getDouble(basePath + "exemptions.min-server-tps", 17.5);

        // 子项一: ElytraSpeed
        this.speedSubCheck.reload(
                config.getBoolean(basePath + "speed.enabled", true),
                config.getInt(basePath + "speed.min-vl", 3),
                config.getString(basePath + "speed.setback-mode", "HARD")
        );
        this.speedMaxUnboosted = config.getDouble(basePath + "speed.max-unboosted-speed", 1.45);
        this.speedMaxBoosted = config.getDouble(basePath + "speed.max-boosted-speed", 2.60);
        this.speedPitchDiveMultiplier = config.getDouble(basePath + "speed.pitch-dive-multiplier", 0.55);

        // 子项二: ElytraAscend
        this.ascendSubCheck.reload(
                config.getBoolean(basePath + "ascend.enabled", true),
                config.getInt(basePath + "ascend.min-vl", 3),
                config.getString(basePath + "ascend.setback-mode", "HARD")
        );
        this.ascendMaxVertical = config.getDouble(basePath + "ascend.max-vertical-ascent", 0.15);
        this.ascendTicksThreshold = config.getInt(basePath + "ascend.ascend-ticks-threshold", 4);

        // 子项三: ElytraHover
        this.hoverSubCheck.reload(
                config.getBoolean(basePath + "hover.enabled", true),
                config.getInt(basePath + "hover.min-vl", 3),
                config.getString(basePath + "hover.setback-mode", "HARD")
        );
        this.hoverMaxHorizontalSpeed = config.getDouble(basePath + "hover.max-horizontal-speed", 0.08);
        this.hoverMaxVerticalSpeed = config.getDouble(basePath + "hover.max-vertical-speed", 0.05);
        this.hoverTicksThreshold = config.getInt(basePath + "hover.hover-ticks-threshold", 15);

        // 子项四: ElytraFirework
        this.fireworkSubCheck.reload(
                config.getBoolean(basePath + "firework.enabled", true),
                config.getInt(basePath + "firework.min-vl", 3),
                config.getString(basePath + "firework.setback-mode", "HARD")
        );
        this.fireworkMinUnpropelledAccel = config.getDouble(basePath + "firework.min-unpropelled-accel", 0.12);
        this.fireworkContinuousHighSpeedTicks = config.getInt(basePath + "firework.continuous-high-speed-ticks", 50);

        // 子项五: ElytraGlideState (伪造鞘翅状态默认 min-vl 调低至 1，置信度极高)
        this.glideStateSubCheck.reload(
                config.getBoolean(basePath + "glide-state.enabled", true),
                config.getInt(basePath + "glide-state.min-vl", 1),
                config.getString(basePath + "glide-state.setback-mode", "HARD")
        );
        this.glideStateCheckDurability = config.getBoolean(basePath + "glide-state.check-chestplate-durability", true);

        // 子项六: ElytraTimer
        this.timerSubCheck.reload(
                config.getBoolean(basePath + "timer.enabled", true),
                config.getInt(basePath + "timer.min-vl", 3),
                config.getString(basePath + "timer.setback-mode", "SOFT")
        );
        this.timerSampleWindowSize = config.getInt(basePath + "timer.sample-window-size", 20);
        this.timerMinAverageIntervalMs = config.getDouble(basePath + "timer.min-average-interval-ms", 44.0);

        // 子项七: ElytraFlip
        this.flipSubCheck.reload(
                config.getBoolean(basePath + "flip.enabled", true),
                config.getInt(basePath + "flip.min-vl", 3),
                config.getString(basePath + "flip.setback-mode", "SOFT")
        );
        this.flipWindowMs = config.getLong(basePath + "flip.window-ms", 1000L);
        this.flipMaxToggles = config.getInt(basePath + "flip.max-toggles", 4);

        // 子项八: ElytraNoFall
        this.noFallSubCheck.reload(
                config.getBoolean(basePath + "nofall.enabled", true),
                config.getInt(basePath + "nofall.min-vl", 3),
                config.getString(basePath + "nofall.setback-mode", "HARD")
        );
        this.noFallMinFallDistance = (float) config.getDouble(basePath + "nofall.min-fall-distance", 3.0);
        this.noFallGroundProximityDistance = config.getDouble(basePath + "nofall.ground-proximity-distance", 1.8);
    }

    // ==============================================================================
    // 核心移动事件入口 (PlayerMoveEvent)
    // ==============================================================================

    /**
     * 处理滑翔状态下的核心移动判定
     */
    public void handleMove(Player player, PlayerData data, PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null || data == null) {
            return;
        }

        ElytraData elytraData = data.getElytraData();

        // 极限性能优化：非滑翔玩家在第一行立即返回，零计算损耗
        if (!player.isGliding() && !elytraData.isGliding()) {
            return;
        }

        // 创造/旁观、允许原版飞行或乘坐载具豁免
        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR
                || player.isFlying() || player.getAllowFlight() || player.isInsideVehicle()) {
            elytraData.resetGlideState();
            return;
        }

        // 回退自免疫期保护
        if (data.isSetbackImmune()) {
            return;
        }

        // 服务器负载与卡顿保护：当 TPS 低于容忍阈值时，整体暂停移动检测，防止由于服务端卡顿批处理引发误判
        if (getRecentTps() < minServerTps) {
            return;
        }

        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to = event.getTo();
        if (to == null || from.getWorld() != to.getWorld()) {
            return;
        }

        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // 检查 Paper 自动落地停止滑翔的合法边界
        if (!player.isGliding() && elytraData.isGliding()) {
            if (to.clone().subtract(0, 0.1, 0).getBlock().getType().isSolid() || to.clone().subtract(0, 0.2, 0).getBlock().getType().isSolid()) {
                elytraData.resetGlideState();
                return;
            }
        }

        // 首次进入滑翔状态时的状态同步
        if (player.isGliding() && !elytraData.isGliding()) {
            elytraData.setGliding(true);
            elytraData.setGlideStartTime(now);
            elytraData.setGlideTicks(0);
        }

        elytraData.incrementGlideTicks();

        // ==============================================================================
        // 子项五: ElytraGlideState (滑翔装备与耐久状态一致性检测)
        // 限频检测，避免每 tick 调用 getInventory()
        // ==============================================================================
        if (glideStateSubCheck.isEnabled()) {
            if (now - elytraData.getLastChestplateCheckTime() > 1000L || elytraData.getGlideTicks() == 1) {
                elytraData.setLastChestplateCheckTime(now);
                ItemStack chest = player.getInventory().getChestplate();
                if (chest == null || chest.getType() != Material.ELYTRA) {
                    flagSubCheck(player, data, glideStateSubCheck, 5,
                            "[ElytraGlideState] Spoofed glide: player is gliding without elytra chestplate", event);
                    player.setGliding(false);
                    elytraData.resetGlideState();
                    return;
                }
                if (glideStateCheckDurability && chest.getItemMeta() instanceof Damageable damageable) {
                    if (damageable.getDamage() >= Material.ELYTRA.getMaxDurability() - 1) {
                        flagSubCheck(player, data, glideStateSubCheck, 5,
                                "[ElytraGlideState] Spoofed glide: elytra durability is 0 (broken)", event);
                        player.setGliding(false);
                        elytraData.resetGlideState();
                        return;
                    }
                }
                elytraData.setElytraEquipped(true);
            }
        }

        // 计算当前 Tick 基础运动学参量
        double deltaX = to.getX() - from.getX();
        double deltaY = to.getY() - from.getY();
        double deltaZ = to.getZ() - from.getZ();
        double horizontalDistance = Math.hypot(deltaX, deltaZ);
        float pitch = to.getPitch();

        // 判断环境与推进状态
        boolean fireworkBoosted = (now - elytraData.getLastFireworkTime()) < fireworkBoostDurationMs;
        var adapter = com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter();
        boolean riptideActive = (now - elytraData.getLastRiptideTime()) < riptideDurationMs || adapter.isRiptiding(player);
        boolean externalVelocityActive = (now - elytraData.getLastExternalVelocityTime()) < externalVelocityDurationMs
                || data.hasAnyLegalVelocity() || data.hasRecentVelocity(externalVelocityDurationMs);
        boolean inBubbleColumn = isInBubbleColumn(from) || isInBubbleColumn(to);
        boolean hasLevitationOrSlowFall = adapter.hasPotionEffect(player, com.example.argusac.compat.CompatPotionType.SLOW_FALLING)
                || adapter.hasPotionEffect(player, com.example.argusac.compat.CompatPotionType.LEVITATION);

        // ==============================================================================
        // 子项六: ElytraTimer (滑翔 Tick 速率异常检测)
        // ==============================================================================
        if (timerSubCheck.isEnabled()) {
            long lastMove = elytraData.getLastMoveTimestamp();
            if (lastMove > 0) {
                long interval = now - lastMove;
                elytraData.recordMoveInterval(interval);
                if (elytraData.getMoveIntervalSampleSize() >= timerSampleWindowSize) {
                    double avgInterval = elytraData.getAverageMoveInterval();
                    double tps = getRecentTps();
                    if (tps >= minServerTps && avgInterval < timerMinAverageIntervalMs) {
                        String details = String.format("[ElytraTimer] avgInterval=%.1fms (< %.1fms), tps=%.1f, samples=%d",
                                avgInterval, timerMinAverageIntervalMs, tps, timerSampleWindowSize);
                        flagSubCheck(player, data, timerSubCheck, 2, details, event);
                        elytraData.clearMoveIntervals();
                    }
                }
            }
            elytraData.setLastMoveTimestamp(now);
        }

        // ==============================================================================
        // 子项一: ElytraSpeed (滑翔速度上限)
        // ==============================================================================
        if (speedSubCheck.isEnabled() && !riptideActive && !externalVelocityActive) {
            double allowedSpeed;
            if (fireworkBoosted) {
                allowedSpeed = speedMaxBoosted;
            } else {
                allowedSpeed = speedMaxUnboosted;
                // 考虑俯冲角度 (pitch > 0 为低头俯冲，重力势能转化为水平速度)
                if (pitch > 0.0f) {
                    double diveBonus = Math.sin(Math.toRadians(pitch)) * speedPitchDiveMultiplier;
                    allowedSpeed += diveBonus;
                }
            }

            // 物理动量惯性补偿：俯冲拉平时因空气阻力 (~0.99/tick)，水平动量在 1-2 tick 内自然衰减
            double maxMomentumSpeed = elytraData.getLastHorizontalSpeed() * 0.992 + 0.05;

            if (horizontalDistance > allowedSpeed && horizontalDistance > maxMomentumSpeed) {
                double baseline = Math.max(allowedSpeed, maxMomentumSpeed);
                double ratio = horizontalDistance / baseline;
                int vlToAdd = (ratio >= 1.5) ? 3 : (ratio >= 1.2 ? 2 : 1);
                String details = String.format("[ElytraSpeed] speed=(%.2f,%.2f,%.2f)[len=%.2f], limit=%.2f, pitch=%.1f, fwBoost=%b, ticks=%d",
                        deltaX, deltaY, deltaZ, horizontalDistance, allowedSpeed, pitch, fireworkBoosted, elytraData.getGlideTicks());
                flagSubCheck(player, data, speedSubCheck, vlToAdd, details, event);
                if (hasSetbackOccurred(to, event.getTo())) {
                    elytraData.resetGlideState();
                    return;
                }
            }
        }

        // ==============================================================================
        // 子项二: ElytraAscend (无推进上升)
        // ==============================================================================
        if (ascendSubCheck.isEnabled()) {
            if (deltaY > ascendMaxVertical && !fireworkBoosted && !riptideActive && !externalVelocityActive
                    && !inBubbleColumn && !adapter.hasPotionEffect(player, com.example.argusac.compat.CompatPotionType.LEVITATION)) {
                // 原版物理仰角拉升爬升判定：前一 tick 拥有充沛初速度且当前水平速度正急剧衰减失速 (能量守恒)
                boolean lawfulPullUpClimb = elytraData.getLastHorizontalSpeed() > 0.75
                        && (horizontalDistance < elytraData.getLastHorizontalSpeed() - 0.02)
                        && elytraData.getAscendTicks() < 3;

                if (!lawfulPullUpClimb) {
                    elytraData.incrementAscendTicks();
                    if (elytraData.getAscendTicks() >= ascendTicksThreshold) {
                        String details = String.format("[ElytraAscend] deltaY=%.3f (> %.2f), ascendTicks=%d, speed=%.2f, pitch=%.1f, no-boost",
                                deltaY, ascendMaxVertical, elytraData.getAscendTicks(), horizontalDistance, pitch);
                        flagSubCheck(player, data, ascendSubCheck, 2, details, event);
                        if (hasSetbackOccurred(to, event.getTo())) {
                            elytraData.resetGlideState();
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    elytraData.setAscendTicks(0);
                }
            } else {
                elytraData.setAscendTicks(0);
            }
        }

        // ==============================================================================
        // 子项三: ElytraHover (空中悬停)
        // ==============================================================================
        if (hoverSubCheck.isEnabled()) {
            long glideDuration = now - elytraData.getGlideStartTime();
            // 起跳瞬间豁免、缓降/悬浮药水豁免、贴墙/方块碰撞豁免
            if (glideDuration >= takeoffGraceDurationMs && !hasLevitationOrSlowFall
                    && !isInLiquidOrClimbableOrNearWall(player, to) && !inBubbleColumn) {
                if (horizontalDistance < hoverMaxHorizontalSpeed && Math.abs(deltaY) < hoverMaxVerticalSpeed) {
                    elytraData.incrementHoverTicks();
                    if (elytraData.getHoverTicks() >= hoverTicksThreshold) {
                        String details = String.format("[ElytraHover] speed=%.3f, deltaY=%.3f, hoverTicks=%d, duration=%dms",
                                horizontalDistance, deltaY, elytraData.getHoverTicks(), glideDuration);
                        flagSubCheck(player, data, hoverSubCheck, 2, details, event);
                        if (hasSetbackOccurred(to, event.getTo())) {
                            elytraData.resetGlideState();
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    elytraData.setHoverTicks(0);
                }
            } else {
                elytraData.setHoverTicks(0);
            }
        }

        // ==============================================================================
        // 子项四: ElytraFirework (烟花消耗与实际加速不匹配)
        // ==============================================================================
        if (fireworkSubCheck.isEnabled() && !riptideActive && !externalVelocityActive) {
            double prevSpeed = elytraData.getLastHorizontalSpeed();
            // 1. 在平飞或仰角状态下持续正向异常加速 (当前与前一 tick 均无俯冲势能且无烟花推进)
            if (pitch <= 5.0f && elytraData.getLastPitch() <= 10.0f && !fireworkBoosted) {
                double accel = horizontalDistance - prevSpeed;
                if (accel > fireworkMinUnpropelledAccel && horizontalDistance > 0.85) {
                    String details = String.format("[ElytraFirework] Unpropelled acceleration in flat flight: accel=+%.2f, speed=%.2f, pitch=%.1f",
                            accel, horizontalDistance, pitch);
                    flagSubCheck(player, data, fireworkSubCheck, 2, details, event);
                    if (hasSetbackOccurred(to, event.getTo())) {
                        elytraData.resetGlideState();
                        return;
                    }
                }
            }

            // 2. 反向检测：滑翔中长期从未使用烟花，但水平速度持续维持在上限附近
            if (!fireworkBoosted && horizontalDistance >= 1.30 && pitch <= 10.0f) {
                elytraData.incrementConsecutiveHighSpeedTicks();
                if (elytraData.getConsecutiveHighSpeedTicks() >= fireworkContinuousHighSpeedTicks) {
                    String details = String.format("[ElytraFirework] Sustained max speed without firework: ticks=%d, speed=%.2f, pitch=%.1f",
                            elytraData.getConsecutiveHighSpeedTicks(), horizontalDistance, pitch);
                    flagSubCheck(player, data, fireworkSubCheck, 2, details, event);
                    if (hasSetbackOccurred(to, event.getTo())) {
                        elytraData.resetGlideState();
                        return;
                    }
                }
            } else {
                elytraData.setConsecutiveHighSpeedTicks(0);
            }
        }

        // 更新上一 Tick 运动学数据
        elytraData.setLastHorizontalSpeed(horizontalDistance);
        elytraData.setLastDeltaY(deltaY);
        elytraData.setLastPitch(pitch);
        elytraData.setLastLocation(to.clone());
        elytraData.setLastVelocity(new Vector(deltaX, deltaY, deltaZ));
    }

    // ==============================================================================
    // 状态切换与辅助事件接入
    // ==============================================================================

    /**
     * 监听滑翔状态切换事件 (EntityToggleGlideEvent)
     */
    public void handleToggleGlide(Player player, PlayerData data, EntityToggleGlideEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null || data == null) {
            return;
        }

        // 创造/旁观、允许飞行或处于载具豁免
        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR
                || player.isFlying() || player.getAllowFlight() || player.isInsideVehicle()) {
            return;
        }

        ElytraData elytraData = data.getElytraData();
        boolean willGlide = event.isGliding();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        if (willGlide) {
            // 校验鞘翅装备
            ItemStack chest = player.getInventory().getChestplate();
            if (chest == null || chest.getType() != Material.ELYTRA) {
                flagSubCheck(player, data, glideStateSubCheck, 5,
                        "[ElytraGlideState] Toggle rejected: no elytra equipped", null);
                event.setCancelled(true);
                elytraData.resetGlideState();
                return;
            }
            if (glideStateCheckDurability && chest.getItemMeta() instanceof Damageable damageable) {
                if (damageable.getDamage() >= Material.ELYTRA.getMaxDurability() - 1) {
                    flagSubCheck(player, data, glideStateSubCheck, 5,
                            "[ElytraGlideState] Toggle rejected: elytra broken (0 durability)", null);
                    event.setCancelled(true);
                    elytraData.resetGlideState();
                    return;
                }
            }

            // 子项八: ElytraNoFall (触地瞬间频繁开关滑翔免摔滥用)
            if (noFallSubCheck.isEnabled()) {
                float fallDist = player.getFallDistance();
                if (fallDist >= noFallMinFallDistance) {
                    double distToGround = getDistanceToGround(player.getLocation());
                    if (distToGround <= noFallGroundProximityDistance) {
                        int attempts = elytraData.recordLowYToggle(8000L);
                        // 首次临地开鞘翅属于合法极限自救 (MLG Elytra)，仅对 8 秒内反复/连续开关刷免摔判定作弊
                        if (attempts >= 2) {
                            String details = String.format("[ElytraNoFall] Repeated glide toggles near ground: attempts=%d, dist=%.2fb, fallDist=%.1f",
                                    attempts, distToGround, fallDist);
                            flagSubCheck(player, data, noFallSubCheck, 2, details, null);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            elytraData.setGliding(true);
            elytraData.setGlideStartTime(now);
            elytraData.setGlideTicks(0);
            elytraData.setElytraEquipped(true);
            elytraData.setLastChestplateCheckTime(now);
        } else {
            elytraData.setGliding(false);
            elytraData.resetGlideState();
        }

        // 子项七: ElytraFlip (短时间内滑翔状态高频切换检测)
        if (flipSubCheck.isEnabled()) {
            elytraData.recordToggle(now, player.getLocation());
            int toggleCount = elytraData.getToggleCountInWindow(flipWindowMs);
            if (toggleCount > flipMaxToggles) {
                String details = String.format("[ElytraFlip] High frequency glide toggle: %d toggles in %dms (max=%d)",
                        toggleCount, flipWindowMs, flipMaxToggles);
                flagSubCheck(player, data, flipSubCheck, 3, details, null);
                elytraData.clearToggleTimestamps();
                elytraData.resetGlideState();
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 监听烟花使用 (PlayerInteractEvent - 支持主手与副手)
     */
    public void handleInteract(Player player, PlayerData data, PlayerInteractEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null || data == null) {
            return;
        }

        Action action = event.getAction();
        if (action != Action.RIGHT_CLICK_AIR && action != Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK) {
            return;
        }

        ItemStack item = event.getItem();
        ItemStack mainHand = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();
        ItemStack offHand = player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand();
        boolean isFirework = (item != null && item.getType() == Material.FIREWORK_ROCKET)
                || (mainHand != null && mainHand.getType() == Material.FIREWORK_ROCKET)
                || (offHand != null && offHand.getType() == Material.FIREWORK_ROCKET);

        if (isFirework) {
            if (player.isGliding() || data.isElytraGliding()) {
                long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
                data.getElytraData().setLastFireworkTime(now);
                data.getElytraData().setLastFireworkLocation(player.getLocation().clone());
                data.grantLegalVelocity(VelocitySourceType.FIREWORK, fireworkBoostDurationMs);
                data.getElytraData().setAscendTicks(0);
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 监听外部受力击退 (PlayerVelocityEvent)
     */
    public void handleVelocity(Player player, PlayerData data, PlayerVelocityEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null || data == null) {
            return;
        }
        Vector v = event.getVelocity();
        if (v.lengthSquared() > 0.04) {
            data.getElytraData().setLastExternalVelocityTime(System.currentTimeMillis());
            data.grantLegalVelocity(VelocitySourceType.PISTON, externalVelocityDurationMs);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 监听爆炸、风弹及外部伤害 (EntityDamageEvent)
     */
    public void handleDamage(Player player, PlayerData data, EntityDamageEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null || data == null) {
            return;
        }
        EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause cause = event.getCause();
        String causeName = cause.name();
        // 兼容 1.20.4 与 1.21+ 的爆炸、风弹 (WIND_CHARGE / BREEZE) 及雷击等外部推力源
        if (causeName.contains("EXPLOSION") || causeName.contains("WIND") || causeName.contains("CHARGE") || causeName.contains("LIGHTNING")) {
            data.getElytraData().setLastExternalVelocityTime(System.currentTimeMillis());
            data.grantLegalVelocity(VelocitySourceType.EXPLOSION, externalVelocityDurationMs);
        }
    }

    // ==============================================================================
    // 违规裁决与回退接入
    // ==============================================================================

    /**
     * 统一调用 ViolationManager 处理各子项判定与回退
     */
    private void flagSubCheck(Player player, PlayerData data, ElytraSubCheck subCheck, int vl, String details, PlayerMoveEvent moveEvent) {
        if (player == null || !player.isOnline()) {
            return;
        }

        // 规范日志字段：拼接穿戴状态、滑翔 tick 及最近烟花时间差
        String fullDetails = details;
        if (data != null) {
            ElytraData ed = data.getElytraData();
            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
            long fwAgo = ed.getLastFireworkTime() > 0 ? (now - ed.getLastFireworkTime()) : -1L;
            String fwStr = fwAgo >= 0 ? (fwAgo + "ms ago") : "none";
            fullDetails = String.format("%s, elytra=%b, fw=%s", details, ed.isElytraEquipped(), fwStr);
        }

        // 调度至 ViolationManager 进行全局裁决
        plugin.getViolationManager().handleViolation(player, subCheck, vl, fullDetails, moveEvent);

        // 回退成功后重置滑翔状态数据，防止二次误报
        if (data != null && data.isSetbackImmune()) {
            data.getElytraData().resetGlideState();
        }
    }

    private boolean hasSetbackOccurred(Location origTo, Location currentTo) {
        if (currentTo == null) {
            return true;
        }
        return origTo.getX() != currentTo.getX()
                || origTo.getY() != currentTo.getY()
                || origTo.getZ() != currentTo.getZ();
    }

    // ==============================================================================
    // 环境物理采样辅助方法
    // ==============================================================================

    private boolean isInBubbleColumn(Location loc) {
        if (loc == null || loc.getWorld() == null) {
            return false;
        }
        for (int y = 0; y <= 3; y++) {
            Block b = loc.clone().subtract(0, y, 0).getBlock();
            if (b.getType() == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN || b.getType() == Material.SOUL_SAND) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private boolean isInLiquidOrClimbableOrNearWall(Player player, Location loc) {
        if (loc == null || loc.getWorld() == null) {
            return false;
        }
        Block b = loc.getBlock();
        if (b.isLiquid()) {
            return true;
        }
        Material m = b.getType();
        if (m == Material.LADDER || m == Material.VINE || m == Material.SCAFFOLDING
                || m == Material.COBWEB || m == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN || m == Material.POWDER_SNOW
                || m.name().contains("VINE") || m.name().contains("BERRY")) {
            return true;
        }
        // 检查脚下方块是否为实心支撑 (避免落脚在栅栏、台阶、地毯上误判悬停)
        Block feet = loc.clone().subtract(0, 0.5, 0).getBlock();
        if (feet.getType().isSolid()) {
            return true;
        }
        for (double dx = -0.4; dx <= 0.4; dx += 0.8) {
            for (double dz = -0.4; dz <= 0.4; dz += 0.8) {
                Block side = loc.clone().add(dx, 0, dz).getBlock();
                if (side.getType().isSolid()) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private double getDistanceToGround(Location loc) {
        if (loc == null || loc.getWorld() == null) {
            return 10.0;
        }
        Location check = loc.clone();
        for (double y = 0.0; y <= 3.0; y += 0.25) {
            Block b = check.clone().subtract(0, y, 0).getBlock();
            if (b.getType().isSolid()) {
                return y;
            }
        }
        return 10.0;
    }

    private double getRecentTps() {
        try {
            double[] tps = Bukkit.getTPS();
            if (tps != null && tps.length > 0) {
                return Math.min(20.0, Math.max(0.0, tps[0]));
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
        return 20.0;
    }

    // ==============================================================================
    // 子检测项 Check 实现实体 (ElytraSubCheck)
    // 承载每个子项独立的回退模式与 min-vl 门槛
    // ==============================================================================

    public static class ElytraSubCheck implements Check {
        private final ElytraFlyCheck parent;
        private final String subName;
        private boolean enabled = true;
        private int minVl = 3;
        private String setbackMode = "HARD";

        public ElytraSubCheck(ElytraFlyCheck parent, String subName) {
            this.parent = parent;
            this.subName = subName;
        }

        public void reload(boolean enabled, int minVl, String setbackMode) {
            this.enabled = enabled;
            this.minVl = minVl;
            this.setbackMode = setbackMode != null ? setbackMode.toUpperCase() : "HARD";
        }

        public String getSubName() {
            return subName;
        }

        @Override
        public String getName() {
            // 保持主检测项唯一名 ElytraFly，使 VL、告警、日志归并集中管控
            return parent.getName();
        }

        @Override
        public CheckType getType() {
            return parent.getType();
        }

        @Override
        public boolean isEnabled() {
            return parent.isEnabled() && this.enabled;
        }

        @Override
        public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) {
            this.enabled = enabled;
        }

        @Override
        public int getMaxVl() {
            return parent.getMaxVl();
        }

        @Override
        public boolean isSetbackEnabled() {
            return parent.isSetbackEnabled() && this.enabled;
        }

        @Override
        public int getSetbackMinVl() {
            return minVl;
        }

        @Override
        public String getSetbackMode() {
            return setbackMode;
        }

        @Override
        public void reloadConfig() {
            parent.reloadConfig();
        }
    }

    // ==================== Typed Getters ====================

    public ElytraSubCheck getSpeedSubCheck() { return speedSubCheck; }
    public ElytraSubCheck getAscendSubCheck() { return ascendSubCheck; }
    public ElytraSubCheck getHoverSubCheck() { return hoverSubCheck; }
    public ElytraSubCheck getFireworkSubCheck() { return fireworkSubCheck; }
    public ElytraSubCheck getGlideStateSubCheck() { return glideStateSubCheck; }
    public ElytraSubCheck getTimerSubCheck() { return timerSubCheck; }
    public ElytraSubCheck getFlipSubCheck() { return flipSubCheck; }
    public ElytraSubCheck getNoFallSubCheck() { return noFallSubCheck; }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/movement/FlyCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check.movement;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.check.AbstractCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import com.example.argusac.compat.CompatPotionType;
import com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapter;
import com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;

/**
 * 飞行与非法滞空检测 (FlyCheck)
 * 检测长时间悬空不掉落 (Hover) 及在空中无跳跃效果持续上升 (Illegal Ascent)
 */
public class FlyCheck extends AbstractCheck {

    private int maxAirTicks;
    private double verticalAscentThreshold;

    public FlyCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Fly", CheckType.MOVEMENT);
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        super.reloadConfig();
        String path = "checks." + name + ".";
        this.maxAirTicks = plugin.getConfig().getInt(path + "max-air-ticks", 25);
        this.verticalAscentThreshold = plugin.getConfig().getDouble(path + "vertical-ascent-threshold", 0.42);
    }

    public void handleMove(Player player, PlayerData data, PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null || data == null) {
            return;
        }

        // 创造/旁观、允许飞行、载具豁免
        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR
                || player.isFlying() || player.getAllowFlight() || player.isInsideVehicle()) {
            data.setAirTicks(0);
            return;
        }

        // 鞘翅滑翔显式互斥：滑翔状态完全移交 ElytraFly 模块集中判定，Fly 模块直接跳过
        if (plugin.getCheckManager().isGlidingExempt(player, data)) {
            data.setAirTicks(0);
            return;
        }

        if (data.isSetbackImmune() || data.hasAnyLegalVelocity() || data.isInGracePeriod() || data.hasRecentVelocity(1000L)) {
            data.setAirTicks(0);
            return;
        }

        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to = event.getTo();
        if (to == null || from.getWorld() != to.getWorld()) {
            return;
        }

        VersionAdapter adapter = VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter();
        double deltaY = to.getY() - from.getY();

        // 检查是否接触爬梯、藤蔓、脚手架、蜘蛛网、水等缓冲方块
        if (isInClimbableOrLiquid(player, adapter)) {
            data.setAirTicks(0);
            return;
        }

        // 检查脚下及周围是否有可支撑方块
        boolean hasGroundNear = hasBlockBelow(from, adapter);

        if (!hasGroundNear) {
            data.incrementAirTicks();
            int airTicks = data.getAirTicks();

            // 1. 非法垂直向上飞升检测 (在空中持续攀升且无飘浮/跳跃提升效果)
            if (deltaY > verticalAscentThreshold
                    && !adapter.hasPotionEffect(player, CompatPotionType.JUMP_BOOST)
                    && !adapter.hasPotionEffect(player, CompatPotionType.LEVITATION)
                    && !adapter.isRiptiding(player)) {
                flag(player, 2, String.format("IllegalAscent deltaY=%.3f", deltaY), event);
                return;
            }

            // 2. 长时间悬空滞空检测 (Hover)
            if (airTicks > maxAirTicks && Math.abs(deltaY) < 0.08) {
                flag(player, 1, String.format("Hover airTicks=%d, deltaY=%.3f", airTicks, deltaY), event);
            }

            // 3. 凌空踩空气行走 (AirWalk / 平飞外挂)：滞空且无缓降药水下，垂直位移完全为 0
            if (airTicks > 8 && Math.abs(deltaY) < 0.005 && !adapter.hasPotionEffect(player, CompatPotionType.SLOW_FALLING)) {
                flag(player, 2, String.format("AirWalk airTicks=%d, deltaY=%.4f", airTicks, deltaY), event);
            }
        } else {
            data.setAirTicks(0);
        }
    }

    private boolean isInClimbableOrLiquid(Player player, VersionAdapter adapter) {
        Location loc = player.getLocation();
        Block b = loc.getBlock();
        return adapter.isClimbable(b) || adapter.isLiquid(b);
    }

    private boolean hasBlockBelow(Location loc, VersionAdapter adapter) {
        Location checkLoc = loc.clone();
        for (double yOffset = -0.1; yOffset >= -1.0; yOffset -= 0.3) {
            Block b = checkLoc.clone().add(0, yOffset, 0).getBlock();
            if (adapter.isSolidBlock(b)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/movement/InventoryMoveCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check.movement;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.check.AbstractCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import com.example.argusac.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;

/**
 * 背包移动检测 (InventoryMoveCheck)
 * 检查玩家在打开背包或容器状态下异常疾跑、跳跃或长距离移动作弊行为
 */
public class InventoryMoveCheck extends AbstractCheck {

    private double maxDistanceWhileOpen;

    public InventoryMoveCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "InventoryMove", CheckType.MOVEMENT);
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        super.reloadConfig();
        this.maxDistanceWhileOpen = plugin.getConfig().getDouble("checks." + name + ".max-distance-while-open", 0.15);
    }

    public void handleMove(Player player, PlayerData data, PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null || data == null) {
            return;
        }

        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR
                || player.isInsideVehicle() || com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().isGliding(player)) {
            return;
        }

        if (data.isSetbackImmune() || data.hasAnyLegalVelocity() || data.isInGracePeriod()) {
            return;
        }

        // 仅在玩家处于打开容器/背包状态下进行检测
        if (!data.isInventoryOpen()) {
            return;
        }

        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        // 打开容器前 350ms 属于奔跑逼近容器的残余惯性缓冲，受流水冲刷或受到伤害击退亦豁免
        if (now - data.getInventoryOpenTime() < 350L || com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().isInWater(player) || data.hasRecentVelocity(800L)) {
            return;
        }

        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to = event.getTo();
        if (to == null) {
            return;
        }

        double distance = MathUtil.getHorizontalDistance(from, to);

        // 如果在开包状态下出现疾跑或水平移动距离明显大于滑行惯性
        if (distance > maxDistanceWhileOpen && (player.isSprinting() || distance > 0.28)) {
            flag(player, 1, String.format("Moving while inv open dist=%.3f", distance), event);
        }
    }
}
```
