`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/movement/NoFallCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check.movement;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.check.AbstractCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;

/**
 * 虚假接地与免摔检测 (NoFallCheck)
 * 检查客户端伪造 onGround 状态以避免摔落伤害作弊行为
 */
public class NoFallCheck extends AbstractCheck {

    public NoFallCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "NoFall", CheckType.MOVEMENT);
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        super.reloadConfig();
    }

    public void handleMove(Player player, PlayerData data, PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null || data == null) {
            return;
        }

        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR
                || player.isFlying() || player.isInsideVehicle()) {
            return;
        }

        // 鞘翅滑翔显式互斥：滑翔状态完全移交 ElytraFly 模块集中判定，NoFall 模块不判定
        if (plugin.getCheckManager().isGlidingExempt(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        if (data.isSetbackImmune() || data.hasAnyLegalVelocity() || data.isInGracePeriod()) {
            return;
        }

        Location to = event.getTo();
        if (to == null) {
            return;
        }

        // 客户端声明处于地面（读取 PlayerData 中由物理检测同步的接地状态）
        boolean clientGround = data.isOnGround();

        if (clientGround) {
            // 服务端真实碰撞体与脚下方块物理检查
            boolean serverGround = checkPhysicalGround(to);

            // 客户端声称接地，但实际脚下及周围悬空且不在水中、蜘蛛网、梯子中
            if (!serverGround && !isInLiquidOrClimbable(to)) {
                // 如果还处于高坠落中，极高置信度 NoFall
                if (player.getFallDistance() > 1.5f || event.getFrom().getY() - to.getY() > 0.4) {
                    flag(player, 1, String.format("Spoofed Ground fallDist=%.1f", player.getFallDistance()), event);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private boolean checkPhysicalGround(Location loc) {
        Location check = loc.clone().subtract(0, 0.15, 0);
        // 采样脚下 3x3 范围
        for (double dx = -0.3; dx <= 0.3; dx += 0.3) {
            for (double dz = -0.3; dz <= 0.3; dz += 0.3) {
                Block b = check.clone().add(dx, 0, dz).getBlock();
                Material mat = b.getType();
                String name = mat.name();
                if (mat.isSolid() || name.contains("SLAB") || name.contains("STAIR")
                        || name.contains("CARPET") || name.contains("SNOW") || name.contains("LILY")) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private boolean isInLiquidOrClimbable(Location loc) {
        Block b = loc.getBlock();
        Material mat = b.getType();
        return b.isLiquid() || mat == Material.LADDER || mat == Material.VINE
                || mat == Material.SCAFFOLDING || mat == Material.COBWEB || mat.name().contains("VINE");
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/movement/SpeedCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check.movement;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.check.AbstractCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import com.example.argusac.compat.CompatPotionType;
import com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapter;
import com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory;
import com.example.argusac.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;

/**
 * 移动速度检测 (SpeedCheck)
 * 检测水平移动距离 (BPS / per-tick offset)，综合考虑药水加速、冰面滑行、疾跑跳跃容差
 */
public class SpeedCheck extends AbstractCheck {

    private double maxHorizontalSpeed;
    private double iceModifier;
    private double speedPotionMultiplier;

    public SpeedCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Speed", CheckType.MOVEMENT);
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        super.reloadConfig();
        String path = "checks." + name + ".";
        this.maxHorizontalSpeed = plugin.getConfig().getDouble(path + "max-horizontal-speed", 0.65);
        this.iceModifier = plugin.getConfig().getDouble(path + "ice-modifier", 1.45);
        this.speedPotionMultiplier = plugin.getConfig().getDouble(path + "speed-potion-multiplier", 0.20);
    }

    /**
     * 处理玩家移动事件
     */
    public void handleMove(Player player, PlayerData data, PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null || data == null) {
            return;
        }

        // 创造/旁观模式、正在飞行、坐在载具中豁免
        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR
                || player.isFlying() || player.isInsideVehicle()) {
            return;
        }

        // 鞘翅滑翔显式互斥：滑翔状态完全移交 ElytraFly 模块集中判定，Speed 模块不进行水平速度检测
        if (plugin.getCheckManager().isGlidingExempt(player, data)) {
            return;
        }

        // 处于传送或登录豁免缓冲期，或处于回退自免疫期、合法高机动期、近期 800ms 内受到击退/伤害
        if (data.isSetbackImmune() || data.hasAnyLegalVelocity() || data.isInGracePeriod() || data.hasRecentVelocity(800L)) {
            return;
        }

        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to = event.getTo();
        if (to == null || from.getWorld() != to.getWorld()) {
            return;
        }

        VersionAdapter adapter = VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter();
        double distance = MathUtil.getHorizontalDistance(from, to);

        // 计算当前环境的最大合法阈值
        double allowedLimit = maxHorizontalSpeed;

        // 速度药水加成
        int speedAmp = adapter.getPotionAmplifier(player, CompatPotionType.SPEED);
        if (speedAmp >= 0) {
            allowedLimit += (speedAmp + 1) * speedPotionMultiplier;
        }

        // 缓慢药水减速约束 (防无视缓慢效果 Anti-Slowness)
        int slownessAmp = adapter.getPotionAmplifier(player, CompatPotionType.SLOWNESS);
        if (slownessAmp >= 0) {
            allowedLimit *= Math.max(0.40, 1.0 - ((slownessAmp + 1) * 0.15));
        }

        // 冰面与粘液块加速修正 (检测脚下方块及再下一格方块)
        Block under = from.clone().subtract(0, 0.5, 0).getBlock();
        if (adapter.isIce(under) || adapter.isSlime(under)) {
            allowedLimit *= iceModifier;
        } else if (adapter.isSoulSand(under) || adapter.isHoney(under)) {
            // 灵魂沙与蜂蜜块减速
            allowedLimit *= 0.65;
        }

        // 水中海豚的恩惠或三叉戟加速保守容差
        if (adapter.hasPotionEffect(player, CompatPotionType.DOLPHINS_GRACE) || adapter.isRiptiding(player)) {
            allowedLimit *= 2.0;
        }

        // 空中加速度反常检测 (Mid-Air Acceleration / BHop)：在空中无地面摩擦力时速度非法激增
        double lastDistance = data.getLastHorizontalDistance();
        if (data.getAirTicks() > 2 && lastDistance > 0.15
                && !adapter.isRiptiding(player)
                && !adapter.isInWater(player)
                && !adapter.hasPotionEffect(player, CompatPotionType.DOLPHINS_GRACE)) {
            double maxAllowedAirSpeed = lastDistance * 0.98 + 0.05;
            if (distance > maxAllowedAirSpeed && distance > 0.40) {
                flag(player, 1, String.format("AirAccelerate dist=%.3f, prev=%.3f, limit=%.3f", distance, lastDistance, maxAllowedAirSpeed), event);
            }
        }
        data.setLastHorizontalDistance(distance);

        if (distance > allowedLimit) {
            double diff = distance - allowedLimit;
            // 阶梯式递增 VL，防止轻微丢包瞬间误封
            int vlToAdd = diff > 0.3 ? 2 : 1;
            String details = String.format("dist=%.3f, max=%.3f", distance, allowedLimit);
            flag(player, vlToAdd, details, event);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/packet/BadPacketsCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check.packet;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.check.AbstractCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;

/**
 * 畸形与异常数据包检测 (BadPacketsCheck)
 * 检测非法俯仰角越界 (Derp / Head Roll)、NaN/无穷大坐标以及异常冗余包
 */
public class BadPacketsCheck extends AbstractCheck {

    private boolean strictPitch;
    private boolean checkDuplicateMove;

    public BadPacketsCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "BadPackets", CheckType.PACKET);
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        super.reloadConfig();
        String path = "checks." + name + ".";
        this.strictPitch = plugin.getConfig().getBoolean(path + "strict-pitch", true);
        this.checkDuplicateMove = plugin.getConfig().getBoolean(path + "check-duplicate-move", true);
    }

    public void handleMove(Player player, PlayerData data, PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null || data == null || data.isSetbackImmune()) {
            return;
        }

        Location to = event.getTo();
        if (to == null) {
            return;
        }

        // 1. 浮点数异常检测 (NaN / Infinite)
        if (Double.isNaN(to.getX()) || Double.isNaN(to.getY()) || Double.isNaN(to.getZ())
                || Double.isNaN(to.getYaw()) || Double.isNaN(to.getPitch())
                || Double.isInfinite(to.getX()) || Double.isInfinite(to.getY()) || Double.isInfinite(to.getZ())
                || Double.isInfinite(to.getYaw()) || Double.isInfinite(to.getPitch())) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(player, 5, "NaN or Infinite coordinates/rotation", event);
            return;
        }

        // 2. 视角俯仰角越界检测 (Pitch [-90.0, 90.0])
        // 原生客户端无法发送超过正负 90 度的俯仰角，大于90度属于 Derp / 旋转外挂
        if (strictPitch) {
            float pitch = to.getPitch();
            if (pitch > 90.001f || pitch < -90.001f) {
                flag(player, 2, String.format("Illegal Pitch pitch=%.2f", pitch), event);
            }
        }

        // 3. 冲突状态检测：常规站姿潜行下强开疾跑 (Sneak-Sprint Exploit，排除 1 格高爬行模式)
        boolean gliding = com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().isGliding(player);
        boolean swimming = com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().isSwimming(player);
        if (player.isSneaking() && player.isSprinting() && !gliding && !swimming && player.getEyeHeight() >= 1.4) {
            flag(player, 1, "Sneak-Sprint Conflicting State");
            player.setSprinting(false);
        }

        // 4. 睡眠状态异常移动检测 (Sleep-Move)
        if (player.isSleeping() && (event.getFrom().distanceSquared(to) > 0.05)) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(player, 2, "Moving while sleeping", event);
        }

        // 5. 重复移动包检测：客户端在同 tick 内发送两个位置完全相同的包
        if (checkDuplicateMove) {
            Location from = event.getFrom();
            if (from.getX() == to.getX() && from.getY() == to.getY() && from.getZ() == to.getZ()
                    && from.getYaw() == to.getYaw() && from.getPitch() == to.getPitch()) {
                flag(player, 1, "Duplicate Move Packet");
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/world/FastBreakCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check.world;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.check.AbstractCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import com.example.argusac.compat.CompatPotionType;
import com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapter;
import com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;

/**
 * 快速破坏检测 (FastBreakCheck)
 * 检测玩家非法缩短挖掘高硬度方块所需理论耗时的作弊行为
 */
public class FastBreakCheck extends AbstractCheck {

    private double timeLeniency;

    public FastBreakCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "FastBreak", CheckType.WORLD);
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        super.reloadConfig();
        this.timeLeniency = plugin.getConfig().getDouble("checks." + name + ".time-leniency", 0.20);
    }

    public void handleBreak(Player player, BlockBreakEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null) {
            return;
        }

        // 创造模式瞬破方块，豁免
        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (data == null || data.isInGracePeriod()) {
            return;
        }

        Block block = event.getBlock();
        Material mat = block.getType();

        // 1. 挖掘超距检测 (Block Break Reach)：生存基准 4.5 格，含对角线半径(0.86)与后退网络延迟，阈值设为 6.20
        Location eye = player.getEyeLocation();
        Location blockCenter = block.getLocation().add(0.5, 0.5, 0.5);
        if (eye.getWorld() == blockCenter.getWorld()) {
            double breakDist = eye.distance(blockCenter);
            if (breakDist > 6.20) {
                flag(player, 2, String.format("FarBreak dist=%.2f, max=6.20", breakDist));
                event.setCancelled(true);
                return;
            }
        }

        // 极高硬度方块 (如黑曜石、哭泣黑曜石、远古残骸、深板岩等)
        float hardness = block.getType().getHardness();
        if (hardness < 1.5f) {
            // 极低硬度方块 (花草、火把、泥土等) 允许秒破，不作过严苛检测
            return;
        }

        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long lastBreakTime = data.getLastBreakTimestamp();
        long elapsed = (lastBreakTime > 0) ? (now - lastBreakTime) : 5000L;
        data.setLastBreakTimestamp(now);

        VersionAdapter adapter = VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter();

        // 挖掘疲劳检测：如果带有挖掘疲劳效果，几乎不可能以正常速度破坏方块
        boolean hasFatigue = adapter.hasPotionEffect(player, CompatPotionType.MINING_FATIGUE);
        // 应用宽容系数：阈值 * (1 + timeLeniency)
        long fatigueThreshold = (long) (1000L * (1.0 + timeLeniency));
        if (hasFatigue && hardness > 2.0f && elapsed < fatigueThreshold) {
            flag(player, 2, String.format("FastBreak under Mining Fatigue block=%s, elapsed=%dms", mat, elapsed));
            event.setCancelled(true);
            return;
        }

        // 黑曜石 (硬度 50.0)，即便是最佳工具+效率V也需要约 1.5 秒以上
        String matName = mat.name();
        if (mat == Material.OBSIDIAN || "CRYING_OBSIDIAN".equals(matName) || "ANCIENT_DEBRIS".equals(matName)) {
            long obsidianThreshold = (long) (800L * (1.0 - timeLeniency));
            if (elapsed < obsidianThreshold && !adapter.hasPotionEffect(player, CompatPotionType.HASTE)) {
                flag(player, 3, String.format("Instant obsidian break block=%s, elapsed=%dms", mat, elapsed));
                event.setCancelled(true);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/world/NukerCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check.world;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.check.AbstractCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;

import java.util.Deque;

/**
 * 范围/群破坏检测 (NukerCheck)
 * 检测单 tick 内破坏多个方块，或 1 秒内异常高频连破方块的炸矿/全域清理作弊
 */
public class NukerCheck extends AbstractCheck {

    private int maxBreaksPerTick;
    private int maxBreaksPerSecond;

    public NukerCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Nuker", CheckType.WORLD);
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        super.reloadConfig();
        String path = "checks." + name + ".";
        this.maxBreaksPerTick = plugin.getConfig().getInt(path + "max-breaks-per-tick", 2);
        this.maxBreaksPerSecond = plugin.getConfig().getInt(path + "max-breaks-per-second", 15);
    }

    public void handleBreak(Player player, BlockBreakEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null) {
            return;
        }

        // 创造模式挖掘豁免
        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (data == null || data.isInGracePeriod()) {
            return;
        }

        long currentTick = Bukkit.getCurrentTick();
        long lastTick = data.getLastBreakTick();

        if (currentTick == lastTick) {
            data.setBreaksThisTick(data.getBreaksThisTick() + 1);
        } else {
            data.setLastBreakTick(currentTick);
            data.setBreaksThisTick(1);
        }

        // 1. 同一游戏刻 (Tick) 内并发破坏多个非零硬度实体方块 (排除双层花草门床自然联动)
        if (event.getBlock().getType().getHardness() > 0.0f && data.getBreaksThisTick() > maxBreaksPerTick) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(player, 2, String.format("Multi-break in single tick count=%d", data.getBreaksThisTick()));
            return;
        }

        // 2. 1秒内累计破坏方块数
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        Deque<Long> breakQueue = data.getBreakTimestamps();
        breakQueue.addLast(now);
        while (!breakQueue.isEmpty() && now - breakQueue.peekFirst() > 1000L) {
            breakQueue.pollFirst();
        }

        // 急迫效果加成 (Haste I/II 信标下允许合法快速连破)
        int effectiveLimit = maxBreaksPerSecond;
        if (com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().hasPotionEffect(player, com.example.argusac.compat.CompatPotionType.HASTE)) {
            effectiveLimit += 10;
        }

        if (breakQueue.size() > effectiveLimit) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(player, 1, String.format("High Break CPS count=%d/s, limit=%d", breakQueue.size(), effectiveLimit));
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/check/world/ScaffoldCheck.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.check.world;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.check.AbstractCheck;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import com.example.argusac.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;

/**
 * 自动搭桥检测 (ScaffoldCheck)
 * 检测玩家非法向脚下空中倒退自动铺路、无潜行疾跑搭桥与异常放置角度
 */
public class ScaffoldCheck extends AbstractCheck {

    private double minPitch;
    private long fastPlacementDelayMs;

    public ScaffoldCheck(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        super(plugin, "Scaffold", CheckType.WORLD);
    }

    @Override
    public void reloadConfig() {
        super.reloadConfig();
        String path = "checks." + name + ".";
        this.minPitch = plugin.getConfig().getDouble(path + "min-pitch", 70.0);
        this.fastPlacementDelayMs = plugin.getConfig().getLong(path + "fast-placement-delay-ms", 120L);
    }

    public void handlePlace(Player player, BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        if (!enabled || player == null) {
            return;
        }

        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (data == null || data.isInGracePeriod()) {
            return;
        }

        Block placed = event.getBlockPlaced();
        Location playerLoc = player.getLocation();
        float pitch = playerLoc.getPitch();

        // 1. 放置超距检测 (FarPlace)：生存模式倒退铺路放方块最大中心距离放宽至 6.20 格
        Location eye = player.getEyeLocation();
        Location blockCenter = placed.getLocation().add(0.5, 0.5, 0.5);
        if (eye.getWorld() == blockCenter.getWorld() && eye.distance(blockCenter) > 6.20) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(player, 2, String.format("FarPlace dist=%.2f, max=6.20", eye.distance(blockCenter)));
            return;
        }

        // 检查放置的方块是否位于玩家正脚下下方
        boolean placedBelowFeet = (placed.getY() == playerLoc.getBlockY() - 1);

        // 2. 异常放置角度 (平视甚至仰头但能放置正脚下方块)
        if (placedBelowFeet && pitch < (float) minPitch) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            flag(player, 2, String.format("Impossible pitch scaffold pitch=%.1f, min=%.1f", pitch, minPitch));
            return;
        }

        // 3. 疾跑状态下连续凌空向脚下铺路 (正常情况下疾跑向后搭桥极难不潜行)
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long lastPlace = data.getLastPlaceTime();
        long interval = now - lastPlace;
        data.setLastPlaceTime(now);

        double horizontalSpeed = MathUtil.getHorizontalDistance(data.getFromLocation(), data.getToLocation());
        if (placedBelowFeet && player.isSprinting() && !player.isSneaking() && horizontalSpeed > 0.15) {
            if (interval < fastPlacementDelayMs) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
                flag(player, 1, String.format("Sprinting Scaffold interval=%dms, pitch=%.1f", interval, pitch));
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/command/ArgusCommand.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.command;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.Check;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import com.example.argusac.i18n.I18n;
import com.example.argusac.log.core.AuditLogManager;
import com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry;
import com.example.argusac.log.model.LogSeverity;
import com.example.argusac.log.model.LogType;
import com.example.argusac.log.query.LogQueryService;
import com.example.argusac.log.replay.AsciiReplayRenderer;
import com.example.argusac.util.ColorUtil;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.command.Command;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandExecutor;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * ArgusAC 主命令处理器 (/argus, /ac, /argusac)
 * 全面接入 i18n 国际化多语言系统，支持根据操作者客户端语言自适应呈现
 */
public class ArgusCommand implements CommandExecutor {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;
    private final AsciiReplayRenderer replayRenderer;

    public ArgusCommand(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.replayRenderer = new AsciiReplayRenderer();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean onCommand(CommandSender sender, Command command, String label, String[] args) {
        if (args.length == 0 || "help".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0])) {
            sendHelp(sender, label);
            return true;
        }

        String sub = args[0].toLowerCase();

        // 1. 玩家自身隐私与数据查询 (GDPR 个人透明度支持，无需 admin 权限)
        if ("my-data".equals(sub)) {
            if (!(sender instanceof Player p)) {
                I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "system.only-player");
                return true;
            }
            int retention = plugin.getConfig().getInt("audit.archive.retention-days", 30);
            boolean hashed = plugin.getConfig().getBoolean("audit.privacy.hash-ip", false);
            I18n.send(p, "command.my-data.header");
            I18n.send(p, "command.my-data.retention", "days", retention);
            I18n.send(p, "command.my-data.anonymization", "status", hashed ? "SHA-256 Hashed" : "Disabled");
            I18n.send(p, "command.my-data.purge-hint", "player", p.getName());
            return true;
        }

        if ("alerts".equals(sub)) {
            handleAlerts(sender, args);
            return true;
        }

        // 以下管理命令必须持有 argusac.admin (兼顾大小写兼容)
        if (!sender.hasPermission("argusac.admin") && !sender.hasPermission("ArgusAC.admin")) {
            I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "system.no-permission", "permission", "argusac.admin");
            return true;
        }

        switch (sub) {
            case "reload" -> {
                long start = System.currentTimeMillis();
                plugin.reloadAll();
                long elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - start;
                I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "system.reload-success", "time", elapsed);
                recordAdminAction(sender, "RELOAD", "all", "SUCCESS");
            }
            case "vl" -> handleVl(sender, label, args);
            case "logs" -> handleLogs(sender, label, args);
            case "history" -> handleHistory(sender, label, args);
            case "export" -> handleExport(sender, label, args);
            case "top" -> handleTop(sender, label, args);
            case "status" -> handleStatus(sender);
            case "verify" -> handleVerify(sender);
            case "debug" -> handleDebug(sender, label, args);
            case "replay" -> handleReplay(sender, label, args);
            case "purge" -> handlePurge(sender, label, args);
            case "compat" -> handleCompat(sender);
            case "toggle" -> handleToggle(sender, label, args);
            case "setback" -> handleSetbackCommand(sender, label, args);
            default -> I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "system.unknown-command", "subcommand", sub);
        }

        return true;
    }

    private void handleAlerts(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {
            I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "system.only-player");
            return;
        }
        if (!player.hasPermission("argusac.alert") && !player.hasPermission("ArgusAC.alert")) {
            I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "system.no-permission", "permission", "argusac.alert");
            return;
        }
        if (args.length > 2) {
            I18n.send(player, "command.alerts.usage");
            return;
        }

        boolean muted = plugin.getAlertManager().isMuted(player);
        String action = args.length == 1 ? (muted ? "on" : "off") : args[1].toLowerCase();
        switch (action) {
            case "on" -> {
                plugin.getAlertManager().setMuted(player, false);
                I18n.send(player, "command.alerts.enabled");
            }
            case "off" -> {
                plugin.getAlertManager().setMuted(player, true);
                I18n.send(player, "command.alerts.muted");
            }
            case "status" -> I18n.send(player, muted
                    ? "command.alerts.status-muted"
                    : "command.alerts.status-enabled");
            default -> I18n.send(player, "command.alerts.usage");
        }
    }

    private void handleCompat(CommandSender sender) {
        var adapter = com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter();
        I18n.send(sender, "command.compat.header");
        I18n.send(sender, "command.compat.env",
                "env", com.example.argusac.compat.ServerVersion.get().getFriendlyName(),
                "code", com.example.argusac.compat.ServerVersion.getCode(),
                "core", com.example.argusac.compat.ServerVersion.isPaper() ? "Paper" : "Spigot");
        I18n.send(sender, "command.compat.adapter", "adapter", adapter.getClass().getSimpleName());
        I18n.send(sender, "command.compat.divider");

        com.example.argusac.compat.CompatibilityManager compatMgr = plugin.getCompatibilityManager();
        compatMgr.getAllStatus().forEach((checkName, status) -> {
            String reason = compatMgr.getReason(checkName);
            adapter.sendMessage(sender, String.format(" &8- &b%-14s %s &8| &7%s",
                    checkName, status.getDisplay(), reason));
        });

        I18n.send(sender, "command.compat.divider");
        I18n.send(sender, "command.compat.legend");
    }

    private void handleVl(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 2) {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.vl.usage", "label", label);
            return;
        }
        Player target = Bukkit.getPlayer(args[1]);
        if (target == null) {
            I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "system.target-not-found", "target", args[1]);
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(target);
        int ping = com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().getPing(target);
        I18n.send(sender, "command.vl.header", "player", target.getName());
        I18n.send(sender, "command.vl.summary", "ping", (ping >= 0 ? ping : 0), "total_vl", data.getTotalVl());

        Map<String, Integer> vls = data.getAllVls();
        if (vls.isEmpty()) {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.vl.clean");
        } else {
            vls.forEach((chk, vl) -> {
                Check c = plugin.getCheckManager().getCheck(chk);
                int max = c != null ? c.getMaxVl() : 15;
                I18n.send(sender, "command.vl.entry", "check", chk, "vl", vl, "max_vl", max);
            });
        }
    }

    private void handleLogs(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 2) {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.logs.usage", "label", label);
            return;
        }
        String targetName = args[1];
        Player target = Bukkit.getPlayer(targetName);
        UUID uuid = target != null ? target.getUniqueId() : Bukkit.getOfflinePlayer(targetName).getUniqueId();

        String check = parseParam(args, "--check");
        long sinceMs = parseDuration(parseParam(args, "--since"));
        boolean setback = containsFlag(args, "--setback");
        int page = parseIntParam(args, "--page", 1);

        List<LogEntry> logs = plugin.getLogQueryService().queryMemoryLogs(uuid, check, sinceMs, setback);
        if (logs.isEmpty()) {
            I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.logs.empty", "player", targetName);
            return;
        }

        renderLogEntries(sender, "内存快速日志 (近 " + logs.size() + " 条)", logs, page, 8);
    }

    private void handleHistory(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 3) {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.history.usage", "label", label);
            return;
        }
        String targetName = args[1];
        int days = 1;
        try {
            days = Integer.parseInt(args[2]);
        } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {}

        Player target = Bukkit.getPlayer(targetName);
        UUID uuid = target != null ? target.getUniqueId() : Bukkit.getOfflinePlayer(targetName).getUniqueId();

        String check = parseParam(args, "--check");
        boolean setback = containsFlag(args, "--setback");
        int page = parseIntParam(args, "--page", 1);

        I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.history.searching");
        plugin.getLogQueryService().streamHistoricalLogs(uuid, days, entry -> {
            if (check != null && (entry.getCheck() == null || !entry.getCheck().equalsIgnoreCase(check))) return false;
            if (setback && (entry.getFrom() == null || entry.getTo() == null)) return false;
            return true;
        }, results -> {
            if (results.isEmpty()) {
                I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.history.empty");
            } else {
                renderLogEntries(sender, "历史审计档案 (共 " + results.size() + " 条)", results, page, 8);
            }
        });
    }

    private void handleExport(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 2) {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.export.usage", "label", label);
            return;
        }
        String targetName = args[1];
        Player target = Bukkit.getPlayer(targetName);
        UUID uuid = target != null ? target.getUniqueId() : Bukkit.getOfflinePlayer(targetName).getUniqueId();

        I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.export.exporting", "player", targetName);
        plugin.getLogQueryService().exportPlayerData(targetName, uuid, file -> {
            if (file != null && file.exists()) {
                I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.export.success", "path", file.getAbsolutePath(), "count", file.length());
                recordAdminAction(sender, "EXPORT", targetName, "SUCCESS");
            } else {
                I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.export.failed");
                recordAdminAction(sender, "EXPORT", targetName, "FAILED");
            }
        });
    }

    private void handleTop(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {
        long sinceMs = parseDuration(parseParam(args, "--since"));
        if (sinceMs <= 0) sinceMs = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000L;

        plugin.getLogQueryService().queryTopViolators(sinceMs, list -> {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.top.header");
            if (list.isEmpty()) {
                I18n.send(sender, "command.top.empty");
                return;
            }
            int rank = 1;
            for (LogQueryService.TopRecord rec : list) {
                I18n.send(sender, "command.top.entry",
                        "rank", rank++,
                        "player", rec.player(),
                        "check", rec.mainCheck(),
                        "total_vl", rec.totalVl(),
                        "setbacks", rec.setbackCount());
            }
        });
    }

    private void handleStatus(CommandSender sender) {
        var adapter = com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter();
        AuditLogManager logMgr = plugin.getAuditLogManager();
        long size = logMgr.getCurrentFileSize();
        int backlog = logMgr.getQueueBacklog();
        int cap = logMgr.getQueueCapacity();
        long dropped = logMgr.getDroppedCount();
        String hash = logMgr.getLatestHash();
        String flushTime = new SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm:ss").format(new Date(logMgr.getLastFlushTime()));

        I18n.send(sender, "command.status.header");
        I18n.send(sender, "command.status.env",
                "env", com.example.argusac.compat.ServerVersion.get().getFriendlyName(),
                "core", com.example.argusac.compat.ServerVersion.isPaper() ? "Paper" : "Spigot");
        I18n.send(sender, "command.status.adapter", "adapter", adapter.getClass().getSimpleName());

        com.example.argusac.compat.CompatibilityManager compatMgr = plugin.getCompatibilityManager();
        if (compatMgr.hasDegradedModules()) {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.status.degraded");
        } else {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.status.full");
        }

        I18n.send(sender, "command.status.queue", "queue_size", backlog, "capacity", cap, "memory_count", (backlog > cap * 0.5 ? "偏高" : "健康"));
        I18n.send(sender, "command.status.chain", "chain_index", dropped, "hash", (hash != null ? hash.substring(0, Math.min(hash.length(), 16)) + "..." : "None"));
        I18n.send(sender, "command.status.archive", "archive_count", 0, "size", (size / 1024) + " KB");
        String droppedHint = dropped > 0 ? " &8(&c丢弃: " + dropped + "&8)" : "";
        I18n.send(sender, "command.status.flush-time", "time", flushTime, "dropped", droppedHint);
    }

    private void handleVerify(CommandSender sender) {
        I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.verify.running");
        plugin.getLogQueryService().verifyHashChainAsync(result -> {
            if (result.valid()) {
                I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.verify.success");
            } else {
                I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.verify.failed", "line", result.line());
            }
            recordAdminAction(sender, "VERIFY_HASH_CHAIN", "global", result.valid() ? "SUCCESS" : "FAILED");
        });
    }

    private void handleDebug(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 2) {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.debug.usage", "label", label);
            return;
        }
        String check = args[1];
        boolean active = plugin.getCheckDebugLogger().toggle(check);
        String statusStr = active ? "<green>ON</green>" : "<red>OFF</red>";
        I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.debug.result", "check", check, "status", statusStr);
        recordAdminAction(sender, "DEBUG_TOGGLE", check, active ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED");
    }

    private void handleReplay(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 2) {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.replay.usage", "label", label);
            return;
        }
        String targetName = args[1];
        Player target = Bukkit.getPlayer(targetName);
        UUID uuid = target != null ? target.getUniqueId() : Bukkit.getOfflinePlayer(targetName).getUniqueId();
        boolean sideView = containsFlag(args, "--side");

        List<LogEntry> logs = plugin.getLogQueryService().queryMemoryLogs(uuid, null, 60_000L, false);
        if (logs.isEmpty()) {
            I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.replay.empty");
            return;
        }

        LogEntry targetLog = logs.get(logs.size() - 1);
        List<String> rendered = replayRenderer.render(targetLog, sideView);
        for (String line : rendered) {
            sender.sendMessage(ColorUtil.toComponent(line));
        }
    }

    private void handlePurge(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 2) {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.purge.usage", "label", label);
            return;
        }
        String targetName = args[1];
        Player target = Bukkit.getPlayer(targetName);
        UUID uuid = target != null ? target.getUniqueId() : Bukkit.getOfflinePlayer(targetName).getUniqueId();

        plugin.getLogQueryService().purgePlayerData(targetName, uuid);
        I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.purge.success", "player", targetName);
        recordAdminAction(sender, "PURGE_DATA", targetName, "SUCCESS");
    }

    private void handleToggle(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 2) {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.toggle.usage", "label", label);
            return;
        }
        String checkName = args[1];
        Check check = plugin.getCheckManager().getCheck(checkName);
        if (check == null) {
            I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.toggle.not-found", "check", checkName);
            return;
        }

        boolean newState = !check.isEnabled();
        check.setEnabled(newState);
        String statusStr = newState ? "<green>ENABLED</green>" : "<red>DISABLED</red>";
        I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.toggle.result", "check", check.getName(), "status", statusStr);
        recordAdminAction(sender, "TOGGLE_CHECK", check.getName(), newState ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED");
    }

    private void handleSetbackCommand(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 2) {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.setback.usage", "label", label);
            return;
        }

        String targetStr = args[1];
        if ("global".equalsIgnoreCase(targetStr)) {
            if (args.length < 3) {
                I18n.send(sender, "command.setback.usage", "label", label);
                return;
            }
            if ("on".equalsIgnoreCase(args[2])) {
                plugin.getSetbackManager().setGlobalEnabled(true);
                I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.setback.global-toggle", "status", "<green>ON</green>");
                recordAdminAction(sender, "SETBACK_GLOBAL", "global", "ON");
            } else if ("off".equalsIgnoreCase(args[2])) {
                plugin.getSetbackManager().setGlobalEnabled(false);
                I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.setback.global-toggle", "status", "<red>OFF</red>");
                recordAdminAction(sender, "SETBACK_GLOBAL", "global", "OFF");
            } else {
                I18n.send(sender, "command.setback.param-error");
            }
            return;
        }

        Player target = Bukkit.getPlayer(targetStr);
        if (target == null) {
            I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "system.target-not-found", "target", targetStr);
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(target);
        if (data == null) {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.setback.data-error");
            return;
        }

        if (args.length < 3) {
            I18n.send(sender, "command.setback.usage", "label", label);
            return;
        }

        String action = args[2].toLowerCase();
        switch (action) {
            case "disable" -> {
                data.setSetbackOverride(com.example.argusac.model.SetbackOverride.DISABLED);
                I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.setback.override-set", "player", target.getName(), "override", "DISABLED");
                recordAdminAction(sender, "SETBACK_OVERRIDE", target.getName(), "DISABLED");
            }
            case "soft" -> {
                data.setSetbackOverride(com.example.argusac.model.SetbackOverride.SOFT_ONLY);
                I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.setback.override-set", "player", target.getName(), "override", "SOFT_ONLY");
                recordAdminAction(sender, "SETBACK_OVERRIDE", target.getName(), "SOFT_ONLY");
            }
            case "hard" -> {
                data.setSetbackOverride(com.example.argusac.model.SetbackOverride.HARD_ONLY);
                I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.setback.override-set", "player", target.getName(), "override", "HARD_ONLY");
                recordAdminAction(sender, "SETBACK_OVERRIDE", target.getName(), "HARD_ONLY");
            }
            case "default" -> {
                data.setSetbackOverride(com.example.argusac.model.SetbackOverride.DEFAULT);
                I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.setback.override-set", "player", target.getName(), "override", "DEFAULT");
                recordAdminAction(sender, "SETBACK_OVERRIDE", target.getName(), "DEFAULT");
            }
            case "info" -> {
                I18n.send(sender, "command.setback.info-header", "player", target.getName());
                long immunity = Math.max(0, data.getSetbackImmunityUntil() - System.currentTimeMillis());
                I18n.send(sender, "command.setback.info-detail", "override", data.getSetbackOverride().name(), "immunity", immunity, "cooldown", plugin.getSetbackManager().isCheckOnCooldown(target.getUniqueId(), "Speed") ? "Active" : "None");
                com.example.argusac.model.LegitimateLocation last = data.getLastLegitimateLocation();
                I18n.send(sender, "command.setback.info-last", "last_loc", last != null ? last.toString() : "None");
            }
            case "now" -> {
                Check defaultCheck = plugin.getCheckManager().getSpeedCheck();
                boolean success = plugin.getSetbackManager().execute(
                        target, data, defaultCheck, data.getTotalVl(), "Admin forced setback", null);
                if (success) {
                    I18n.sendPrefixed(sender, "command.setback.triggered", "player", target.getName());
                    recordAdminAction(sender, "SETBACK_FORCE", target.getName(), "SUCCESS");
                } else {
                    I18n.send(sender, "command.setback.fail-blocked");
                    recordAdminAction(sender, "SETBACK_FORCE", target.getName(), "FAILED");
                }
            }
            default -> I18n.send(sender, "command.setback.usage", "label", label);
        }
    }

    private void recordAdminAction(CommandSender sender, String action, String target, String result) {
        String actor = sender.getName();
        UUID actorUuid = (sender instanceof Player p) ? p.getUniqueId() : null;
        LogEntry adminLog = new LogEntry.Builder(LogType.ADMIN, plugin.getAuditLogManager().getServerId())
                .actor(actor)
                .actorUuid(actorUuid)
                .action(action)
                .target(target)
                .result(result)
                .build();
        plugin.getAuditLogManager().submitLog(adminLog);
    }

    private void renderLogEntries(CommandSender sender, String title, List<LogEntry> logs, int page, int pageSize) {
        int total = logs.size();
        int totalPages = Math.max(1, (int) Math.ceil((double) total / pageSize));
        int curPage = Math.min(Math.max(1, page), totalPages);

        I18n.send(sender, "command.logs.header", "title", title, "page", curPage, "total_pages", totalPages);

        int start = (curPage - 1) * pageSize;
        int end = Math.min(start + pageSize, total);
        SimpleDateFormat sdf = new SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm:ss");

        for (int i = start; i < end; i++) {
            LogEntry e = logs.get(i);
            String timeStr = sdf.format(new Date(e.getTime()));

            String sevColor = "&a";
            if (e.getSeverity() == LogSeverity.MEDIUM) sevColor = "&e";
            else if (e.getSeverity() == LogSeverity.HIGH) sevColor = "&c";

            String line;
            if (e.getType() == LogType.VIOLATION || e.getType() == LogType.SETBACK_FAIL) {
                String coord = (e.getFrom() != null && e.getTo() != null)
                        ? " " + e.getFrom() + "→" + e.getTo()
                        : "";
                line = String.format("&8[%s] &b%s &7VL&c%d " + sevColor + "%s &3%s%s",
                        timeStr,
                        e.getCheck() != null ? e.getCheck() : "Unknown",
                        e.getVl() != null ? e.getVl() : 0,
                        e.getSeverity() != null ? e.getSeverity().name() : "LOW",
                        e.getMode() != null ? e.getMode() : "NONE",
                        coord);
            } else if (e.getType() == LogType.SESSION) {
                line = String.format("&8[%s] &6SESSION &7%s %s", timeStr, e.getSessionAction(), e.getExtra() != null ? e.getExtra() : "");
            } else if (e.getType() == LogType.ADMIN) {
                line = String.format("&8[%s] &dADMIN &7%s 执行 &e%s &7目标:&b%s", timeStr, e.getActor(), e.getAction(), e.getTarget());
            } else {
                line = String.format("&8[%s] &5INTEGRITY &7行数:&f%d", timeStr, e.getLineCount());
            }

            sender.sendMessage(ColorUtil.toComponent(line));
        }
    }

    private String parseParam(String[] args, String flag) {
        for (int i = 0; i < args.length - 1; i++) {
            if (args[i].equalsIgnoreCase(flag)) return args[i + 1];
        }
        return null;
    }

    private int parseIntParam(String[] args, String flag, int def) {
        String p = parseParam(args, flag);
        if (p == null) return def;
        try { return Integer.parseInt(p); } catch (Exception e) { return def; }
    }

    private boolean containsFlag(String[] args, String flag) {
        for (String s : args) if (s.equalsIgnoreCase(flag)) return true;
        return false;
    }

    private long parseDuration(String str) {
        if (str == null || str.isEmpty()) return -1L;
        try {
            if (str.endsWith("h")) return Long.parseLong(str.substring(0, str.length() - 1)) * 3600_000L;
            if (str.endsWith("d")) return Long.parseLong(str.substring(0, str.length() - 1)) * 86400_000L;
            if (str.endsWith("m")) return Long.parseLong(str.substring(0, str.length() - 1)) * 60_000L;
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        return -1L;
    }

    private void sendHelp(CommandSender sender, String label) {
        sender.sendMessage(I18n.component(sender, "help.header"));
        for (Component entry : I18n.componentList(sender, "help.entries")) {
            sender.sendMessage(entry);
        }
        sender.sendMessage(I18n.component(sender, "help.footer"));
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/command/ArgusTabCompleter.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.command;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.command.Command;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;
import org.bukkit.command.TabCompleter;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * 命令补全器 (ArgusTabCompleter)
 * 完整覆盖重构后的所有子命令及其标志参数
 */
public class ArgusTabCompleter implements TabCompleter {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;
    private final List<String> subCommands = Arrays.asList(
            "help", "reload", "vl", "logs", "history", "export",
            "top", "status", "verify", "debug", "replay", "my-data",
            "purge", "compat", "toggle", "setback", "alerts"
    );
    private final List<String> setbackActions = Arrays.asList("disable", "soft", "hard", "default", "now", "info");
    private final List<String> logFilterFlags = Arrays.asList("--check", "--since", "--setback", "--page");

    public ArgusTabCompleter(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    @Override
    public List<String> onTabComplete(CommandSender sender, Command command, String alias, String[] args) {
        boolean hasAdmin = sender.hasPermission("argusac.admin") || sender.hasPermission("ArgusAC.admin");
        boolean hasAlert = sender.hasPermission("argusac.alert") || sender.hasPermission("ArgusAC.alert");

        if (!hasAdmin) {
            if (args.length == 1) {
                List<String> available = new ArrayList<>(Arrays.asList("help", "my-data"));
                if (hasAlert) {
                    available.add("alerts");
                }
                return available.stream()
                        .filter(sub -> sub.startsWith(args[0].toLowerCase()))
                        .collect(Collectors.toList());
            }
            if (hasAlert && args.length == 2
                    && "alerts".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0])) {
                return Arrays.asList("on", "off", "status").stream()
                        .filter(action -> action.startsWith(args[1].toLowerCase()))
                        .collect(Collectors.toList());
            }
            return Collections.emptyList();
        }

        if (args.length == 1) {
            return subCommands.stream()
                    .filter(s -> s.toLowerCase().startsWith(args[0].toLowerCase()))
                    .collect(Collectors.toList());
        }

        String sub = args[0].toLowerCase();

        if (args.length == 2) {
            if ("alerts".equals(sub)) {
                return Arrays.asList("on", "off", "status").stream()
                        .filter(action -> action.startsWith(args[1].toLowerCase()))
                        .collect(Collectors.toList());
            }
            if ("vl".equals(sub) || "logs".equals(sub) || "history".equals(sub) ||
                "export".equals(sub) || "replay".equals(sub) || "purge".equals(sub)) {
                return Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers().stream()
                        .map(p -> p.getName())
                        .filter(name -> name.toLowerCase().startsWith(args[1].toLowerCase()))
                        .collect(Collectors.toList());
            }

            if ("toggle".equals(sub) || "debug".equals(sub)) {
                return plugin.getCheckManager().getAllChecks().stream()
                        .map(c -> c.getName())
                        .filter(name -> name.toLowerCase().startsWith(args[1].toLowerCase()))
                        .collect(Collectors.toList());
            }

            if ("top".equals(sub)) {
                return Collections.singletonList("--since");
            }

            if ("setback".equals(sub)) {
                List<String> list = new ArrayList<>();
                list.add("global");
                for (Player p : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
                    list.add(p.getName());
                }
                return list.stream()
                        .filter(name -> name.toLowerCase().startsWith(args[1].toLowerCase()))
                        .collect(Collectors.toList());
            }
        }

        if (args.length == 3) {
            if ("setback".equalsIgnoreCase(sub)) {
                List<String> actions = "global".equalsIgnoreCase(args[1]) ? Arrays.asList("on", "off") : setbackActions;
                return actions.stream()
                        .filter(act -> act.toLowerCase().startsWith(args[2].toLowerCase()))
                        .collect(Collectors.toList());
            }
            if ("history".equalsIgnoreCase(sub)) {
                return Arrays.asList("1", "3", "7", "30");
            }
            if ("top".equalsIgnoreCase(sub) && "--since".equalsIgnoreCase(args[1])) {
                return Arrays.asList("1h", "6h", "24h", "7d");
            }
            if ("replay".equalsIgnoreCase(sub)) {
                return Collections.singletonList("--side");
            }
            if ("logs".equalsIgnoreCase(sub)) {
                return logFilterFlags.stream()
                        .filter(f -> f.toLowerCase().startsWith(args[2].toLowerCase()))
                        .collect(Collectors.toList());
            }
        }

        if (args.length > 3 && ("logs".equalsIgnoreCase(sub) || "history".equalsIgnoreCase(sub))) {
            String prev = args[args.length - 2].toLowerCase();
            if ("--check".equals(prev)) {
                return plugin.getCheckManager().getAllChecks().stream()
                        .map(c -> c.getName())
                        .filter(name -> name.toLowerCase().startsWith(args[args.length - 1].toLowerCase()))
                        .collect(Collectors.toList());
            }
            if ("--since".equals(prev)) {
                return Arrays.asList("1h", "24h", "7d");
            }
            return logFilterFlags.stream()
                    .filter(f -> f.toLowerCase().startsWith(args[args.length - 1].toLowerCase()))
                    .collect(Collectors.toList());
        }

        return Collections.emptyList();
    }
}
```

```java
package com.example.argusac.compat;

public enum CheckCompatStatus {
    FULL("&a完整支持", "当前服务端核心 API 满足本模块全部高精度数学检测模型"),
    DEGRADED("&e降级运行", "部分高版本 API 不可用，已自动切换为几何近似或旧版本兜底模型"),
    DISABLED("&c版本禁用", "当前版本缺少本机制所需的基础物理特性，已优雅静默关闭以杜绝误报");

    private final String display;
    private final String description;

    CheckCompatStatus(String display, String description) {
        this.display = display;
        this.description = description;
    }

    public String getDisplay() { return display; }
    public String getDescription() { return description; }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/compat/CompatBoundingBox.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.compat;

import org.bukkit.Location;

/**
 * 跨版本统一实体与方块碰撞箱结构体
 */
public record CompatBoundingBox(double minX, double minY, double minZ, double maxX, double maxY, double maxZ) {

    public boolean intersects(CompatBoundingBox other) {
        return this.minX <= other.maxX && this.maxX >= other.minX &&
               this.minY <= other.maxY && this.maxY >= other.minY &&
               this.minZ <= other.maxZ && this.maxZ >= other.minZ;
    }

    public boolean intersectsWithMargin(CompatBoundingBox other, double margin) {
        return this.minX < other.maxX - margin && this.maxX > other.minX + margin &&
               this.minY < other.maxY - margin && this.maxY > other.minY + margin &&
               this.minZ < other.maxZ - margin && this.maxZ > other.minZ + margin;
    }

    public CompatBoundingBox expand(double amount) {
        return new CompatBoundingBox(
                minX - amount, minY - amount, minZ - amount,
                maxX + amount, maxY + amount, maxZ + amount
        );
    }

    public double distanceTo(Location loc) {
        double clampedX = Math.max(minX, Math.min(loc.getX(), maxX));
        double clampedY = Math.max(minY, Math.min(loc.getY(), maxY));
        double clampedZ = Math.max(minZ, Math.min(loc.getZ(), maxZ));
        double dx = loc.getX() - clampedX;
        double dy = loc.getY() - clampedY;
        double dz = loc.getZ() - clampedZ;
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
    }

    public double getCenterX() { return (minX + maxX) / 2.0; }
    public double getCenterY() { return (minY + maxY) / 2.0; }
    public double getCenterZ() { return (minZ + maxZ) / 2.0; }

    public double getWidth() { return maxX - minX; }
    public double getHeight() { return maxY - minY; }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/compat/CompatibilityManager.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.compat;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.Check;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 启动时自检与运行时能力矩阵调度中心
 */
public class CompatibilityManager {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;
    private final Map<String, CheckCompatStatus> checkStatusMap = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, String> statusReasons = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    public CompatibilityManager(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        // 注意：此处不调用 evaluateAllChecks()，因为 checkManager 在主类 onEnable 第 78 行才初始化。
        // 真正生效的 evaluateAllChecks() 调用在 ArgusACPlugin.onEnable() 第 79 行执行。
    }

    public void evaluateAllChecks() {
        checkStatusMap.clear();
        statusReasons.clear();

        // 1. ElytraFly 鞘翅飞行
        if (ServerVersion.isOlderThan(ServerVersion.V1_12_2)) {
            markCheck("ElytraFly", CheckCompatStatus.DISABLED, "Minecraft 1.9 以前不存在滑翔机制");
        } else {
            markCheck("ElytraFly", CheckCompatStatus.FULL, "完整支持滑翔姿态采样");
        }

        // 2. Speed / Fly 移动检测
        if (ServerVersion.isOlderThan(ServerVersion.V1_13)) {
            markCheck("Speed", CheckCompatStatus.DEGRADED, "1.13 以前无 swimming 姿态，在水中采用老版浮力模型推算");
            markCheck("Fly", CheckCompatStatus.FULL, "基础位移向量与重力加速度全版本支持");
        } else {
            markCheck("Speed", CheckCompatStatus.FULL, "支持游泳与原生 BoundingBox 边缘碰撞");
            markCheck("Fly", CheckCompatStatus.FULL, "完整支持现代高度边界计算");
        }

        // 3. KillAura / Reach 战斗检测
        if (ServerVersion.isOlderThan(ServerVersion.V1_13)) {
            markCheck("Reach", CheckCompatStatus.DEGRADED, "无 Entity#getBoundingBox，采用 0.6x1.8 盒状近似计算");
        } else {
            markCheck("Reach", CheckCompatStatus.FULL, "支持原生精确碰撞盒交互距离运算");
        }

        // 4. BadPackets 协议检测
        boolean hasProtocolLib = Bukkit.getPluginManager().getPlugin("ProtocolLib") != null;
        if (!hasProtocolLib) {
            markCheck("BadPackets", CheckCompatStatus.DEGRADED, "未装载 ProtocolLib，仅依赖 Bukkit 移动/传送事件做基础校验");
        } else {
            markCheck("BadPackets", CheckCompatStatus.FULL, "ProtocolLib 联动已建立，支持底层数据包时序追踪");
        }

        // 5. 其余基础检测 (NoFall, FastBreak, Scaffold, InventoryMove)
        markCheck("NoFall", CheckCompatStatus.FULL, "坠落动量与地面状态全版本支持");
        markCheck("FastBreak", CheckCompatStatus.FULL, "方块挖掘时序全版本支持");
        markCheck("Scaffold", CheckCompatStatus.FULL, "搭建朝向与视线交点全版本支持");
        markCheck("InventoryMove", CheckCompatStatus.FULL, "背包开启状态监听全版本支持");
    }

    private void markCheck(String checkName, CheckCompatStatus status, String reason) {
        checkStatusMap.put(checkName, status);
        statusReasons.put(checkName, reason);

        if (plugin.getCheckManager() != null) {
            Check check = plugin.getCheckManager().getCheck(checkName);
            if (check != null && status == CheckCompatStatus.DISABLED) {
                check.setEnabled(false);
            }
        }
    }

    public CheckCompatStatus getStatus(String checkName) {
        return checkStatusMap.getOrDefault(checkName, CheckCompatStatus.FULL);
    }

    public String getReason(String checkName) {
        return statusReasons.getOrDefault(checkName, "正常运转");
    }

    public Map<String, CheckCompatStatus> getAllStatus() {
        return checkStatusMap;
    }

    public boolean hasDegradedModules() {
        return checkStatusMap.values().stream().anyMatch(s -> s == CheckCompatStatus.DEGRADED || s == CheckCompatStatus.DISABLED);
    }

    public void printStartupDiagnosis() {
        Bukkit.getLogger().info("==================================================");
        Bukkit.getLogger().info(" [ArgusAC] 多版本自适应环境自检就绪:");
        Bukkit.getLogger().info(" 识别内核: " + ServerVersion.get().getFriendlyName() + " (" + (ServerVersion.isPaper() ? "Paper" : "Spigot/CraftBukkit") + ")");
        Bukkit.getLogger().info(" 版本编码: " + ServerVersion.getCode());
        Bukkit.getLogger().info(" 适配器通道: " + VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().getClass().getSimpleName());
        Bukkit.getLogger().info(" 模块能力矩阵: " + checkStatusMap.size() + " 个检测项已分配运行等级");
        if (hasDegradedModules()) {
            Bukkit.getLogger().info(" [提示] 检测到部分模块运行在降级兼容模式，使用 /argus compat 可查看详情。");
        }
        Bukkit.getLogger().info("==================================================");
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/compat/ServerVersion.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.compat;

import org.bukkit.Bukkit;

import java.util.regex.Matcher;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * 服务器核心版本识别与比较工具 (集中化管理，严禁在检测模块内散落解析)
 * 采用 5 位整数编码规则：major * 10000 + minor * 100 + patch
 * 例：1.12.2 -> 11202, 1.16.5 -> 11605, 1.20.4 -> 12004, 1.21.1 -> 12101
 */
public enum ServerVersion {
    UNKNOWN(0, "Unknown"),
    V1_12_2(11202, "1.12.2"),
    V1_13(11300, "1.13"),
    V1_13_2(11302, "1.13.2"),
    V1_14_4(11404, "1.14.4"),
    V1_15_2(11502, "1.15.2"),
    V1_16_5(11605, "1.16.5"),
    V1_17_1(11701, "1.17.1"),
    V1_18_2(11802, "1.18.2"),
    V1_19_4(11904, "1.19.4"),
    V1_20_4(12004, "1.20.4"),
    V1_21(12100, "1.21");

    private final int versionCode;
    private final String friendlyName;

    private static final ServerVersion CURRENT_VERSION;
    private static final int CURRENT_CODE;
    private static final boolean IS_PAPER;

    static {
        boolean paperCheck = false;
        try {
            Class.forName("com.destroystokyo.paper.PaperConfig");
            paperCheck = true;
        } catch (ClassNotFoundException ignored) {
            try {
                Class.forName("io.papermc.paper.configuration.Configuration");
                paperCheck = true;
            } catch (ClassNotFoundException ignored2) {
                paperCheck = false;
            }
        }
        IS_PAPER = paperCheck;

        int code = parseServerVersionCode();
        CURRENT_CODE = code;
        CURRENT_VERSION = matchVersionFromCode(code);
    }

    ServerVersion(int versionCode, String friendlyName) {
        this.versionCode = versionCode;
        this.friendlyName = friendlyName;
    }

    public int getVersionCode() {
        return versionCode;
    }

    public String getFriendlyName() {
        return friendlyName;
    }

    public static ServerVersion get() {
        return CURRENT_VERSION;
    }

    public static int getCode() {
        return CURRENT_CODE;
    }

    public static boolean isPaper() {
        return IS_PAPER;
    }

    public static boolean isAtLeast(ServerVersion target) {
        return CURRENT_CODE >= target.versionCode;
    }

    public static boolean isOlderThan(ServerVersion target) {
        return CURRENT_CODE < target.versionCode;
    }

    public static boolean isBetween(ServerVersion min, ServerVersion max) {
        return CURRENT_CODE >= min.versionCode && CURRENT_CODE <= max.versionCode;
    }

    private static int parseServerVersionCode() {
        try {
            String versionStr = Bukkit.getVersion();
            Pattern pattern = Pattern.compile("(?i)\\(MC:\\s*([0-9]+)\\.([0-9]+)(?:\\.([0-9]+))?\\)");
            Matcher matcher = pattern.matcher(versionStr);

            if (matcher.find()) {
                int major = Integer.parseInt(matcher.group(1));
                int minor = Integer.parseInt(matcher.group(2));
                int patch = (matcher.group(3) != null) ? Integer.parseInt(matcher.group(3)) : 0;
                return major * 10000 + minor * 100 + patch;
            }

            String bukkitVer = Bukkit.getBukkitVersion();
            Pattern bPattern = Pattern.compile("^([0-9]+)\\.([0-9]+)(?:\\.([0-9]+))?");
            Matcher bMatcher = bPattern.matcher(bukkitVer);
            if (bMatcher.find()) {
                int major = Integer.parseInt(bMatcher.group(1));
                int minor = Integer.parseInt(bMatcher.group(2));
                int patch = (bMatcher.group(3) != null) ? Integer.parseInt(bMatcher.group(3)) : 0;
                return major * 10000 + minor * 100 + patch;
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}

        return 12004;
    }

    private static ServerVersion matchVersionFromCode(int code) {
        ServerVersion best = UNKNOWN;
        for (ServerVersion v : values()) {
            if (v == UNKNOWN) continue;
            if (code >= v.versionCode) {
                best = v;
            }
        }
        return best;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/compat/VersionAdapter.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.compat;

import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

/**
 * 跨版本多态抽象接口：所有存在 NoSuchMethodError 风险的操作必须经过本接口
 */
public interface VersionAdapter {

    // 玩家状态
    int getPing(Player player);

    boolean isSwimming(Player player);

    boolean isGliding(Player player);

    boolean isRiptiding(Player player);

    boolean isInWater(Player player);

    float getAttackCooldown(Player player);

    String getClientBrand(Player player);

    // 几何与碰撞
    CompatBoundingBox getEntityBoundingBox(Entity entity);

    CompatBoundingBox getBlockBoundingBox(Block block);

    boolean isSolidBlock(Block block);

    boolean isSpacePassable(World world, CompatBoundingBox box);

    // 环境与方块材质特性安全判定 (彻底消除 NoSuchFieldError)
    boolean isIce(Block block);

    boolean isSlime(Block block);

    boolean isHoney(Block block);

    boolean isSoulSand(Block block);

    boolean isClimbable(Block block);

    boolean isLiquid(Block block);

    // 世界与环境
    int getMinHeight(World world);

    int getMaxHeight(World world);

    double[] getTPS();

    // 状态属性
    boolean hasPotionEffect(LivingEntity entity, CompatPotionType potionType);

    int getPotionAmplifier(LivingEntity entity, CompatPotionType potionType);

    double getAttributeValue(LivingEntity entity, CompatAttribute attribute, double defaultValue);

    // 交互与渲染
    void sendMessage(CommandSender sender, String colorFormattedText);

    void sendActionBar(Player player, String colorFormattedText);

    void playSound(Player player, Location loc, String soundName, float volume, float pitch);
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/compat/VersionAdapterFactory.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.compat;

import com.example.argusac.compat.impl.LegacyVersionAdapter;
import com.example.argusac.compat.impl.ModernVersionAdapter;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;

/**
 * 适配器工厂：根据服务器版本与环境动态分发最匹配的实现
 */
public final class VersionAdapterFactory {

    private static final VersionAdapter ADAPTER;

    static {
        if (ServerVersion.isAtLeast(ServerVersion.V1_20_4) && ServerVersion.isPaper()) {
            ADAPTER = new ModernVersionAdapter();
            Bukkit.getLogger().info("[ArgusAC-Compat] 运行环境判定: Paper 1.20+ 架构 -> 已激活 [现代原生适配器]");
        } else {
            ADAPTER = new LegacyVersionAdapter();
            Bukkit.getLogger().info(String.format("[ArgusAC-Compat] 运行环境判定: %s (编码: %d) -> 已激活 [跨版本兼容与降级适配器]",
                    ServerVersion.get().getFriendlyName(), ServerVersion.getCode()));
        }
    }

    public static VersionAdapter getAdapter() {
        return ADAPTER;
    }
}
```
