`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/compat/impl/LegacyVersionAdapter.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.compat.impl;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.compat.CompatAttribute;
import com.example.argusac.compat.CompatBoundingBox;
import com.example.argusac.compat.CompatPotionType;
import com.example.argusac.compat.ServerVersion;
import com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapter;
import com.example.argusac.util.ColorUtil;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.ChatColor;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Sound;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffect;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.logging.Level;

/**
 * 低版本与 Spigot 降级实现类 (包含反射穿透、NMS 碰撞箱安全采样与零崩溃兜底)
 */
@SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
public class LegacyVersionAdapter implements VersionAdapter {

    private Method getHandleMethod;
    private Field pingField;
    private boolean pingReflectionFailed = false;

    // NMS 实体 AxisAlignedBB 反射
    private Method getBoundingBoxMethod;
    private Field minXField, minYField, minZField, maxXField, maxYField, maxZField;
    private boolean aabbReflectionFailed = false;

    // Spigot TPS 反射缓存
    private Object minecraftServerInstance;
    private Field recentTpsField;
    private boolean tpsReflectionFailed = false;

    // 材质名称集合缓存 (避免使用高版本 Material 枚举静态常量导致 NoSuchFieldError)
    private static final Set<String> ICE_MATERIALS = new HashSet<>(Arrays.asList("ICE", "PACKED_ICE", "FROSTED_ICE", "BLUE_ICE"));
    private static final Set<String> CLIMBABLE_MATERIALS = new HashSet<>(Arrays.asList(
            "LADDER", "VINE", "SCAFFOLDING", "COBWEB", "POWDER_SNOW", "WEEPING_VINES", "TWISTING_VINES"
    ));

    public LegacyVersionAdapter() {
        initPlayerReflection();
        initAabbReflection();
        initTpsReflection();
    }

    private void initPlayerReflection() {
        try {
            Class<?> craftPlayerClass = Class.forName(Bukkit.getServer().getClass().getPackage().getName() + ".entity.CraftPlayer");
            this.getHandleMethod = craftPlayerClass.getMethod("getHandle");
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }

    private void initAabbReflection() {
        try {
            Class<?> nmsEntityClass = Class.forName(Bukkit.getServer().getClass().getPackage().getName().replace("org.bukkit.craftbukkit", "net.minecraft.server") + ".Entity");
            this.getBoundingBoxMethod = nmsEntityClass.getMethod("getBoundingBox");
            Class<?> aabbClass = Class.forName(Bukkit.getServer().getClass().getPackage().getName().replace("org.bukkit.craftbukkit", "net.minecraft.server") + ".AxisAlignedBB");
            this.minXField = aabbClass.getField("a");
            this.minYField = aabbClass.getField("b");
            this.minZField = aabbClass.getField("c");
            this.maxXField = aabbClass.getField("d");
            this.maxYField = aabbClass.getField("e");
            this.maxZField = aabbClass.getField("f");
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            this.aabbReflectionFailed = true;
        }
    }

    private void initTpsReflection() {
        try {
            Class<?> craftServerClass = Class.forName(Bukkit.getServer().getClass().getPackage().getName() + ".CraftServer");
            Method getServerMethod = craftServerClass.getMethod("getServer");
            this.minecraftServerInstance = getServerMethod.invoke(Bukkit.getServer());
            Class<?> mcServerClass = this.minecraftServerInstance.getClass();
            this.recentTpsField = mcServerClass.getField("recentTps");
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            this.tpsReflectionFailed = true;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public int getPing(Player player) {
        if (ServerVersion.isAtLeast(ServerVersion.V1_16_5)) {
            try {
                return player.getPing();
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }

        if (pingReflectionFailed || getHandleMethod == null) return -1;

        try {
            Object nmsPlayer = getHandleMethod.invoke(player);
            if (pingField == null) {
                try {
                    pingField = nmsPlayer.getClass().getField("ping");
                } catch (NoSuchFieldException e) {
                    pingField = nmsPlayer.getClass().getDeclaredField("ping");
                    pingField.setAccessible(true);
                }
            }
            return pingField.getInt(nmsPlayer);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            pingReflectionFailed = true;
            Bukkit.getLogger().log(Level.FINE, "[ArgusAC-Compat] 反射读取 NMS 延迟失败，后续转为默认容差: " + e.getMessage());
            return -1;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSwimming(Player player) {
        if (ServerVersion.isAtLeast(ServerVersion.V1_13)) {
            try {
                return player.isSwimming();
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        // 1.12.2 降级逻辑：玩家处于水中
        return isInWater(player);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isGliding(Player player) {
        try {
            return player.isGliding();
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isRiptiding(Player player) {
        if (ServerVersion.isAtLeast(ServerVersion.V1_13)) {
            try {
                return player.isRiptiding();
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isInWater(Player player) {
        if (ServerVersion.isAtLeast(ServerVersion.V1_15_2)) {
            try {
                return player.isInWater();
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        String matName = player.getLocation().getBlock().getType().name();
        return matName.contains("WATER");
    }

    @Override
    public float getAttackCooldown(Player player) {
        try {
            return player.getCooledAttackStrength(0.0f);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            return 1.0f;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String getClientBrand(Player player) {
        if (ServerVersion.isAtLeast(ServerVersion.V1_13)) {
            try {
                String brand = player.getClientBrandName();
                if (brand != null) return brand;
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        if (ArgusACPlugin.getInstance() != null && ArgusACPlugin.getInstance().getPlayerManager() != null) {
            var data = ArgusACPlugin.getInstance().getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
            if (data != null && data.getClientBrand() != null) {
                return data.getClientBrand();
            }
        }
        return "legacy/vanilla";
    }

    @Override
    public CompatBoundingBox getEntityBoundingBox(Entity entity) {
        if (ServerVersion.isAtLeast(ServerVersion.V1_13)) {
            try {
                org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox b = entity.getBoundingBox();
                return new CompatBoundingBox(b.getMinX(), b.getMinY(), b.getMinZ(), b.getMaxX(), b.getMaxY(), b.getMaxZ());
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }

        // 1.12.2 尝试反射 NMS AxisAlignedBB
        if (!aabbReflectionFailed && getHandleMethod != null && getBoundingBoxMethod != null) {
            try {
                Object nmsEntity = getHandleMethod.invoke(entity);
                Object aabb = getBoundingBoxMethod.invoke(nmsEntity);
                if (aabb != null) {
                    return new CompatBoundingBox(
                            minXField.getDouble(aabb),
                            minYField.getDouble(aabb),
                            minZField.getDouble(aabb),
                            maxXField.getDouble(aabb),
                            maxYField.getDouble(aabb),
                            maxZField.getDouble(aabb)
                    );
                }
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                this.aabbReflectionFailed = true;
            }
        }

        // 安全数学兜底：基于中心坐标与标准尺寸构建盒模型
        Location loc = entity.getLocation();
        double w = 0.6 / 2.0;
        double h = 1.8;
        return new CompatBoundingBox(loc.getX() - w, loc.getY(), loc.getZ() - w, loc.getX() + w, loc.getY() + h, loc.getZ() + w);
    }

    @Override
    public CompatBoundingBox getBlockBoundingBox(Block block) {
        if (ServerVersion.isAtLeast(ServerVersion.V1_13)) {
            try {
                org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox b = block.getBoundingBox();
                return new CompatBoundingBox(b.getMinX(), b.getMinY(), b.getMinZ(), b.getMaxX(), b.getMaxY(), b.getMaxZ());
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        int x = block.getX();
        int y = block.getY();
        int z = block.getZ();
        return new CompatBoundingBox(x, y, z, x + 1.0, y + 1.0, z + 1.0);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSolidBlock(Block block) {
        try {
            return block.getType().isSolid();
        } catch (Throwable e) {
            return true;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSpacePassable(World world, CompatBoundingBox box) {
        if (world == null || box == null) {
            return false;
        }
        int minBlockX = (int) Math.floor(box.minX());
        int maxBlockX = (int) Math.floor(box.maxX());
        int minBlockY = (int) Math.floor(box.minY());
        int maxBlockY = (int) Math.floor(box.maxY());
        int minBlockZ = (int) Math.floor(box.minZ());
        int maxBlockZ = (int) Math.floor(box.maxZ());

        int minChunkX = minBlockX >> 4;
        int maxChunkX = maxBlockX >> 4;
        int minChunkZ = minBlockZ >> 4;
        int maxChunkZ = maxBlockZ >> 4;
        for (int cx = minChunkX; cx <= maxChunkX; cx++) {
            for (int cz = minChunkZ; cz <= maxChunkZ; cz++) {
                if (!world.isChunkLoaded(cx, cz)) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        for (int x = minBlockX; x <= maxBlockX; x++) {
            for (int y = minBlockY; y <= maxBlockY; y++) {
                for (int z = minBlockZ; z <= maxBlockZ; z++) {
                    Block block = world.getBlockAt(x, y, z);
                    if (block.getType() == org.bukkit.Material.AIR || isLiquid(block)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (ServerVersion.isAtLeast(ServerVersion.V1_13)) {
                        try {
                            if (block.isPassable()) {
                                continue;
                            }
                        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                    } else {
                        try {
                            if (!block.getType().isSolid()) {
                                continue;
                            }
                        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                    }
                    CompatBoundingBox blockBox = getBlockBoundingBox(block);
                    if (box.intersectsWithMargin(blockBox, 1.0e-4)) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isIce(Block block) {
        return ICE_MATERIALS.contains(block.getType().name());
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSlime(Block block) {
        return "SLIME_BLOCK".equals(block.getType().name());
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isHoney(Block block) {
        return "HONEY_BLOCK".equals(block.getType().name());
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSoulSand(Block block) {
        String name = block.getType().name();
        return "SOUL_SAND".equals(name) || "SOUL_SOIL".equals(name);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isClimbable(Block block) {
        String name = block.getType().name();
        return CLIMBABLE_MATERIALS.contains(name) || name.contains("VINE") || name.contains("BERRY");
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isLiquid(Block block) {
        try {
            return block.isLiquid();
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            String name = block.getType().name();
            return name.contains("WATER") || name.contains("LAVA");
        }
    }

    @Override
    public int getMinHeight(World world) {
        if (ServerVersion.isAtLeast(ServerVersion.V1_17_1)) {
            try {
                return world.getMinHeight();
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        return 0;
    }

    @Override
    public int getMaxHeight(World world) {
        if (ServerVersion.isAtLeast(ServerVersion.V1_17_1)) {
            try {
                return world.getMaxHeight();
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        return 256;
    }

    @Override
    public double[] getTPS() {
        if (ServerVersion.isPaper()) {
            try {
                return Bukkit.getTPS();
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }

        // Spigot 环境反射读取 recentTps
        if (!tpsReflectionFailed && minecraftServerInstance != null && recentTpsField != null) {
            try {
                return (double[]) recentTpsField.get(minecraftServerInstance);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                tpsReflectionFailed = true;
            }
        }

        // 最终兜底：内置 TickMonitor 采样器或安全默认值
        double currentTps = TickMonitor.getEstimatedTps();
        return new double[]{ currentTps, currentTps, currentTps };
    }

    @Override
    public boolean hasPotionEffect(LivingEntity entity, CompatPotionType potionType) {
        PotionEffectType type = matchLegacyPotionType(potionType);
        return type != null && entity.hasPotionEffect(type);
    }

    @Override
    public int getPotionAmplifier(LivingEntity entity, CompatPotionType potionType) {
        PotionEffectType type = matchLegacyPotionType(potionType);
        if (type == null) return -1;
        for (PotionEffect effect : entity.getActivePotionEffects()) {
            if (effect.getType().equals(type)) {
                return effect.getAmplifier();
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }

    @Override
    public double getAttributeValue(LivingEntity entity, CompatAttribute attribute, double defaultValue) {
        try {
            org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute bukkitAttr = org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute.valueOf(attribute.name());
            org.bukkit.attribute.AttributeInstance instance = entity.getAttribute(bukkitAttr);
            return instance != null ? instance.getValue() : defaultValue;
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            return defaultValue;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void sendMessage(CommandSender sender, String colorFormattedText) {
        try {
            sender.sendMessage(ColorUtil.toComponent(colorFormattedText));
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            sender.sendMessage(ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', colorFormattedText));
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void sendActionBar(Player player, String colorFormattedText) {
        try {
            player.sendActionBar(ColorUtil.toComponent(colorFormattedText));
            return;
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}

        String msg = ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', colorFormattedText);
        try {
            player.spigot().sendMessage(net.md_5.bungee.api.ChatMessageType.ACTION_BAR,
                    net.md_5.bungee.api.chat.TextComponent.fromLegacyText(msg));
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            player.sendMessage(msg);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void playSound(Player player, Location loc, String soundName, float volume, float pitch) {
        try {
            Sound sound = Sound.valueOf(mapLegacySoundName(soundName));
            player.playSound(loc, sound, volume, pitch);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    private PotionEffectType matchLegacyPotionType(CompatPotionType type) {
        return switch (type) {
            case SPEED -> PotionEffectType.SPEED;
            case SLOWNESS -> PotionEffectType.SLOW;
            case JUMP_BOOST -> PotionEffectType.JUMP;
            case LEVITATION -> PotionEffectType.getByName("LEVITATION");
            case SLOW_FALLING -> PotionEffectType.getByName("SLOW_FALLING");
            case DOLPHINS_GRACE -> PotionEffectType.getByName("DOLPHINS_GRACE");
            case HASTE -> PotionEffectType.FAST_DIGGING;
            case MINING_FATIGUE -> PotionEffectType.SLOW_DIGGING;
        };
    }

    private String mapLegacySoundName(String soundName) {
        if (ServerVersion.isOlderThan(ServerVersion.V1_13)) {
            if ("BLOCK_NOTE_BLOCK_BASS".equalsIgnoreCase(soundName)) return "BLOCK_NOTE_BASS";
            if ("ENTITY_EXPERIENCE_ORB_PICKUP".equalsIgnoreCase(soundName)) return "ENTITY_EXPERIENCE_ORB_TOUCH";
        }
        return soundName;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/compat/impl/ModernVersionAdapter.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.compat.impl;

import com.example.argusac.compat.CompatAttribute;
import com.example.argusac.compat.CompatBoundingBox;
import com.example.argusac.compat.CompatPotionType;
import com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapter;
import com.example.argusac.util.ColorUtil;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Sound;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute;
import org.bukkit.attribute.AttributeInstance;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffect;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;

import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 现代版本适配实现 (面向 Paper 1.20+ 原生特性，具备最高的执行效率)
 * 注意：potionType 解析使用 getByName 动态查找+缓存，适配 1.20.4 ~ 1.21.x 常量名称变更
 */
@SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
public class ModernVersionAdapter implements VersionAdapter {

    /**
     * PotionEffectType 静态名称映射表 (优先使用新名，回退旧名)。
     * 1.20.4 API: SLOW / JUMP / FAST_DIGGING / SLOW_DIGGING
     * 1.20.6+ API: SLOWNESS / JUMP_BOOST / HASTE / MINING_FATIGUE (旧名保留为 alias)
     * 通过 getByName() 运行时动态解析，彻底规避跨版本常量丢失问题。
     */
    private static final Map<CompatPotionType, PotionEffectType> POTION_TYPE_CACHE;

    static {
        POTION_TYPE_CACHE = new EnumMap<>(CompatPotionType.class);
        register(CompatPotionType.SPEED,          "SPEED");
        register(CompatPotionType.SLOWNESS,        "SLOWNESS",       "SLOW");
        register(CompatPotionType.JUMP_BOOST,      "JUMP_BOOST",     "JUMP");
        register(CompatPotionType.LEVITATION,      "LEVITATION");
        register(CompatPotionType.SLOW_FALLING,    "SLOW_FALLING");
        register(CompatPotionType.DOLPHINS_GRACE,  "DOLPHINS_GRACE");
        register(CompatPotionType.HASTE,           "HASTE",          "FAST_DIGGING");
        register(CompatPotionType.MINING_FATIGUE,  "MINING_FATIGUE", "SLOW_DIGGING");
    }

    /**
     * 按名称列表优先级尝试注册 PotionEffectType，首个成功命中者缓存。
     */
    private static void register(CompatPotionType key, String... names) {
        for (String name : names) {
            PotionEffectType pet = PotionEffectType.getByName(name);
            if (pet != null) {
                POTION_TYPE_CACHE.put(key, pet);
                return;
            }
        }
        // getByName 全部失败时尝试通过枚举字段名反射匹配
        for (String name : names) {
            try {
                java.lang.reflect.Field f = PotionEffectType.class.getField(name);
                Object val = f.get(null);
                if (val instanceof PotionEffectType pet) {
                    POTION_TYPE_CACHE.put(key, pet);
                    return;
                }
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
    }

    @Override
    public int getPing(Player player) {
        return player.getPing();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSwimming(Player player) {
        return player.isSwimming();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isGliding(Player player) {
        return player.isGliding();
    }

    @Override
    public float getAttackCooldown(Player player) {
        return player.getCooledAttackStrength(0.0f);
    }

    @Override
    public String getClientBrand(Player player) {
        String brand = player.getClientBrandName();
        return brand != null ? brand : "vanilla";
    }

    @Override
    public CompatBoundingBox getEntityBoundingBox(Entity entity) {
        BoundingBox b = entity.getBoundingBox();
        return new CompatBoundingBox(b.getMinX(), b.getMinY(), b.getMinZ(), b.getMaxX(), b.getMaxY(), b.getMaxZ());
    }

    @Override
    public CompatBoundingBox getBlockBoundingBox(Block block) {
        BoundingBox b = block.getBoundingBox();
        return new CompatBoundingBox(b.getMinX(), b.getMinY(), b.getMinZ(), b.getMaxX(), b.getMaxY(), b.getMaxZ());
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isRiptiding(Player player) {
        return player.isRiptiding();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isInWater(Player player) {
        return player.isInWater();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSolidBlock(Block block) {
        return block.getType().isSolid();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSpacePassable(World world, CompatBoundingBox box) {
        if (world == null || box == null) {
            return false;
        }
        int minBlockX = (int) Math.floor(box.minX());
        int maxBlockX = (int) Math.floor(box.maxX());
        int minBlockY = (int) Math.floor(box.minY());
        int maxBlockY = (int) Math.floor(box.maxY());
        int minBlockZ = (int) Math.floor(box.minZ());
        int maxBlockZ = (int) Math.floor(box.maxZ());

        int minChunkX = minBlockX >> 4;
        int maxChunkX = maxBlockX >> 4;
        int minChunkZ = minBlockZ >> 4;
        int maxChunkZ = maxBlockZ >> 4;
        for (int cx = minChunkX; cx <= maxChunkX; cx++) {
            for (int cz = minChunkZ; cz <= maxChunkZ; cz++) {
                if (!world.isChunkLoaded(cx, cz)) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        for (int x = minBlockX; x <= maxBlockX; x++) {
            for (int y = minBlockY; y <= maxBlockY; y++) {
                for (int z = minBlockZ; z <= maxBlockZ; z++) {
                    Block block = world.getBlockAt(x, y, z);
                    if (block.isEmpty() || block.isLiquid()) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (block.isPassable()) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox blockBox = block.getBoundingBox();
                    CompatBoundingBox compatBlockBox = new CompatBoundingBox(
                            blockBox.getMinX(), blockBox.getMinY(), blockBox.getMinZ(),
                            blockBox.getMaxX(), blockBox.getMaxY(), blockBox.getMaxZ()
                    );
                    if (box.intersectsWithMargin(compatBlockBox, 1.0e-4)) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isIce(Block block) {
        org.bukkit.Material m = block.getType();
        return m == org.bukkit.Material.ICE || m == org.bukkit.Material.PACKED_ICE
                || m == org.bukkit.Material.BLUE_ICE || m == org.bukkit.Material.FROSTED_ICE;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSlime(Block block) {
        return block.getType() == org.bukkit.Material.SLIME_BLOCK;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isHoney(Block block) {
        return block.getType() == org.bukkit.Material.HONEY_BLOCK;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSoulSand(Block block) {
        org.bukkit.Material m = block.getType();
        return m == org.bukkit.Material.SOUL_SAND || m == org.bukkit.Material.SOUL_SOIL;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isClimbable(Block block) {
        org.bukkit.Material m = block.getType();
        return m == org.bukkit.Material.LADDER || m == org.bukkit.Material.VINE || m == org.bukkit.Material.SCAFFOLDING
                || m == org.bukkit.Material.COBWEB || m == org.bukkit.Material.POWDER_SNOW
                || m == org.bukkit.Material.WEEPING_VINES || m == org.bukkit.Material.TWISTING_VINES;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isLiquid(Block block) {
        return block.isLiquid();
    }

    @Override
    public int getMinHeight(World world) {
        return world.getMinHeight();
    }

    @Override
    public int getMaxHeight(World world) {
        return world.getMaxHeight();
    }

    @Override
    public double[] getTPS() {
        return Bukkit.getTPS();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean hasPotionEffect(LivingEntity entity, CompatPotionType potionType) {
        PotionEffectType pet = resolvePotionType(potionType);
        return pet != null && entity.hasPotionEffect(pet);
    }

    @Override
    public int getPotionAmplifier(LivingEntity entity, CompatPotionType potionType) {
        PotionEffectType pet = resolvePotionType(potionType);
        if (pet == null) return -1;
        PotionEffect effect = entity.getPotionEffect(pet);
        return effect != null ? effect.getAmplifier() : -1;
    }

    @Override
    public double getAttributeValue(LivingEntity entity, CompatAttribute attribute, double defaultValue) {
        Attribute bukkitAttr = resolveAttribute(attribute);
        if (bukkitAttr == null) return defaultValue;
        AttributeInstance inst = entity.getAttribute(bukkitAttr);
        return inst != null ? inst.getValue() : defaultValue;
    }

    @Override
    public void sendMessage(CommandSender sender, String colorFormattedText) {
        sender.sendMessage(ColorUtil.toComponent(colorFormattedText));
    }

    @Override
    public void sendActionBar(Player player, String colorFormattedText) {
        player.sendActionBar(ColorUtil.toComponent(colorFormattedText));
    }

    @Override
    public void playSound(Player player, Location loc, String soundName, float volume, float pitch) {
        try {
            Sound sound = Sound.valueOf(soundName.toUpperCase());
            player.playSound(loc, sound, volume, pitch);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {}
    }

    private PotionEffectType resolvePotionType(CompatPotionType type) {
        return POTION_TYPE_CACHE.get(type);
    }

    private Attribute resolveAttribute(CompatAttribute attr) {
        return switch (attr) {
            case GENERIC_MOVEMENT_SPEED -> Attribute.GENERIC_MOVEMENT_SPEED;
            case GENERIC_ATTACK_SPEED -> Attribute.GENERIC_ATTACK_SPEED;
            case GENERIC_MAX_HEALTH -> Attribute.GENERIC_MAX_HEALTH;
        };
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/compat/impl/TickMonitor.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.compat.impl;

import org.bukkit.plugin.Plugin;
import org.bukkit.scheduler.BukkitRunnable;

/**
 * 轻量级主线程 Tick 采样器：在缺乏 Bukkit.getTPS() 的纯 Spigot 服务端上作为零开销兜底
 */
public final class TickMonitor {

    private static final int HISTORY_TICKS = 600; // 记录最近 30 秒 (600 ticks)
    private static final long[] TICK_DURATIONS = new long[HISTORY_TICKS];
    private static int tickIndex = 0;
    private static long lastTickTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
    private static boolean running = false;

    private TickMonitor() {}

    public static synchronized void start(Plugin plugin) {
        if (running) return;
        running = true;
        lastTickTime = System.currentTimeMillis();

        new BukkitRunnable() {
            @Override
            public void run() {
                long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
                long elapsed = now - lastTickTime;
                lastTickTime = now;

                TICK_DURATIONS[tickIndex % HISTORY_TICKS] = elapsed;
                tickIndex++;
            }
        }.runTaskTimer(plugin, 1L, 1L);
    }

    public static double getEstimatedTps() {
        int count = Math.min(tickIndex, HISTORY_TICKS);
        if (count == 0) return 20.0;

        long totalElapsed = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            totalElapsed += TICK_DURATIONS[i];
        }

        if (totalElapsed <= 0) return 20.0;
        double avgTickMs = (double) totalElapsed / count;
        double tps = 1000.0 / Math.max(50.0, avgTickMs);
        return Math.min(20.0, Math.max(0.0, tps));
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/data/ElytraData.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.data;

import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;

/**
 * 玩家鞘翅滑翔运行时状态数据模型 (ElytraData)
 * 专门追踪滑翔生命周期、物理运动学参数、烟花/激流/外部推力时效窗口与反作弊状态机。
 */
public class ElytraData {

    // 滑翔状态与生命周期
    private boolean gliding = false;
    private long glideStartTime = 0L;
    private int glideTicks = 0;
    private boolean elytraEquipped = false;
    private boolean elytraBroken = false;
    private long lastChestplateCheckTime = 0L;

    // 上一 Tick 运动学状态
    private Location lastLocation = null;
    private Vector lastVelocity = new Vector(0, 0, 0);
    private double lastHorizontalSpeed = 0.0;
    private double lastDeltaY = 0.0;
    private float lastPitch = 0.0f;

    // 烟花推进时效追踪
    private long lastFireworkTime = 0L;
    private Location lastFireworkLocation = null;
    private int consecutiveHighSpeedTicks = 0;

    // 激流三叉戟与外部速度源时效
    private long lastRiptideTime = 0L;
    private long lastExternalVelocityTime = 0L;

    // 滑翔状态切换追踪 (GlideFlip & GlideState)
    private long lastToggleTime = 0L;
    private Location lastToggleLocation = null;
    private final Deque<Long> toggleTimestamps = new ArrayDeque<>(8);

    // 物理子项连续异常计数器
    private int ascendTicks = 0;
    private int hoverTicks = 0;

    // 滑翔移动 Tick 速率追踪 (Timer)
    private long lastMoveTimestamp = 0L;
    private final Deque<Long> moveIntervals = new ArrayDeque<>(20);

    // 免摔滥用追踪 (ElytraLowY / ElytraNoFall)
    private float fallDistanceBeforeGlide = 0.0f;
    private long glideStartTimeForNoFall = 0L;
    private int consecutiveLowYToggles = 0;
    private long lastLowYToggleTime = 0L;

    public ElytraData() {
    }

    /**
     * 重置所有滑翔临时状态（例如在回退成功、玩家死亡、换界、传送或正常落地时调用）
     */
    public void resetGlideState() {
        this.gliding = false;
        this.glideTicks = 0;
        this.ascendTicks = 0;
        this.hoverTicks = 0;
        this.consecutiveHighSpeedTicks = 0;
        this.lastHorizontalSpeed = 0.0;
        this.lastDeltaY = 0.0;
        this.moveIntervals.clear();
        this.lastMoveTimestamp = 0L;
        this.fallDistanceBeforeGlide = 0.0f;
    }

    /**
     * 完全重置所有数据（用于玩家加入、重生或数据清理）
     */
    public void resetAll() {
        resetGlideState();
        this.glideStartTime = 0L;
        this.elytraEquipped = false;
        this.elytraBroken = false;
        this.lastChestplateCheckTime = 0L;
        this.lastLocation = null;
        this.lastVelocity = new Vector(0, 0, 0);
        this.lastFireworkTime = 0L;
        this.lastFireworkLocation = null;
        this.lastRiptideTime = 0L;
        this.lastExternalVelocityTime = 0L;
        this.lastToggleTime = 0L;
        this.lastToggleLocation = null;
        this.toggleTimestamps.clear();
        this.consecutiveLowYToggles = 0;
        this.lastLowYToggleTime = 0L;
    }

    /**
     * 清空 Timer 滑动采样队列（在触发告警或状态重置后冲刷，避免连续雪崩式重复误判）
     */
    public void clearMoveIntervals() {
        this.moveIntervals.clear();
    }

    /**
     * 清空 Flip 切换时间戳队列
     */
    public void clearToggleTimestamps() {
        this.toggleTimestamps.clear();
    }

    /**
     * 记录一次临地开关滑翔操作，并返回近期窗口内的连续违规次数
     */
    public int recordLowYToggle(long windowMs) {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (now - lastLowYToggleTime < windowMs) {
            consecutiveLowYToggles++;
        } else {
            consecutiveLowYToggles = 1;
        }
        lastLowYToggleTime = now;
        return consecutiveLowYToggles;
    }

    public void resetLowYToggles() {
        this.consecutiveLowYToggles = 0;
    }

    /**
     * 记录一次滑翔状态切换事件时间戳
     */
    public void recordToggle(long timestamp, Location location) {
        this.lastToggleTime = timestamp;
        if (location != null) {
            this.lastToggleLocation = location.clone();
        }
        this.toggleTimestamps.addLast(timestamp);
        // 清理超过 2 秒的旧记录
        while (!this.toggleTimestamps.isEmpty() && timestamp - this.toggleTimestamps.peekFirst() > 2000L) {
            this.toggleTimestamps.pollFirst();
        }
    }

    /**
     * 获取指定时间窗口（毫秒）内的滑翔状态切换次数
     */
    public int getToggleCountInWindow(long windowMs) {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        int count = 0;
        for (Long ts : toggleTimestamps) {
            if (now - ts <= windowMs) {
                count++;
            }
        }
        return count;
    }

    /**
     * 记录一次移动间隔用于 Timer 滑动窗口
     */
    public void recordMoveInterval(long interval) {
        if (interval > 0 && interval < 1000) {
            moveIntervals.addLast(interval);
            while (moveIntervals.size() > 20) {
                moveIntervals.pollFirst();
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 计算滑动窗口内的平均 Move 间隔（毫秒）
     */
    public double getAverageMoveInterval() {
        if (moveIntervals.isEmpty()) {
            return 50.0;
        }
        long sum = 0;
        for (Long interval : moveIntervals) {
            sum += interval;
        }
        return (double) sum / moveIntervals.size();
    }

    public int getMoveIntervalSampleSize() {
        return moveIntervals.size();
    }

    // ==================== Getters & Setters ====================

    public boolean isGliding() {
        return gliding;
    }

    public void setGliding(boolean gliding) {
        this.gliding = gliding;
    }

    public long getGlideStartTime() {
        return glideStartTime;
    }

    public void setGlideStartTime(long glideStartTime) {
        this.glideStartTime = glideStartTime;
    }

    public int getGlideTicks() {
        return glideTicks;
    }

    public void setGlideTicks(int glideTicks) {
        this.glideTicks = glideTicks;
    }

    public void incrementGlideTicks() {
        this.glideTicks++;
    }

    public boolean isElytraEquipped() {
        return elytraEquipped;
    }

    public void setElytraEquipped(boolean elytraEquipped) {
        this.elytraEquipped = elytraEquipped;
    }

    public boolean isElytraBroken() {
        return elytraBroken;
    }

    public void setElytraBroken(boolean elytraBroken) {
        this.elytraBroken = elytraBroken;
    }

    public long getLastChestplateCheckTime() {
        return lastChestplateCheckTime;
    }

    public void setLastChestplateCheckTime(long lastChestplateCheckTime) {
        this.lastChestplateCheckTime = lastChestplateCheckTime;
    }

    public Location getLastLocation() {
        return lastLocation;
    }

    public void setLastLocation(Location lastLocation) {
        this.lastLocation = lastLocation;
    }

    public Vector getLastVelocity() {
        return lastVelocity;
    }

    public void setLastVelocity(Vector lastVelocity) {
        this.lastVelocity = lastVelocity;
    }

    public double getLastHorizontalSpeed() {
        return lastHorizontalSpeed;
    }

    public void setLastHorizontalSpeed(double lastHorizontalSpeed) {
        this.lastHorizontalSpeed = lastHorizontalSpeed;
    }

    public double getLastDeltaY() {
        return lastDeltaY;
    }

    public void setLastDeltaY(double lastDeltaY) {
        this.lastDeltaY = lastDeltaY;
    }

    public float getLastPitch() {
        return lastPitch;
    }

    public void setLastPitch(float lastPitch) {
        this.lastPitch = lastPitch;
    }

    public long getLastFireworkTime() {
        return lastFireworkTime;
    }

    public void setLastFireworkTime(long lastFireworkTime) {
        this.lastFireworkTime = lastFireworkTime;
    }

    public Location getLastFireworkLocation() {
        return lastFireworkLocation;
    }

    public void setLastFireworkLocation(Location lastFireworkLocation) {
        this.lastFireworkLocation = lastFireworkLocation;
    }

    public int getConsecutiveHighSpeedTicks() {
        return consecutiveHighSpeedTicks;
    }

    public void setConsecutiveHighSpeedTicks(int consecutiveHighSpeedTicks) {
        this.consecutiveHighSpeedTicks = consecutiveHighSpeedTicks;
    }

    public void incrementConsecutiveHighSpeedTicks() {
        this.consecutiveHighSpeedTicks++;
    }

    public long getLastRiptideTime() {
        return lastRiptideTime;
    }

    public void setLastRiptideTime(long lastRiptideTime) {
        this.lastRiptideTime = lastRiptideTime;
    }

    public long getLastExternalVelocityTime() {
        return lastExternalVelocityTime;
    }

    public void setLastExternalVelocityTime(long lastExternalVelocityTime) {
        this.lastExternalVelocityTime = lastExternalVelocityTime;
    }

    public long getLastToggleTime() {
        return lastToggleTime;
    }

    public void setLastToggleTime(long lastToggleTime) {
        this.lastToggleTime = lastToggleTime;
    }

    public Location getLastToggleLocation() {
        return lastToggleLocation;
    }

    public void setLastToggleLocation(Location lastToggleLocation) {
        this.lastToggleLocation = lastToggleLocation;
    }

    public int getAscendTicks() {
        return ascendTicks;
    }

    public void setAscendTicks(int ascendTicks) {
        this.ascendTicks = ascendTicks;
    }

    public void incrementAscendTicks() {
        this.ascendTicks++;
    }

    public int getHoverTicks() {
        return hoverTicks;
    }

    public void setHoverTicks(int hoverTicks) {
        this.hoverTicks = hoverTicks;
    }

    public void incrementHoverTicks() {
        this.hoverTicks++;
    }

    public long getLastMoveTimestamp() {
        return lastMoveTimestamp;
    }

    public void setLastMoveTimestamp(long lastMoveTimestamp) {
        this.lastMoveTimestamp = lastMoveTimestamp;
    }

    public float getFallDistanceBeforeGlide() {
        return fallDistanceBeforeGlide;
    }

    public void setFallDistanceBeforeGlide(float fallDistanceBeforeGlide) {
        this.fallDistanceBeforeGlide = fallDistanceBeforeGlide;
    }

    public long getGlideStartTimeForNoFall() {
        return glideStartTimeForNoFall;
    }

    public void setGlideStartTimeForNoFall(long glideStartTimeForNoFall) {
        this.glideStartTimeForNoFall = glideStartTimeForNoFall;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/data/PlayerData.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.data;

import com.example.argusac.model.LegitimateLocation;
import com.example.argusac.model.SetbackFailureLog;
import com.example.argusac.model.SetbackOverride;
import com.example.argusac.model.VelocitySourceType;
import com.example.argusac.model.ViolationLog;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedDeque;

/**
 * 玩家反作弊运行时核心数据模型 (PlayerData)
 * 保存单一回退点 (lastLegitimateLocation)、前一位置兜底 (previousLegitimateLocation)、
 * 合法速度来源时效表、自免疫截止期与检测上下文
 */
public class PlayerData {

    private final UUID uuid;
    private final String name;

    // 各检测模块的 VL 等级映射
    private final Map<String, Integer> violationLevels = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    // 历史违规日志队列 (限制容量)
    private final Deque<ViolationLog> violationLogs = new ConcurrentLinkedDeque<>();

    // 回退失败审计日志队列
    private final Deque<SetbackFailureLog> failureLogs = new ConcurrentLinkedDeque<>();

    // 合法速度与机动来源时效表 (Key: 来源类型, Value: 有效期截止毫秒时间戳)
    private final Map<VelocitySourceType, Long> legalVelocitySources = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    private volatile int lastPunishedVl = 0;

    // 移动上下文
    private Location fromLocation;
    private Location toLocation;
    private double lastHorizontalDistance = 0.0;
    private int airTicks = 0;
    private int groundTicks = 0;
    private boolean onGround = true;
    private boolean lastOnGround = true;

    // 击退响应追踪 (Velocity Check)
    private Vector expectedVelocity = null;
    private long lastVelocityReceivedTime = 0L;
    private boolean velocityHandled = true;

    // 基础缓冲时间戳
    private long lastTeleportTime = 0L;
    private long lastFlightToggleTime = 0L;
    private long lastJoinTime = 0L;

    // 加入游戏后的豁免期时长（毫秒）
    private static final long GRACE_PERIOD_MS = 5000L;
    private long lastDamageTime = 0L;

    // 背包状态追踪 (InventoryMove)
    private boolean inventoryOpen = false;
    private Location inventoryOpenLocation = null;
    private long inventoryOpenTime = 0L;

    // 战斗相关数据 (AutoClicker, KillAura, Reach)
    private final Deque<Long> clickTimestamps = new ConcurrentLinkedDeque<>();
    private long lastClickTimestamp = 0L;
    private UUID lastAttackedTarget = null;
    private long lastAttackTime = 0L;

    // 方块破坏与放置追踪 (FastBreak, Nuker, Scaffold)
    private long lastBreakTick = -1L;
    private long lastBreakTimestamp = 0L;
    private int breaksThisTick = 0;
    private final Deque<Long> breakTimestamps = new ConcurrentLinkedDeque<>();
    private long lastPlaceTime = 0L;

    // 鞘翅滑翔数据追踪 (ElytraFly)
    private final ElytraData elytraData = new ElytraData();

    public PlayerData(Player player) {
        this.uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        this.name = player.getName();
        this.lastJoinTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    public UUID getUuid() {
        return uuid;
    }

    public String getName() {
        return name;
    }

    // ==================== VL 相关方法 ====================

    public int getVl(String checkName) {
        return violationLevels.getOrDefault(checkName, 0);
    }

    public int addVl(String checkName, int amount) {
        return violationLevels.compute(checkName, (k, v) -> (v == null ? 0 : v) + amount);
    }

    public void setVl(String checkName, int vl) {
        if (vl <= 0) {
            violationLevels.remove(checkName);
        } else {
            violationLevels.put(checkName, vl);
        }
    }

    public void decayVl(int amount) {
        for (Map.Entry<String, Integer> entry : violationLevels.entrySet()) {
            int newVl = Math.max(0, entry.getValue() - amount);
            if (newVl == 0) {
                violationLevels.remove(entry.getKey());
            } else {
                violationLevels.put(entry.getKey(), newVl);
            }
        }
        if (getTotalVl() < lastPunishedVl) {
            lastPunishedVl = getTotalVl();
        }
    }

    public int getLastPunishedVl() {
        return lastPunishedVl;
    }

    public void setLastPunishedVl(int lastPunishedVl) {
        this.lastPunishedVl = lastPunishedVl;
    }

    public int getTotalVl() {
        int total = 0;
        for (int v : violationLevels.values()) {
            total += v;
        }
        return total;
    }

    public Map<String, Integer> getAllVls() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableMap(violationLevels);
    }

    // ==================== 回退代理方法 (全部路由至 SetbackManager) ====================

    public LegitimateLocation getLastLegitimateLocation() {
        return com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin.getInstance().getSetbackManager().getLastLocation(uuid);
    }

    public LegitimateLocation getPreviousLegitimateLocation() {
        return com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin.getInstance().getSetbackManager().getPreviousLocation(uuid);
    }

    public SetbackOverride getSetbackOverride() {
        return com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin.getInstance().getSetbackManager().getOverride(uuid);
    }

    public void setSetbackOverride(SetbackOverride setbackOverride) {
        com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin.getInstance().getSetbackManager().setOverride(uuid, setbackOverride);
    }

    public boolean isSetbackImmune() {
        return com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin.getInstance().getSetbackManager().isImmune(uuid);
    }

    public long getSetbackImmunityUntil() {
        com.example.argusac.manager.SetbackManager.PlayerSetbackContext ctx =
                com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin.getInstance().getSetbackManager().getContext(uuid);
        return ctx != null ? ctx.getImmunityUntil() : 0L;
    }

    /**
     * 记录合法高机动/速度来源
     */
    public void grantLegalVelocity(VelocitySourceType type, long durationMs) {
        legalVelocitySources.put(type, System.currentTimeMillis() + durationMs);
    }

    /**
     * 判断指定合法速度来源当前是否有效
     */
    public boolean hasLegalVelocity(VelocitySourceType type) {
        Long expireTime = legalVelocitySources.get(type);
        return expireTime != null && expireTime > System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    /**
     * 判断玩家当前是否存在任何生效中的合法高机动来源
     */
    public boolean hasAnyLegalVelocity() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        for (Long expireTime : legalVelocitySources.values()) {
            if (expireTime > now) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    // ==================== 日志队列 ====================

    public void addLog(ViolationLog log, int maxLogs) {
        violationLogs.addLast(log);
        while (violationLogs.size() > maxLogs) {
            violationLogs.pollFirst();
        }
    }

    public List<ViolationLog> getLogs() {
        return new ArrayList<>(violationLogs);
    }

    public void addFailureLog(SetbackFailureLog log, int maxLogs) {
        failureLogs.addLast(log);
        while (failureLogs.size() > maxLogs) {
            failureLogs.pollFirst();
        }
    }

    public List<SetbackFailureLog> getFailureLogs() {
        return new ArrayList<>(failureLogs);
    }

    // ==================== 状态追踪 Getter & Setter ====================

    public Location getFromLocation() {
        return fromLocation;
    }

    public void setFromLocation(Location fromLocation) {
        this.fromLocation = fromLocation;
    }

    public Location getToLocation() {
        return toLocation;
    }

    public void setToLocation(Location toLocation) {
        this.toLocation = toLocation;
    }

    public double getLastHorizontalDistance() {
        return lastHorizontalDistance;
    }

    public void setLastHorizontalDistance(double lastHorizontalDistance) {
        this.lastHorizontalDistance = lastHorizontalDistance;
    }

    public int getAirTicks() {
        return airTicks;
    }

    public void setAirTicks(int airTicks) {
        this.airTicks = airTicks;
    }

    public void incrementAirTicks() {
        this.airTicks++;
    }

    public int getGroundTicks() {
        return groundTicks;
    }

    public void setGroundTicks(int groundTicks) {
        this.groundTicks = groundTicks;
    }

    public void incrementGroundTicks() {
        this.groundTicks++;
    }

    public boolean isOnGround() {
        return onGround;
    }

    public void setOnGround(boolean onGround) {
        this.onGround = onGround;
    }

    public boolean isLastOnGround() {
        return lastOnGround;
    }

    public void setLastOnGround(boolean lastOnGround) {
        this.lastOnGround = lastOnGround;
    }

    public Vector getExpectedVelocity() {
        return expectedVelocity;
    }

    public void setExpectedVelocity(Vector expectedVelocity) {
        this.expectedVelocity = expectedVelocity;
        this.lastVelocityReceivedTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        this.velocityHandled = false;
    }

    public long getLastVelocityReceivedTime() {
        return lastVelocityReceivedTime;
    }

    public boolean isVelocityHandled() {
        return velocityHandled;
    }

    public void setVelocityHandled(boolean velocityHandled) {
        this.velocityHandled = velocityHandled;
    }

    public long getLastTeleportTime() {
        return lastTeleportTime;
    }

    public void setLastTeleportTime(long lastTeleportTime) {
        this.lastTeleportTime = lastTeleportTime;
    }

    public long getLastFlightToggleTime() {
        return lastFlightToggleTime;
    }

    public void setLastFlightToggleTime(long lastFlightToggleTime) {
        this.lastFlightToggleTime = lastFlightToggleTime;
    }

    public long getLastJoinTime() {
        return lastJoinTime;
    }

    /**
     * 判断玩家是否处于加入后的初始豁免期（GRACE_PERIOD_MS 毫秒内）
     * 豁免期内跳过全部检测，防止因传送/加载延迟产生误报
     */
    public boolean isInGracePeriod() {
        return System.currentTimeMillis() - lastJoinTime < GRACE_PERIOD_MS;
    }

    public boolean isInventoryOpen() {
        return inventoryOpen;
    }

    public void setInventoryOpen(boolean inventoryOpen, Location loc) {
        this.inventoryOpen = inventoryOpen;
        this.inventoryOpenLocation = loc;
        if (inventoryOpen) {
            this.inventoryOpenTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        }
    }

    public long getInventoryOpenTime() {
        return inventoryOpenTime;
    }

    public Location getInventoryOpenLocation() {
        return inventoryOpenLocation;
    }

    public Deque<Long> getClickTimestamps() {
        return clickTimestamps;
    }

    public long getLastClickTimestamp() {
        return lastClickTimestamp;
    }

    public void setLastClickTimestamp(long lastClickTimestamp) {
        this.lastClickTimestamp = lastClickTimestamp;
    }

    public UUID getLastAttackedTarget() {
        return lastAttackedTarget;
    }

    public void setLastAttackedTarget(UUID lastAttackedTarget) {
        this.lastAttackedTarget = lastAttackedTarget;
    }

    public long getLastAttackTime() {
        return lastAttackTime;
    }

    public void setLastAttackTime(long lastAttackTime) {
        this.lastAttackTime = lastAttackTime;
    }

    public long getLastBreakTick() {
        return lastBreakTick;
    }

    public void setLastBreakTick(long lastBreakTick) {
        this.lastBreakTick = lastBreakTick;
    }

    public long getLastBreakTimestamp() {
        return lastBreakTimestamp;
    }

    public void setLastBreakTimestamp(long lastBreakTimestamp) {
        this.lastBreakTimestamp = lastBreakTimestamp;
    }

    public long getLastDamageTime() {
        return lastDamageTime;
    }

    public void setLastDamageTime(long lastDamageTime) {
        this.lastDamageTime = lastDamageTime;
    }

    public boolean hasRecentVelocity(long thresholdMs) {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        return (now - lastVelocityReceivedTime < thresholdMs) || (now - lastDamageTime < thresholdMs);
    }

    public int getBreaksThisTick() {
        return breaksThisTick;
    }

    public void setBreaksThisTick(int breaksThisTick) {
        this.breaksThisTick = breaksThisTick;
    }

    public Deque<Long> getBreakTimestamps() {
        return breakTimestamps;
    }

    public long getLastPlaceTime() {
        return lastPlaceTime;
    }

    public void setLastPlaceTime(long lastPlaceTime) {
        this.lastPlaceTime = lastPlaceTime;
    }

    public ElytraData getElytraData() {
        return elytraData;
    }

    public boolean isElytraGliding() {
        return elytraData != null && elytraData.isGliding();
    }

    private volatile String clientBrand = "vanilla";

    public String getClientBrand() {
        return clientBrand;
    }

    public void setClientBrand(String clientBrand) {
        this.clientBrand = clientBrand;
    }

    /**
     * 彻底清除该玩家的所有运行时违规、日志与高机动时效状态 (用于 GDPR/合规性安全注销)
     */
    public void clearAllData() {
        violationLevels.clear();
        violationLogs.clear();
        failureLogs.clear();
        legalVelocitySources.clear();
        lastPunishedVl = 0;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/data/PlayerManager.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.data;

import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * 玩家数据管理器 (PlayerManager)
 * 负责玩家上线注册、下线回收以及线程安全的数据检索
 */
public class PlayerManager {

    private final Map<UUID, PlayerData> dataMap = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public PlayerData getPlayerData(Player player) {
        if (player == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return dataMap.computeIfAbsent(player.getUniqueId(), k -> new PlayerData(player));
    }

    public PlayerData getPlayerData(UUID uuid) {
        return dataMap.get(uuid);
    }

    public void removePlayerData(UUID uuid) {
        dataMap.remove(uuid);
    }

    public Collection<PlayerData> getAllData() {
        return dataMap.values();
    }

    public void clear() {
        dataMap.clear();
    }
}
```
