`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/i18n/I18n.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.i18n;

import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * 多语言系统静态全局门面 (I18n Facade)
 * 为插件各功能模块（命令、事件、检测、惩罚、日志）提供极简、直观、空安全的调用入口。
 */
public final class I18n {

    private static LanguageManager manager;

    private I18n() {}

    /**
     * 在插件主类中初始化绑定管理器实例
     */
    public static void init(LanguageManager languageManager) {
        manager = Objects.requireNonNull(languageManager, "LanguageManager cannot be null");
    }

    /**
     * 获取绑定的底层语言管理器实例
     */
    public static LanguageManager getManager() {
        return manager;
    }

    /**
     * 向目标发送者（玩家或控制台）发送本地化消息（不带全局前缀）
     *
     * @param sender 消息接收方 (为 null 时静默返回)
     * @param key    多语言键名 (为 null 时静默返回)
     * @param args   成对占位符 ("key", value) 或数字索引参数
     */
    public static void send(CommandSender sender, String key, Object... args) {
        if (manager != null) {
            manager.sendMessage(sender, key, args);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 向目标发送者发送带有插件统一前缀的本地化消息
     *
     * @param sender 消息接收方
     * @param key    多语言键名
     * @param args   占位符参数
     */
    public static void sendPrefixed(CommandSender sender, String key, Object... args) {
        if (manager != null) {
            manager.sendPrefixedMessage(sender, key, args);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 获取已完成占位符替换和颜色解析的 Component
     *
     * @param sender 消息接收方（用于决定语言代码）
     * @param key    多语言键名
     * @param args   占位符参数
     * @return Adventure Component
     */
    public static Component component(CommandSender sender, String key, Object... args) {
        if (manager == null) {
            return Component.text(key != null ? key : "");
        }
        return manager.getComponent(sender, key, args);
    }

    /**
     * 获取带有插件前缀的 Component
     */
    public static Component prefixedComponent(CommandSender sender, String key, Object... args) {
        if (manager == null) {
            return Component.text(key != null ? key : "");
        }
        return manager.getPrefixedComponent(sender, key, args);
    }

    /**
     * 获取多行本地化 Component 列表 (适用于 Help 菜单、GUI Lore 等)
     */
    public static List<Component> componentList(CommandSender sender, String key, Object... args) {
        if (manager == null) {
            return List.of(Component.text(key != null ? key : ""));
        }
        return manager.getComponentList(sender, key, args);
    }

    /**
     * 获取原始未解析的占位符替换后字符串
     */
    public static String raw(CommandSender sender, String key, Object... args) {
        if (manager == null) {
            return key != null ? key : "";
        }
        String lang = manager.resolveLanguage(sender);
        String raw = manager.getRawMessage(lang, key);
        return manager.format(raw, args);
    }

    /**
     * 全局广播消息：每位在线玩家根据其客户端语言独立本地化接收
     */
    public static void broadcast(String key, Object... args) {
        if (manager != null) {
            manager.broadcast(key, args);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 向所有拥有管理员权限 (argusac.admin) 的在线玩家广播消息
     */
    public static void broadcastStaff(String key, Object... args) {
        if (manager != null) {
            manager.broadcastPermission("argusac.admin", key, args);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 向玩家发送本地化 Action Bar
     */
    public static void sendActionBar(Player player, String key, Object... args) {
        if (manager != null) {
            manager.sendActionBar(player, key, args);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 向玩家发送本地化 Title 与 Subtitle
     *
     * @param player      目标玩家
     * @param titleKey    主标题多语言键 (传 null 则不显示主标题)
     * @param subtitleKey 副标题多语言键 (传 null 则不显示副标题)
     * @param fadeIn      淡入时间 (刻, tick)
     * @param stay        停留时间 (刻, tick)
     * @param fadeOut     淡出时间 (刻, tick)
     * @param args        占位符参数
     */
    public static void sendTitle(Player player, String titleKey, String subtitleKey, int fadeIn, int stay, int fadeOut, Object... args) {
        if (manager != null) {
            manager.sendTitle(player, titleKey, subtitleKey, fadeIn, stay, fadeOut, args);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/i18n/LanguageManager.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.i18n;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.minimessage.MiniMessage;
import net.kyori.adventure.title.Title;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.YamlConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.net.JarURLConnection;
import java.net.URL;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.jar.JarEntry;
import java.util.jar.JarFile;
import java.util.logging.Level;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * 现代多语言国际化管理器 (Language & i18n Manager)
 * 专为 Paper / Spigot 1.20+ 架构打造：
 * 1. 支持自动释放内置语言文件，增量补全且不覆盖服主修改
 * 2. 具备四级兜底（客户端语言 -> 客户端回退语言 -> 硬编码底线语言 -> 原始 Key）
 * 3. 完美兼容 MiniMessage 现代标签与传统颜色代码 (包含 Legacy Hex &#RRGGBB)
 * 4. 线程安全，严密的空指针保护与异常捕获
 */
public class LanguageManager {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;
    private final MiniMessage miniMessage;

    // 配置项缓存
    private String serverLanguage = "zh_cn";
    private String clientDefaultLanguage = "en_us";
    private boolean autoClientLanguage = true;

    // 硬编码最终兜底语言代码
    private static final String HARDCODED_FALLBACK_LANG = "en_us";

    // 预置保底内置语言文件列表
    private static final Set<String> DEFAULT_BUILTIN_LANGS = Set.of("zh_cn.yml", "en_us.yml");

    // 运行时语言配置缓存 (语言代码小写 -> 配置对象)
    private final Map<String, FileConfiguration> languageConfigs = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    // Jar 内置语言默认配置缓存 (用于 key 级热补全回退)
    private final Map<String, FileConfiguration> defaultConfigs = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    // 玩家 UUID -> 解析后语言代码缓存
    private final Map<UUID, String> playerLocaleCache = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    // 传统 Hex 颜色匹配正则: &#RRGGBB 或 &#rrggbb
    private static final Pattern HEX_COLOR_PATTERN = Pattern.compile("&#([0-9a-fA-F]{6})");
    // 经典 Section 格式 Hex: &x&r&r&g&g&b&b
    private static final Pattern SECTION_HEX_PATTERN = Pattern.compile("&x(&[0-9a-fA-F]){6}");

    // 经典颜色代码映射表
    private static final Map<Character, String> LEGACY_TO_MINIMESSAGE = Map.ofEntries(
            Map.entry('0', "<black>"),
            Map.entry('1', "<dark_blue>"),
            Map.entry('2', "<dark_green>"),
            Map.entry('3', "<dark_aqua>"),
            Map.entry('4', "<dark_red>"),
            Map.entry('5', "<dark_purple>"),
            Map.entry('6', "<gold>"),
            Map.entry('7', "<gray>"),
            Map.entry('8', "<dark_gray>"),
            Map.entry('9', "<blue>"),
            Map.entry('a', "<green>"),
            Map.entry('b', "<aqua>"),
            Map.entry('c', "<red>"),
            Map.entry('d', "<light_purple>"),
            Map.entry('e', "<yellow>"),
            Map.entry('f', "<white>"),
            Map.entry('k', "<obfuscated>"),
            Map.entry('l', "<bold>"),
            Map.entry('m', "<strikethrough>"),
            Map.entry('n', "<underlined>"),
            Map.entry('o', "<italic>"),
            Map.entry('r', "<reset>")
    );

    public LanguageManager(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = Objects.requireNonNull(plugin, "ArgusACPlugin cannot be null");
        this.miniMessage = MiniMessage.miniMessage();
        this.reload();
    }

    /**
     * 重载多语言系统：重新读取 config.yml 配置、提取新增内置语言文件、重载所有语言配置
     */
    public synchronized void reload() {
        this.languageConfigs.clear();
        this.defaultConfigs.clear();
        this.playerLocaleCache.clear();

        // 1. 读取主配置文件 config.yml 中的 i18n 设定
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfig();
        this.serverLanguage = normalizeLanguageCode(config.getString("language.server-language", "zh_cn"));
        this.clientDefaultLanguage = normalizeLanguageCode(config.getString("language.client-default-language", "en_us"));
        this.autoClientLanguage = config.getBoolean("language.auto-client-language", true);

        // 2. 检查并提取内置语言文件 (增量保存，不覆盖已有文件)
        extractDefaultLanguages();

        // 3. 加载 lang 目录下的所有语言文件
        loadAllLanguageFiles();

        plugin.getLogger().info(String.format(
                "[LanguageManager] 多语言模块初始化完成，已载入 %d 种语言配置 (服务端默认: %s, 客户端兜底: %s, 自动识别: %b)",
                languageConfigs.size(), serverLanguage, clientDefaultLanguage, autoClientLanguage
        ));
    }

    /**
     * 检查并释放内置语言文件到 plugins/ArgusAC/lang/ 目录
     * 支持增量释放：若文件已存在则跳过，不破坏服主自定义修改
     */
    private void extractDefaultLanguages() {
        File langFolder = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "lang");
        if (!langFolder.exists() && !langFolder.mkdirs()) {
            plugin.getLogger().warning("[LanguageManager] 无法创建语言文件夹: " + langFolder.getAbsolutePath());
        }

        // 动态发现或使用预设列表
        Set<String> builtinFiles = discoverBuiltinLanguageFiles();
        for (String fileName : builtinFiles) {
            File targetFile = new File(langFolder, fileName);
            if (!targetFile.exists()) {
                try {
                    plugin.saveResource("lang/" + fileName, false);
                    plugin.getLogger().info("[LanguageManager] 已自动释放内置语言文件: " + fileName);
                } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
                    plugin.getLogger().warning("[LanguageManager] 无法提取内置资源: lang/" + fileName + " (" + e.getMessage() + ")");
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 扫描 Jar 包内部的 lang/ 目录，自动发现所有内置语言文件
     */
    private Set<String> discoverBuiltinLanguageFiles() {
        Set<String> files = new HashSet<>(DEFAULT_BUILTIN_LANGS);
        try {
            URL resourceUrl = plugin.getClass().getClassLoader().getResource("lang");
            if (resourceUrl != null) {
                if ("jar".equals(resourceUrl.getProtocol())) {
                    JarURLConnection connection = (JarURLConnection) resourceUrl.openConnection();
                    try (JarFile jar = connection.getJarFile()) {
                        Enumeration<JarEntry> entries = jar.entries();
                        while (entries.hasMoreElements()) {
                            String name = entries.nextElement().getName();
                            if (name.startsWith("lang/") && name.endsWith(".yml") && name.length() > 9) {
                                files.add(name.substring("lang/".length()));
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            // 环境或安全策略限制扫描时，安全使用 DEFAULT_BUILTIN_LANGS 兜底
        }
        return files;
    }

    /**
     * 加载数据目录下 lang/ 中的所有语言文件，并以内置资源设置 defaults
     */
    private void loadAllLanguageFiles() {
        File langFolder = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "lang");
        File[] files = langFolder.listFiles((dir, name) -> name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).endsWith(".yml"));

        if (files != null) {
            for (File file : files) {
                String fileName = file.getName();
                String langCode = normalizeLanguageCode(fileName.substring(0, fileName.length() - 4));
                try {
                    YamlConfiguration diskConfig = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(file);

                    // 尝试加载 Jar 包内同名的内置资源，作为 key 缺失时的兜底 defaults
                    YamlConfiguration defaults = loadResourceDefaults("lang/" + fileName);
                    if (defaults != null) {
                        diskConfig.setDefaults(defaults);
                        defaultConfigs.put(langCode, defaults);
                    }

                    languageConfigs.put(langCode, diskConfig);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    plugin.getLogger().log(Level.SEVERE, "[LanguageManager] 加载语言文件失败: " + fileName, e);
                }
            }
        }

        // 极端兜底：如果磁盘上没有任何语言文件，直接将内置资源载入内存，确保插件能正常渲染消息
        if (languageConfigs.isEmpty()) {
            plugin.getLogger().warning("[LanguageManager] 磁盘未检测到语言文件，紧急启动内存内置语言包！");
            for (String fileName : DEFAULT_BUILTIN_LANGS) {
                String langCode = normalizeLanguageCode(fileName.substring(0, fileName.length() - 4));
                YamlConfiguration defaults = loadResourceDefaults("lang/" + fileName);
                if (defaults != null) {
                    languageConfigs.put(langCode, defaults);
                    defaultConfigs.put(langCode, defaults);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 读取 Jar 内置资源文件为 YamlConfiguration
     */
    private YamlConfiguration loadResourceDefaults(String resourcePath) {
        try (InputStream in = plugin.getResource(resourcePath)) {
            if (in == null) return null;
            try (InputStreamReader reader = new InputStreamReader(in, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
                return YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(reader);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            plugin.getLogger().log(Level.WARNING, "[LanguageManager] 读取内置默认资源异常: " + resourcePath, e);
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 获取发送者（控制台或玩家）对应的目标语言代码
     */
    public String resolveLanguage(CommandSender sender) {
        if (sender == null || !(sender instanceof Player player)) {
            return serverLanguage;
        }
        if (!autoClientLanguage) {
            return serverLanguage;
        }
        return getPlayerLocale(player);
    }

    /**
     * 查询玩家客户端设置的语言代码并标准化 (带本地缓存，提高高频调用吞吐量)
     * 支持 Paper 原生 Adventure API，跨版本向下兼容 Spigot
     */
    public String getPlayerLocale(Player player) {
        if (player == null) {
            return clientDefaultLanguage;
        }

        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        String cached = playerLocaleCache.get(uuid);
        if (cached != null) {
            return cached;
        }

        String rawLocale = null;

        // 1. 尝试优先调用 Paper / Adventure 的 player.locale() -> java.util.Locale
        try {
            Method localeMethod = player.getClass().getMethod("locale");
            Object locObj = localeMethod.invoke(player);
            if (locObj instanceof Locale loc) {
                rawLocale = loc.toString();
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            // Paper 1.16 以前或部分 Spigot 实现无此方法
        }

        // 2. 降级尝试调用 Spigot 传统接口 player.getLocale() -> String
        if (rawLocale == null) {
            try {
                Method getLocaleMethod = player.getClass().getMethod("getLocale");
                Object locObj = getLocaleMethod.invoke(player);
                if (locObj instanceof String s) {
                    rawLocale = s;
                }
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                // 忽略反射异常
            }
        }

        if (rawLocale == null || rawLocale.isBlank()) {
            return clientDefaultLanguage;
        }

        String normalized = normalizeLanguageCode(rawLocale);
        String resolved = null;

        // 精确匹配 (例如 zh_cn)
        if (languageConfigs.containsKey(normalized)) {
            resolved = normalized;
        } else if (normalized.contains("_")) {
            // 模糊前缀降级：如玩家客户端是 zh_tw 或 zh_hk，匹配首个 zh 开头的语言包
            String prefix = normalized.substring(0, normalized.indexOf('_'));
            for (String code : languageConfigs.keySet()) {
                if (code.startsWith(prefix)) {
                    resolved = code;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        if (resolved == null) {
            resolved = languageConfigs.containsKey(clientDefaultLanguage) ? clientDefaultLanguage : serverLanguage;
        }

        playerLocaleCache.put(uuid, resolved);
        return resolved;
    }

    /**
     * 清理指定玩家的语言缓存 (在玩家切换客户端语言或退出时调用)
     */
    public void invalidateCache(UUID uuid) {
        if (uuid != null) {
            playerLocaleCache.remove(uuid);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 规范化语言代码：去除空格、转为小写、横杠统一转为下划线 (例: "zh-CN" -> "zh_cn")
     */
    public static String normalizeLanguageCode(String raw) {
        if (raw == null) return "zh_cn";
        return raw.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).replace('-', '_');
    }

    /**
     * 四级 Fallback 获取未格式化原始文本：
     * 1. 目标语言磁盘配置 -> 2. 目标语言内置配置 -> 3. 客户端回退语言 -> 4. 硬编码底线语言 -> 5. 原始 key
     */
    public String getRawMessage(String langCode, String key) {
        if (key == null) return "";

        String targetLang = (langCode != null && languageConfigs.containsKey(langCode)) ? langCode : clientDefaultLanguage;

        // 1. 查询目标语言
        String val = lookupString(targetLang, key);
        if (val != null) return val;

        // 2. 查询客户端回退语言
        if (!targetLang.equalsIgnoreCase(clientDefaultLanguage)) {
            val = lookupString(clientDefaultLanguage, key);
            if (val != null) return val;
        }

        // 3. 查询服务端默认语言
        if (!targetLang.equalsIgnoreCase(serverLanguage) && !clientDefaultLanguage.equalsIgnoreCase(serverLanguage)) {
            val = lookupString(serverLanguage, key);
            if (val != null) return val;
        }

        // 4. 硬编码底线语言 (en_us)
        if (!HARDCODED_FALLBACK_LANG.equalsIgnoreCase(targetLang) && languageConfigs.containsKey(HARDCODED_FALLBACK_LANG)) {
            val = lookupString(HARDCODED_FALLBACK_LANG, key);
            if (val != null) return val;
        }

        // 5. 遍历已加载的所有语言包碰运气
        for (String lang : languageConfigs.keySet()) {
            val = lookupString(lang, key);
            if (val != null) return val;
        }

        // 6. 极端保底：直接返回 key，避免 NPE
        return key;
    }

    private String lookupString(String langCode, String key) {
        FileConfiguration config = languageConfigs.get(langCode);
        if (config != null) {
            String str = config.getString(key);
            if (str != null) return str;
        }
        FileConfiguration defaults = defaultConfigs.get(langCode);
        if (defaults != null) {
            return defaults.getString(key);
        }
        return null;
    }

    /**
     * 获取多行文本列表 (适用于帮助菜单、GUI Lore 等)
     */
    public List<String> getRawList(String langCode, String key) {
        if (key == null) return Collections.emptyList();

        String targetLang = (langCode != null && languageConfigs.containsKey(langCode)) ? langCode : clientDefaultLanguage;

        List<String> list = lookupList(targetLang, key);
        if (list != null && !list.isEmpty()) return list;

        if (!targetLang.equalsIgnoreCase(clientDefaultLanguage)) {
            list = lookupList(clientDefaultLanguage, key);
            if (list != null && !list.isEmpty()) return list;
        }

        return Collections.singletonList(key);
    }

    private List<String> lookupList(String langCode, String key) {
        FileConfiguration config = languageConfigs.get(langCode);
        if (config != null) {
            List<String> list = config.getStringList(key);
            if (!list.isEmpty()) return list;
        }
        FileConfiguration defaults = defaultConfigs.get(langCode);
        if (defaults != null) {
            List<String> list = defaults.getStringList(key);
            if (!list.isEmpty()) return list;
        }
        return null;
    }

    /**
     * 替换占位符：同时支持成对参数 (key, value) 以及索引参数 ({0}, {1})
     */
    public String format(String message, Object... args) {
        if (message == null || message.isEmpty() || args == null || args.length == 0) {
            return message != null ? message : "";
        }

        String result = message;

        // 如果参数个数为偶数，且首个参数是字符串，优先作为 key-value 替换
        if (args.length % 2 == 0 && (args[0] instanceof CharSequence)) {
            for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i += 2) {
                String k = String.valueOf(args[i]);
                String v = args[i + 1] != null ? String.valueOf(args[i + 1]) : "null";
                result = result.replace("{" + k + "}", v).replace("%" + k + "%", v);
            }
        }

        // 同时支持 {0}, {1}, {2} 格式的数字索引占位符
        for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
            String v = args[i] != null ? String.valueOf(args[i]) : "null";
            result = result.replace("{" + i + "}", v);
        }

        return result;
    }

    /**
     * 替换 Map 形式的占位符
     */
    public String format(String message, Map<String, ?> placeholders) {
        if (message == null || message.isEmpty() || placeholders == null || placeholders.isEmpty()) {
            return message != null ? message : "";
        }
        String result = message;
        for (Map.Entry<String, ?> entry : placeholders.entrySet()) {
            String k = entry.getKey();
            String v = entry.getValue() != null ? String.valueOf(entry.getValue()) : "null";
            result = result.replace("{" + k + "}", v).replace("%" + k + "%", v);
        }
        return result;
    }

    /**
     * 智能文本渲染引擎：
     * 1. 将传统颜色代码 (&a, &c) 与 Legacy Hex (&#RRGGBB) 转换为标准 MiniMessage 标签
     * 2. 使用 MiniMessage 反序列化为现代 Paper Component
     */
    public Component parseToComponent(String raw) {
        if (raw == null || raw.isEmpty()) {
            return Component.empty();
        }

        // 步骤 1: 处理 Hex 颜色代码 &#RRGGBB -> <#RRGGBB>
        String converted = raw;
        Matcher hexMatcher = HEX_COLOR_PATTERN.matcher(converted);
        if (hexMatcher.find()) {
            converted = hexMatcher.replaceAll("<#$1>");
        }

        // 步骤 2: 处理 Spigot Section Hex 格式 (&x&r&r&g&g&b&b)
        if (converted.contains("&x")) {
            Matcher sectionMatcher = SECTION_HEX_PATTERN.matcher(converted);
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            while (sectionMatcher.find()) {
                String hex = sectionMatcher.group().replace("&x", "").replace("&", "");
                sectionMatcher.appendReplacement(sb, "<#" + hex + ">");
            }
            sectionMatcher.appendTail(sb);
            converted = sb.toString();
        }

        // 步骤 3: 处理传统 & 颜色与样式代码 -> MiniMessage 标签
        if (converted.indexOf('&') != -1) {
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(converted.length() + 32);
            char[] chars = converted.toCharArray();
            for (int i = 0; i < chars.length; i++) {
                if (chars[i] == '&' && i + 1 < chars.length) {
                    char code = Character.toLowerCase(chars[i + 1]);
                    String tag = LEGACY_TO_MINIMESSAGE.get(code);
                    if (tag != null) {
                        sb.append(tag);
                        i++; // 跳过颜色字符
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                sb.append(chars[i]);
            }
            converted = sb.toString();
        }

        // 步骤 4: 统一使用 MiniMessage 一次性解析 (原生支持渐变、点击、悬浮等现代特性)
        try {
            return miniMessage.deserialize(converted);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            // 解析 MiniMessage 标签语法异常时的容错兜底
            return Component.text(raw);
        }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 面向外部调用的高层 API
    // =========================================================================

    /**
     * 获取已格式化并本地化的 Component 消息
     */
    public Component getComponent(CommandSender sender, String key, Object... args) {
        String lang = resolveLanguage(sender);
        String raw = getRawMessage(lang, key);
        String formatted = format(raw, args);
        return parseToComponent(formatted);
    }

    /**
     * 获取包含全局前缀的 Component 消息
     */
    public Component getPrefixedComponent(CommandSender sender, String key, Object... args) {
        String lang = resolveLanguage(sender);
        String prefix = getRawMessage(lang, "prefix");
        String raw = getRawMessage(lang, key);
        String formatted = format(prefix + raw, args);
        return parseToComponent(formatted);
    }

    /**
     * 获取已本地化的多行 Component 列表
     */
    public List<Component> getComponentList(CommandSender sender, String key, Object... args) {
        String lang = resolveLanguage(sender);
        List<String> rawList = getRawList(lang, key);
        List<Component> result = new ArrayList<>(rawList.size());
        for (String raw : rawList) {
            result.add(parseToComponent(format(raw, args)));
        }
        return result;
    }

    /**
     * 发送带本地化及占位符解析的消息
     */
    public void sendMessage(CommandSender sender, String key, Object... args) {
        if (sender == null || key == null) return;
        sender.sendMessage(getComponent(sender, key, args));
    }

    /**
     * 发送附带插件前缀的本地化消息
     */
    public void sendPrefixedMessage(CommandSender sender, String key, Object... args) {
        if (sender == null || key == null) return;
        sender.sendMessage(getPrefixedComponent(sender, key, args));
    }

    /**
     * 全局广播消息：每位在线玩家根据其各自客户端语言独立本地化接收
     */
    public void broadcast(String key, Object... args) {
        // 给控制台发送服务端语言
        plugin.getServer().getConsoleSender().sendMessage(getComponent(null, key, args));
        // 给各玩家按其各自语言发送
        for (Player player : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
            player.sendMessage(getComponent(player, key, args));
        }
    }

    /**
     * 权限广播：给拥有特定权限的在线玩家独立本地化发送
     */
    public void broadcastPermission(String permission, String key, Object... args) {
        // 控制台天然拥有权限
        plugin.getServer().getConsoleSender().sendMessage(getComponent(null, key, args));
        for (Player player : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
            if (player.hasPermission(permission)) {
                player.sendMessage(getComponent(player, key, args));
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 发送本地化 Action Bar
     */
    public void sendActionBar(Player player, String key, Object... args) {
        if (player == null || key == null) return;
        player.sendActionBar(getComponent(player, key, args));
    }

    /**
     * 发送本地化 Title 与 Subtitle
     */
    public void sendTitle(Player player, String titleKey, String subtitleKey, int fadeIn, int stay, int fadeOut, Object... args) {
        if (player == null) return;
        Component titleComp = titleKey != null ? getComponent(player, titleKey, args) : Component.empty();
        Component subComp = subtitleKey != null ? getComponent(player, subtitleKey, args) : Component.empty();

        Title.Times times = Title.Times.times(
                Duration.ofMillis(fadeIn * 50L),
                Duration.ofMillis(stay * 50L),
                Duration.ofMillis(fadeOut * 50L)
        );
        player.showTitle(Title.title(titleComp, subComp, times));
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // Getters & 状态查询
    // =========================================================================

    public String getServerLanguage() {
        return serverLanguage;
    }

    public String getClientDefaultLanguage() {
        return clientDefaultLanguage;
    }

    public boolean isAutoClientLanguage() {
        return autoClientLanguage;
    }

    public Set<String> getLoadedLanguages() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableSet(languageConfigs.keySet());
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/listener/ModernEventListener.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.listener;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import com.example.argusac.model.VelocitySourceType;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerRiptideEvent;

import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerLocaleChangeEvent;

/**
 * 现代版本 (1.13+) 专属事件监听器
 * 仅在当前运行环境 >= 1.13 时动态注册，杜绝 1.12.2 等低版本在类加载时因缺少现代专有事件类抛出 NoClassDefFoundError
 */
public class ModernEventListener implements Listener {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;

    public ModernEventListener(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlayerRiptide(PlayerRiptideEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            // 激流三叉戟赋予合法高机动豁免
            data.grantLegalVelocity(VelocitySourceType.RIPTIDE, 2000L);
            data.getElytraData().setLastRiptideTime(System.currentTimeMillis());
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onLocaleChange(PlayerLocaleChangeEvent event) {
        if (plugin.getLanguageManager() != null) {
            plugin.getLanguageManager().invalidateCache(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId());
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/listener/PlayerEventListener.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.listener;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.check.CheckManager;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;

import com.example.argusac.model.VelocitySourceType;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.block.Action;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityToggleGlideEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.PlayerDeathEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryCloseEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryOpenEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.*;
import org.bukkit.event.vehicle.VehicleEnterEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.vehicle.VehicleExitEvent;



/**
 * 玩家事件主监听器 (PlayerEventListener)
 * 统一调度移动、战斗、世界交互及生命周期事件。
 * <p>
 * 【回退机制重构原则】：
 * 1. 每次移动通过所有检测模块验证后，立即更新覆盖唯一的 lastLegitimateLocation。
 * 2. 死亡时清空，加入/重生/换界/外部传送时重置，进出载具时清空 previousLegitimateLocation。
 * 3. 彻底删除危险方块判定、空气层探测与快照队列。
 */
public class PlayerEventListener implements Listener {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;
    private final CheckManager checkManager;

    public PlayerEventListener(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.checkManager = plugin.getCheckManager();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onPlayerJoin(PlayerJoinEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (data != null) {
            data.getElytraData().resetAll();
        }

        // 记录统一会话 SESSION 加入日志
        String brand = com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().getClientBrand(player);
        int ping = com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().getPing(player);
        String ip = resolvePlayerIp(player);
        com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry sessionLog = new com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry.Builder(
                com.example.argusac.log.model.LogType.SESSION,
                plugin.getAuditLogManager().getServerId()
        )
                .player(player.getName())
                .uuid(player.getUniqueId())
                .ip(ip)
                .brand(brand)
                .ping(ping)
                .sessionAction("JOIN")
                .extra("world=" + player.getWorld().getName())
                .build();
        plugin.getAuditLogManager().submitLog(sessionLog);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onPlayerQuit(PlayerQuitEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        long duration = 0L;
        if (data != null) {
            data.getElytraData().resetAll();
            duration = Math.max(0L, System.currentTimeMillis() - data.getLastJoinTime());
        }

        // 记录统一会话 SESSION 退出日志
        com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry sessionLog = new com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry.Builder(
                com.example.argusac.log.model.LogType.SESSION,
                plugin.getAuditLogManager().getServerId()
        )
                .player(player.getName())
                .uuid(player.getUniqueId())
                .sessionAction("QUIT")
                .duration(duration)
                .build();
        plugin.getAuditLogManager().submitLog(sessionLog);

        plugin.getPlayerManager().removePlayerData(player.getUniqueId());
        plugin.getAlertManager().clearSessionState(player);
        if (plugin.getLanguageManager() != null) {
            plugin.getLanguageManager().invalidateCache(player.getUniqueId());
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onPlayerDeath(PlayerDeathEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(event.getEntity());
        if (data != null) {
            data.getElytraData().resetAll();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onPlayerRespawn(PlayerRespawnEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.getElytraData().resetAll();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onPlayerChangedWorld(PlayerChangedWorldEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (data != null) {
            data.getElytraData().resetAll();
        }

        // 记录跨世界 SESSION 日志
        com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry sessionLog = new com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry.Builder(
                com.example.argusac.log.model.LogType.SESSION,
                plugin.getAuditLogManager().getServerId()
        )
                .player(player.getName())
                .uuid(player.getUniqueId())
                .sessionAction("WORLD_CHANGE")
                .extra("from=" + event.getFrom().getName() + ";to=" + player.getWorld().getName())
                .build();
        plugin.getAuditLogManager().submitLog(sessionLog);
    }

    private String resolvePlayerIp(Player player) {
        if (!plugin.getConfig().getBoolean("audit.privacy.log-ip", false)) {
            return null;
        }
        if (player.getAddress() == null) return null;
        String raw = player.getAddress().getAddress().getHostAddress();
        if (plugin.getConfig().getBoolean("audit.privacy.hash-ip", true)) {
            String salt = plugin.getConfig().getString("audit.privacy.salt", "ArgusAC-Salt");
            String hashed = com.example.argusac.log.core.HashChainEngine.sha256(salt + ":" + raw);
            return hashed.substring(0, 16);
        }
        return raw;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlayerTeleportCheck(PlayerTeleportEvent event) {
        // 浮点数异常检测拦截 (NaN / Infinite)
        Location to = event.getTo();
        if (to != null && (Double.isNaN(to.getX()) || Double.isNaN(to.getY()) || Double.isNaN(to.getZ())
                || Double.isInfinite(to.getX()) || Double.isInfinite(to.getY()) || Double.isInfinite(to.getZ()))) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlayerTeleport(PlayerTeleportEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (plugin.getSetbackManager().isSelfTeleport(player.getUniqueId())) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (data != null) {
            data.setLastTeleportTime(System.currentTimeMillis());
            data.getElytraData().resetAll();

            // 外部传送赋予合法速度豁免
            if (event.getCause() == PlayerTeleportEvent.TeleportCause.ENDER_PEARL) {
                data.grantLegalVelocity(VelocitySourceType.ENDER_PEARL, 1000L);
            } else {
                data.grantLegalVelocity(VelocitySourceType.EXTERNAL_TELEPORT, 1500L);
            }
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onEntityToggleGlide(EntityToggleGlideEvent event) {
        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) {
            if (plugin.isPlayerExempt(player)) {
                return;
            }
            PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
            if (data != null) {
                checkManager.getElytraFlyCheck().handleToggleGlide(player, data, event);
            }
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onVehicleEnter(VehicleEnterEvent event) {
        if (event.getEntered() instanceof Player player) {
            PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
            if (data != null) {
                data.grantLegalVelocity(VelocitySourceType.VEHICLE, 1000L);
                data.getElytraData().resetGlideState();
            }
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onVehicleExit(VehicleExitEvent event) {
        if (event.getExited() instanceof Player player) {
            PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
            if (data != null) {
                data.grantLegalVelocity(VelocitySourceType.VEHICLE, 1000L);
                data.getElytraData().resetGlideState();
            }
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlayerToggleFlight(PlayerToggleFlightEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.setLastFlightToggleTime(System.currentTimeMillis());
            data.getElytraData().resetGlideState();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlayerVelocity(PlayerVelocityEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (data != null) {
            checkManager.getVelocityCheck().handleVelocity(player, event);
            checkManager.getElytraFlyCheck().handleVelocity(player, data, event);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlayerMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (plugin.isPlayerExempt(player)) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }

        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to = event.getTo();
        if (to == null || (from.getX() == to.getX() && from.getY() == to.getY() && from.getZ() == to.getZ())) {
            return;
        }

        data.setFromLocation(from);
        data.setToLocation(to);
        // 用服务端物理碰撞判断接地状态，替代已废弃的 player.isOnGround()
        boolean onGround = to.clone().subtract(0, 0.1, 0).getBlock().getType().isSolid();
        data.setOnGround(onGround);

        // P1 改法：将“防止回退递归/网络校正误报”与“关闭所有检测”拆开
        // 仅在当前移动为服务端回退的网络校正包时赋予短时校正速度豁免，检测流程继续正常运行，彻底消除 1.5 秒盲区
        if (plugin.getSetbackManager().isCorrectionMove(player, from, to)) {
            data.grantLegalVelocity(com.example.argusac.model.VelocitySourceType.SETBACK_CORRECTION, 300L);
        }

        Location originalTo = to.clone();

        // 1. 数据包完整性与异常角度检测
        checkManager.getBadPacketsCheck().handleMove(player, data, event);
        if (event.isCancelled() || hasSetbackOccurred(originalTo, event.getTo())) {
            data.setToLocation(event.getTo());
            return;
        }

        // 鞘翅滑翔检测 (ElytraFly) 与传统陆地/悬空移动检测 (Speed, Fly, NoFall) 显式互斥
        if (checkManager.isGlidingExempt(player, data)) {
            // 滑翔状态由 ElytraFly 集中全面接管 (包含 ElytraSpeed, ElytraAscend, ElytraHover, ElytraTimer 等)
            checkManager.getElytraFlyCheck().handleMove(player, data, event);
            if (event.isCancelled() || hasSetbackOccurred(originalTo, event.getTo())) {
                data.setToLocation(event.getTo());
                return;
            }
        } else {
            // 2. 移动速度检测
            checkManager.getSpeedCheck().handleMove(player, data, event);
            if (event.isCancelled() || hasSetbackOccurred(originalTo, event.getTo())) {
                data.setToLocation(event.getTo());
                return;
            }

            // 3. 飞行与非法滞空检测
            checkManager.getFlyCheck().handleMove(player, data, event);
            if (event.isCancelled() || hasSetbackOccurred(originalTo, event.getTo())) {
                data.setToLocation(event.getTo());
                return;
            }

            // 4. 虚假落地检测
            checkManager.getNoFallCheck().handleMove(player, data, event);
            if (event.isCancelled() || hasSetbackOccurred(originalTo, event.getTo())) {
                data.setToLocation(event.getTo());
                return;
            }
        }

        // 5. 背包移动检测
        checkManager.getInventoryMoveCheck().handleMove(player, data, event);
        if (event.isCancelled() || hasSetbackOccurred(originalTo, event.getTo())) {
            data.setToLocation(event.getTo());
            return;
        }

        // 6. 击退位移追踪
        checkManager.getVelocityCheck().handleMove(player, data, event);
    }

    private boolean hasSetbackOccurred(Location origTo, Location currentTo) {
        if (currentTo == null) {
            return true;
        }
        return origTo.getX() != currentTo.getX()
                || origTo.getY() != currentTo.getY()
                || origTo.getZ() != currentTo.getZ();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onEntityDamage(EntityDamageEvent event) {
        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) {
            PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
            if (data != null) {
                data.setLastDamageTime(System.currentTimeMillis());
                // 受伤击退赋予合法速度豁免
                data.grantLegalVelocity(VelocitySourceType.EXPLOSION, 800L);
                checkManager.getElytraFlyCheck().handleDamage(player, data, event);
            }
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onEntityDamageByEntity(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {
        Entity damager = event.getDamager();
        if (!(damager instanceof Player attacker)) {
            return;
        }

        if (plugin.isPlayerExempt(attacker)) {
            return;
        }

        // 点击计数
        checkManager.getAutoClickerCheck().handleClick(attacker);

        // 杀戮光环检测
        checkManager.getKillAuraCheck().handleAttack(attacker, event.getEntity(), event);
        if (event.isCancelled()) {
            return;
        }

        // 攻击距离检测
        checkManager.getReachCheck().handleAttack(attacker, event.getEntity(), event);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public void onPlayerInteract(PlayerInteractEvent event) {
        if (plugin.isPlayerExempt(event.getPlayer())) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            checkManager.getElytraFlyCheck().handleInteract(event.getPlayer(), data, event);
        }
        Action action = event.getAction();
        if (action == Action.LEFT_CLICK_AIR || action == Action.LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK) {
            checkManager.getAutoClickerCheck().handleClick(event.getPlayer());
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBlockBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (plugin.isPlayerExempt(player)) {
            return;
        }
        checkManager.getFastBreakCheck().handleBreak(player, event);
        if (event.isCancelled()) {
            return;
        }
        checkManager.getNukerCheck().handleBreak(player, event);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBlockPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (plugin.isPlayerExempt(player)) {
            return;
        }
        checkManager.getScaffoldCheck().handlePlace(player, event);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onInventoryOpen(InventoryOpenEvent event) {
        if (event.getPlayer() instanceof Player player) {
            PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
            if (data != null) {
                data.setInventoryOpen(true, player.getLocation());
            }
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onInventoryClose(InventoryCloseEvent event) {
        if (event.getPlayer() instanceof Player player) {
            PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
            if (data != null) {
                data.setInventoryOpen(false, null);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/log/buffer/PlayerLogRingBuffer.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.log.buffer;

import com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantReadWriteLock;

/**
 * 玩家独立定容环形缓冲 (默认 30 条)
 * 采用读写锁：满时自动覆盖最老元素，读取快照时不阻塞追加写入
 */
public class PlayerLogRingBuffer {

    private final int capacity;
    private final LogEntry[] buffer;
    private int head = 0;
    private int size = 0;
    private final ReentrantReadWriteLock rwLock = new ReentrantReadWriteLock();

    public PlayerLogRingBuffer(int capacity) {
        this.capacity = Math.max(5, capacity);
        this.buffer = new LogEntry[this.capacity];
    }

    public void push(LogEntry entry) {
        rwLock.writeLock().lock();
        try {
            int writeIndex = (head + size) % capacity;
            if (size == capacity) {
                buffer[writeIndex] = entry;
                head = (head + 1) % capacity;
            } else {
                buffer[writeIndex] = entry;
                size++;
            }
        } finally {
            rwLock.writeLock().unlock();
        }
    }

    public List<LogEntry> snapshot() {
        rwLock.readLock().lock();
        try {
            if (size == 0) return Collections.emptyList();
            List<LogEntry> list = new ArrayList<>(size);
            for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
                int idx = (head + i) % capacity;
                list.add(buffer[idx]);
            }
            return list;
        } finally {
            rwLock.readLock().unlock();
        }
    }

    public int size() {
        rwLock.readLock().lock();
        try {
            return size;
        } finally {
            rwLock.readLock().unlock();
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/log/core/AuditLogManager.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.log.core;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.log.buffer.PlayerLogRingBuffer;
import com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry;
import com.example.argusac.log.model.LogType;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.scheduler.BukkitTask;

import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermission;
import java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ArrayBlockingQueue;
import java.util.concurrent.BlockingQueue;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
import java.util.logging.Level;

/**
 * 审计日志写入总枢纽：
 * 1. 主线程零 I/O：仅入队引用
 * 2. 背压机制：满队列无异常丢弃并计数
 * 3. 单线程串行长驻 Writer
 * 4. 自动定期 Flush 与定期写 INTEGRITY 检查点
 */
public class AuditLogManager {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;
    private final File baseLogDir;
    private final String serverId;
    private final boolean hashChainEnabled;

    // 内存环形缓冲集合 (玩家独立)
    private final Map<UUID, PlayerLogRingBuffer> memoryBuffers = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final int memoryBufferCapacity;

    // 异步背压队列 (默认 5000 条硬上限)
    private final BlockingQueue<LogEntry> diskQueue;
    private final int queueCapacity;
    private final AtomicLong droppedLogsCount = new AtomicLong(0);

    // 单线程异步执行器与状态
    private final ExecutorService diskExecutor;
    private final AtomicBoolean running = new AtomicBoolean(true);

    // 长驻 Writer 状态 (仅在单线程内操作)
    private BufferedWriter currentWriter;
    private File currentLogFile;
    private String currentDateStr;
    private final Object writerLock = new Object();

    // 防篡改哈希链
    private final HashChainEngine hashChainEngine;
    private final AtomicLong writtenLinesCount = new AtomicLong(0);
    private long lastFlushTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
    private int uncommittedEntries = 0;
    private long lastIntegrityCheckpointTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
    private final int integrityIntervalEntries;
    private BukkitTask flushTimerTask;

    public AuditLogManager(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.baseLogDir = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "logs/audit");
        if (!baseLogDir.exists()) baseLogDir.mkdirs();

        this.serverId = plugin.getConfig().getString("audit.server-id", "survival-01");
        this.hashChainEnabled = plugin.getConfig().getBoolean("audit.hash-chain.enabled", true);
        String seed = plugin.getConfig().getString("audit.hash-chain.seed", "ArgusAC-Secure-Seed-2026");
        this.hashChainEngine = new HashChainEngine(seed);
        this.integrityIntervalEntries = plugin.getConfig().getInt("audit.hash-chain.checkpoint-interval-lines", 100);

        this.memoryBufferCapacity = plugin.getConfig().getInt("audit.memory-buffer-size", 30);
        this.queueCapacity = plugin.getConfig().getInt("audit.queue-capacity", 5000);
        this.diskQueue = new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(queueCapacity);

        // 守护进程单线程池
        this.diskExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(r -> {
            Thread t = new Thread(r, "ArgusAC-AuditWriter");
            t.setDaemon(true);
            return t;
        });

        // 启动后台写入轮询任务
        this.diskExecutor.submit(this::processDiskQueueLoop);

        // 定期 Flush 调度 (每 5 秒 = 100 ticks)
        this.flushTimerTask = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimerAsynchronously(plugin, () -> {
            if (!running.get()) return;
            flushIfNeeded(false);
            checkAndAlertBackpressure();
        }, 100L, 100L);
    }

    /**
     * 主线程零 I/O 提交日志入口：
     * 1. 放入玩家内存环形缓冲
     * 2. offer 进异步队列；满时直接丢弃计数，无阻塞、无异常
     */
    public void submitLog(LogEntry entry) {
        if (entry == null) return;

        // 1. 写入玩家内存缓冲 (仅限关联玩家的事件)
        if (entry.getUuid() != null) {
            PlayerLogRingBuffer buf = memoryBuffers.computeIfAbsent(
                    entry.getUuid(),
                    k -> new PlayerLogRingBuffer(memoryBufferCapacity)
            );
            buf.push(entry);
        }

        // 2. 提交到异步队列进行背压落盘
        if (!diskQueue.offer(entry)) {
            droppedLogsCount.incrementAndGet();
        }
    }

    private void processDiskQueueLoop() {
        while (running.get() || !diskQueue.isEmpty()) {
            try {
                LogEntry entry = diskQueue.poll(500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
                if (entry != null) {
                    writeEntryDirectly(entry);
                    uncommittedEntries++;
                    if (uncommittedEntries >= 100) {
                        flushInternal();
                    }
                }
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                break;
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                plugin.getLogger().log(Level.SEVERE, "[AuditLog] 写入日志异常", ex);
            }
        }
        flushInternal();
        closeWriterInternal();
    }

    private void writeEntryDirectly(LogEntry entry) throws IOException {
        ensureWriterReady();

        if (hashChainEnabled) {
            String signablePayload = entry.buildSignablePayload();
            String hash = hashChainEngine.computeNextHash(signablePayload);
            entry.setHash(hash);
        }

        String jsonLine = entry.toJson();
        synchronized (writerLock) {
            if (currentWriter != null) {
                currentWriter.write(jsonLine);
                currentWriter.newLine();
                writtenLinesCount.incrementAndGet();
            }
        }

        maybeTriggerIntegrityLog();
    }

    private void ensureWriterReady() throws IOException {
        String today = LocalDate.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ISO_DATE);
        synchronized (writerLock) {
            if (currentWriter == null || !today.equals(currentDateStr)) {
                rotateDayFileInternal(today);
            }
        }
    }

    private void rotateDayFileInternal(String newDateStr) throws IOException {
        closeWriterInternal();

        this.currentDateStr = newDateStr;
        this.currentLogFile = new File(baseLogDir, "audit-" + newDateStr + ".jsonl");
        boolean isNewFile = !currentLogFile.exists();

        FileOutputStream fos = new FileOutputStream(currentLogFile, true);
        this.currentWriter = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(fos, StandardCharsets.UTF_8), 32768);

        if (isNewFile) {
            applyFilePermissions(currentLogFile);
        }
    }

    private void applyFilePermissions(File file) {
        try {
            Set<PosixFilePermission> perms = PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-r-----");
            Files.setPosixFilePermissions(file.toPath(), perms);
        } catch (UnsupportedOperationException ignored) {
            // Windows 系统不支持 POSIX 文件权限，安全跳过
        } catch (Exception e) {
            plugin.getLogger().log(Level.FINE, "设置文件权限跳过: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void maybeTriggerIntegrityLog() {
        long lines = writtenLinesCount.get();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        boolean byLines = (lines % integrityIntervalEntries == 0);
        boolean byTime = (now - lastIntegrityCheckpointTime >= 60_000L);

        if (hashChainEnabled && (byLines || byTime)) {
            lastIntegrityCheckpointTime = now;
            LogEntry integrity = new LogEntry.Builder(LogType.INTEGRITY, serverId)
                    .latestHash(hashChainEngine.getLastHash())
                    .lineCount(lines)
                    .build();
            diskQueue.offer(integrity);
        }
    }

    private void flushIfNeeded(boolean force) {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (force || uncommittedEntries > 0 || (now - lastFlushTime >= 5000L)) {
            diskExecutor.submit(this::flushInternal);
        }
    }

    private void flushInternal() {
        synchronized (writerLock) {
            if (currentWriter != null) {
                try {
                    currentWriter.flush();
                    lastFlushTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
                    uncommittedEntries = 0;
                } catch (IOException e) {
                    plugin.getLogger().log(Level.WARNING, "[AuditLog] Flush 失败: " + e.getMessage());
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private void closeWriterInternal() {
        synchronized (writerLock) {
            if (currentWriter != null) {
                try {
                    currentWriter.flush();
                    currentWriter.close();
                } catch (IOException ignored) {}
                currentWriter = null;
            }
        }
    }

    private void checkAndAlertBackpressure() {
        int size = diskQueue.size();
        if (size > (queueCapacity * 0.5)) {
            String msg = String.format("§c[ArgusAC 警告] 审计日志队列积压已达 %d/%d (%.1f%%)！请注意磁盘 I/O！",
                    size, queueCapacity, (size * 100.0 / queueCapacity));
            Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers().stream()
                    .filter(p -> p.hasPermission("argusac.alert") || p.hasPermission("ArgusAC.alert"))
                    .filter(p -> !plugin.getAlertManager().isMuted(p))
                    .forEach(p -> p.sendMessage(msg));
            plugin.getLogger().warning(msg);
        }
    }

    public void rotateNow() {
        diskExecutor.submit(() -> {
            try {
                String today = LocalDate.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ISO_DATE);
                rotateDayFileInternal(today);
            } catch (IOException e) {
                plugin.getLogger().log(Level.SEVERE, "手动轮转日志失败", e);
            }
        });
    }

    public PlayerLogRingBuffer getMemoryBuffer(UUID uuid) {
        return memoryBuffers.get(uuid);
    }

    public void removeMemoryBuffer(UUID uuid) {
        memoryBuffers.remove(uuid);
    }

    public void shutdown() {
        running.set(false);
        if (flushTimerTask != null) {
            flushTimerTask.cancel();
        }
        diskExecutor.shutdown();
        try {
            if (!diskExecutor.awaitTermination(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
                diskExecutor.shutdownNow();
            }
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            diskExecutor.shutdownNow();
        }
    }

    // Status Getters
    public long getDroppedCount() { return droppedLogsCount.get(); }
    public int getQueueBacklog() { return diskQueue.size(); }
    public int getQueueCapacity() { return queueCapacity; }
    public long getCurrentFileSize() { return currentLogFile != null && currentLogFile.exists() ? currentLogFile.length() : 0L; }
    public long getLastFlushTime() { return lastFlushTime; }
    public String getLatestHash() { return hashChainEngine.getLastHash(); }
    public File getBaseLogDir() { return baseLogDir; }
    public String getServerId() { return serverId; }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/log/core/HashChainEngine.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.log.core;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.security.NoSuchAlgorithmException;

/**
 * 轻量 SHA-256 链式防篡改哈希计算器
 */
public class HashChainEngine {

    private final String initialSeed;
    private String lastHash;

    public HashChainEngine(String initialSeed) {
        this.initialSeed = initialSeed != null && !initialSeed.isEmpty() ? initialSeed : "ArgusAC-Default-Seed";
        this.lastHash = null;
    }

    public synchronized void reset(String lastHash) {
        this.lastHash = lastHash;
    }

    public synchronized String computeNextHash(String signablePayload) {
        String prev = (this.lastHash == null) ? this.initialSeed : this.lastHash;
        String raw = prev + ":" + signablePayload;
        String newHash = sha256(raw);
        this.lastHash = newHash;
        return newHash;
    }

    public synchronized String getLastHash() {
        return lastHash != null ? lastHash : initialSeed;
    }

    public static String computeVerificationHash(String prevHashOrSeed, String signablePayload) {
        return sha256(prevHashOrSeed + ":" + signablePayload);
    }

    public static String sha256(String input) {
        try {
            MessageDigest digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
            byte[] encodedhash = digest.digest(input.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            StringBuilder hexString = new StringBuilder(2 * encodedhash.length);
            for (byte b : encodedhash) {
                String hex = Integer.toHexString(0xff & b);
                if (hex.length() == 1) hexString.append('0');
                hexString.append(hex);
            }
            return hexString.toString();
        } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException e) {
            throw new RuntimeException("SHA-256 算法不支持", e);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/log/debug/CheckDebugLogger.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.log.debug;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;

import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileWriter;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

/**
 * 临时检测模块调试日志记录器 (不进入哈希链，独立写入 logs/debug/ 目录)
 */
public class CheckDebugLogger {

    private final File debugDir;
    private final Set<String> activeDebugChecks = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
    private final ExecutorService debugExecutor;

    public CheckDebugLogger(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        this.debugDir = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "logs/debug");
        if (!debugDir.exists()) debugDir.mkdirs();
        this.debugExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(r -> {
            Thread t = new Thread(r, "ArgusAC-DebugLogWriter");
            t.setDaemon(true);
            return t;
        });
    }

    public boolean toggle(String checkName) {
        String normalized = checkName.toLowerCase();
        if (activeDebugChecks.contains(normalized)) {
            activeDebugChecks.remove(normalized);
            return false;
        } else {
            activeDebugChecks.add(normalized);
            return true;
        }
    }

    public boolean isDebug(String checkName) {
        return checkName != null && activeDebugChecks.contains(checkName.toLowerCase());
    }

    public void log(String checkName, String playerName, String message) {
        if (!isDebug(checkName)) return;

        debugExecutor.submit(() -> {
            File checkFile = new File(debugDir, checkName.toLowerCase() + ".log");
            try (BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(new FileWriter(checkFile, true))) {
                String now = LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.SSS"));
                writer.write(String.format("[%s] [%s] %s%n", now, playerName, message));
            } catch (IOException ignored) {}
        });
    }

    public void shutdown() {
        debugExecutor.shutdown();
    }
}
```
