`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/log/maintenance/LogArchiveService.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.log.maintenance;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.log.core.AuditLogManager;
import com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry;
import com.example.argusac.log.model.LogType;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.scheduler.BukkitTask;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileInputStream;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit;
import java.util.zip.GZIPOutputStream;

/**
 * 日志文件生命周期管控：
 * 1. 自动轮转
 * 2. 超过 7 天的文件分片压缩为 .jsonl.gz (附带 TPS 动态降级保护)
 * 3. 超过 retention-days (默认30天) 的文件自动物理清理并记录 ADMIN 日志
 */
public class LogArchiveService {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;
    private final AuditLogManager logManager;
    private final int retentionDays;
    private final boolean keepAdminPermanent;
    private final double minTpsThreshold;
    private BukkitTask archiveTask;

    public LogArchiveService(ArgusACPlugin plugin, AuditLogManager logManager) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.logManager = logManager;
        this.retentionDays = plugin.getConfig().getInt("audit.archive.retention-days", 30);
        this.keepAdminPermanent = plugin.getConfig().getBoolean("audit.archive.keep-admin-permanent", true);
        this.minTpsThreshold = plugin.getConfig().getDouble("audit.archive.min-tps-threshold", 18.5);

        scheduleDailyMaintenance();
    }

    private void scheduleDailyMaintenance() {
        // 每 1 小时检查一次 (72000 ticks)
        this.archiveTask = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimerAsynchronously(plugin, this::performMaintenance, 200L, 72000L);
    }

    public synchronized void performMaintenance() {
        File dir = logManager.getBaseLogDir();
        if (!dir.exists() || !dir.isDirectory()) return;

        LocalDate now = LocalDate.now();
        File[] files = dir.listFiles((d, name) -> name.startsWith("audit-") && (name.endsWith(".jsonl") || name.endsWith(".jsonl.gz")));
        if (files == null) return;

        // 1. 检查压缩 (大于 7 天的 .jsonl 压缩为 .jsonl.gz)
        for (File file : files) {
            String name = file.getName();
            if (name.endsWith(".jsonl")) {
                LocalDate fileDate = extractDateFromFilename(name);
                if (fileDate != null && ChronoUnit.DAYS.between(fileDate, now) >= 7) {
                    compressFileWithTpsThrottling(file);
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. 检查过期清理
        for (File file : files) {
            LocalDate fileDate = extractDateFromFilename(file.getName());
            if (fileDate != null && ChronoUnit.DAYS.between(fileDate, now) > retentionDays) {
                deleteExpiredFile(file);
            }
        }
    }

    private void compressFileWithTpsThrottling(File source) {
        File targetGz = new File(source.getParentFile(), source.getName() + ".gz");
        if (targetGz.exists()) return;

        byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];
        try (FileInputStream fis = new FileInputStream(source);
             FileOutputStream fos = new FileOutputStream(targetGz);
             GZIPOutputStream gzos = new GZIPOutputStream(fos)) {

            int len;
            int counter = 0;
            while ((len = fis.read(buffer)) > 0) {
                gzos.write(buffer, 0, len);
                counter++;
                // 每读写 64KB (8 次) 检查一次 TPS
                if (counter % 8 == 0) {
                    throttleIfTpsDrops();
                }
            }
            gzos.finish();

            source.delete();
            plugin.getLogger().info("[LogArchive] 成功归档压缩历史文件: " + targetGz.getName());
        } catch (IOException e) {
            plugin.getLogger().warning("[LogArchive] 压缩文件失败 " + source.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
            if (targetGz.exists()) targetGz.delete();
        }
    }

    private void throttleIfTpsDrops() {
        try {
            double currentTps = Bukkit.getTPS()[0];
            while (currentTps < minTpsThreshold) {
                Thread.sleep(1000L);
                currentTps = Bukkit.getTPS()[0];
            }
        } catch (InterruptedException ignored) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }

    private void deleteExpiredFile(File file) {
        String filename = file.getName();
        if (keepAdminPermanent && !filename.contains("admin-permanent")) {
            extractAndPreserveAdminLogs(file);
        }
        boolean deleted = file.delete();
        if (deleted) {
            plugin.getLogger().info("[LogArchive] 已清理过期日志文件: " + filename);
            LogEntry adminLog = new LogEntry.Builder(LogType.ADMIN, logManager.getServerId())
                    .actor("SYSTEM")
                    .action("LOG_PURGE_EXPIRED")
                    .args("file=" + filename + ";retentionDays=" + retentionDays)
                    .result("SUCCESS")
                    .build();
            logManager.submitLog(adminLog);
        }
    }

    private void extractAndPreserveAdminLogs(File file) {
        File permanentFile = new File(logManager.getBaseLogDir(), "audit-admin-permanent.jsonl");
        try (java.io.BufferedReader reader = openReader(file);
             java.io.BufferedWriter writer = new java.io.BufferedWriter(new java.io.FileWriter(permanentFile, true))) {
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                if (line.contains("\"type\":\"ADMIN\"")) {
                    writer.write(line);
                    writer.newLine();
                }
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            plugin.getLogger().warning("[LogArchive] 提取永久管理员日志失败: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private java.io.BufferedReader openReader(File file) throws IOException {
        if (file.getName().endsWith(".gz")) {
            return new java.io.BufferedReader(new java.io.InputStreamReader(new java.util.zip.GZIPInputStream(new FileInputStream(file)), java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        } else {
            return new java.io.BufferedReader(new java.io.InputStreamReader(new FileInputStream(file), java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        }
    }

    private LocalDate extractDateFromFilename(String name) {
        try {
            String datePart = name.substring(6, 16);
            return LocalDate.parse(datePart, DateTimeFormatter.ISO_DATE);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    public void cancel() {
        if (archiveTask != null) {
            archiveTask.cancel();
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/log/model/CoordPoint.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.log.model;

import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import org.bukkit.Location;

/**
 * 结构化紧凑坐标模型 (统一格式，yaw/pitch 仅在必要时包含)
 */
public record CoordPoint(String world, double x, double y, double z, Float yaw, Float pitch) {

    public CoordPoint(String world, double x, double y, double z) {
        this(world, x, y, z, null, null);
    }

    public static CoordPoint fromLocation(Location loc, boolean includeRotations) {
        if (loc == null) return null;
        String w = loc.getWorld() != null ? loc.getWorld().getName() : "world";
        double rx = Math.round(loc.getX() * 1000.0) / 1000.0;
        double ry = Math.round(loc.getY() * 1000.0) / 1000.0;
        double rz = Math.round(loc.getZ() * 1000.0) / 1000.0;
        if (includeRotations) {
            float ryaw = Math.round(loc.getYaw() * 10.0f) / 10.0f;
            float rpitch = Math.round(loc.getPitch() * 10.0f) / 10.0f;
            return new CoordPoint(w, rx, ry, rz, ryaw, rpitch);
        }
        return new CoordPoint(w, rx, ry, rz);
    }

    public JsonObject toJson() {
        JsonObject obj = new JsonObject();
        obj.addProperty("world", world);
        obj.addProperty("x", x);
        obj.addProperty("y", y);
        obj.addProperty("z", z);
        if (yaw != null) obj.addProperty("yaw", yaw);
        if (pitch != null) obj.addProperty("pitch", pitch);
        return obj;
    }

    public static CoordPoint fromJson(JsonObject obj) {
        if (obj == null) return null;
        String world = obj.has("world") ? obj.get("world").getAsString() : "world";
        double x = obj.has("x") ? obj.get("x").getAsDouble() : 0.0;
        double y = obj.has("y") ? obj.get("y").getAsDouble() : 0.0;
        double z = obj.has("z") ? obj.get("z").getAsDouble() : 0.0;
        Float yaw = obj.has("yaw") ? obj.get("yaw").getAsFloat() : null;
        Float pitch = obj.has("pitch") ? obj.get("pitch").getAsFloat() : null;
        return new CoordPoint(world, x, y, z, yaw, pitch);
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return String.format("(%.1f,%.1f,%.1f)", x, y, z);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/log/model/LogEntry.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.log.model;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder;
import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;

import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * 统一日志实体：包含 5 个基础必须字段与按需可选字段
 * 序列化/反序列化时严格遵循不输出 null 原则
 */
public class LogEntry {

    private static final Gson GSON = new GsonBuilder().disableHtmlEscaping().create();

    // ==================== 统一必须基础字段 ====================
    private final int v;                  // 格式版本号 (初始为 1)
    private final LogType type;           // 事件类型
    private final long time;              // 毫秒时间戳
    private final String server;          // 服务器节点标识
    private final UUID traceId;           // 链路关联 ID
    private String hash;                  // 链式哈希 (落盘时填入)

    // ==================== 玩家相关 (可选) ====================
    private String player;
    private UUID uuid;
    private String ip;
    private String brand;
    private Integer ping;

    // ==================== 违规相关 (可选) ====================
    private String check;
    private Integer vl;
    private LogSeverity severity;
    private String reason;
    private Map<String, Object> metrics;

    // ==================== 回退相关 (可选) ====================
    private String mode;                  // HARD / SOFT / LOG_ONLY
    private CoordPoint from;
    private CoordPoint to;
    private Boolean fallbackUsed;

    // ==================== 管理员相关 (可选) ====================
    private String actor;
    private UUID actorUuid;
    private String action;
    private String target;
    private String args;
    private String result;                // SUCCESS / FAILED / DENIED

    // ==================== 会话相关 (可选) ====================
    private String sessionAction;         // JOIN / QUIT / WORLD_CHANGE
    private Long duration;                // 在线时长 (ms)
    private String extra;                 // 附加上下文

    // ==================== 完整性检查点相关 (可选) ====================
    private String latestHash;
    private Long lineCount;

    private LogEntry(Builder builder) {
        this.v = builder.v;
        this.type = builder.type;
        this.time = builder.time;
        this.server = builder.server;
        this.traceId = builder.traceId != null ? builder.traceId : UUID.randomUUID();
        this.hash = builder.hash;

        this.player = builder.player;
        this.uuid = builder.uuid;
        this.ip = builder.ip;
        this.brand = builder.brand;
        this.ping = builder.ping;

        this.check = builder.check;
        this.vl = builder.vl;
        this.severity = builder.severity;
        this.reason = builder.reason;
        this.metrics = builder.metrics != null ? Collections.unmodifiableMap(builder.metrics) : null;

        this.mode = builder.mode;
        this.from = builder.from;
        this.to = builder.to;
        this.fallbackUsed = builder.fallbackUsed;

        this.actor = builder.actor;
        this.actorUuid = builder.actorUuid;
        this.action = builder.action;
        this.target = builder.target;
        this.args = builder.args;
        this.result = builder.result;

        this.sessionAction = builder.sessionAction;
        this.duration = builder.duration;
        this.extra = builder.extra;

        this.latestHash = builder.latestHash;
        this.lineCount = builder.lineCount;
    }

    /**
     * 构建用于计算哈希的规范化载荷字符串 (除 hash 字段以外的规范化 JSON)
     */
    public String buildSignablePayload() {
        JsonObject obj = serializeToJsonObject();
        obj.remove("hash");
        return GSON.toJson(obj);
    }

    public String toJson() {
        return GSON.toJson(serializeToJsonObject());
    }

    public JsonObject serializeToJsonObject() {
        JsonObject root = new JsonObject();
        root.addProperty("v", v);
        root.addProperty("type", type.name());
        root.addProperty("time", time);
        root.addProperty("server", server);
        root.addProperty("traceId", traceId.toString());
        if (hash != null) root.addProperty("hash", hash);

        if (player != null) root.addProperty("player", player);
        if (uuid != null) root.addProperty("uuid", uuid.toString());
        if (ip != null) root.addProperty("ip", ip);
        if (brand != null) root.addProperty("brand", brand);
        if (ping != null) root.addProperty("ping", ping);

        if (check != null) root.addProperty("check", check);
        if (vl != null) root.addProperty("vl", vl);
        if (severity != null) root.addProperty("severity", severity.name());
        if (reason != null) root.addProperty("reason", reason);
        if (metrics != null && !metrics.isEmpty()) {
            root.add("metrics", GSON.toJsonTree(metrics));
        }

        if (mode != null) root.addProperty("mode", mode);
        if (from != null) root.add("from", from.toJson());
        if (to != null) root.add("to", to.toJson());
        if (fallbackUsed != null) root.addProperty("fallbackUsed", fallbackUsed);

        if (actor != null) root.addProperty("actor", actor);
        if (actorUuid != null) root.addProperty("actorUuid", actorUuid.toString());
        if (action != null) root.addProperty("action", action);
        if (target != null) root.addProperty("target", target);
        if (args != null) root.addProperty("args", args);
        if (result != null) root.addProperty("result", result);

        if (sessionAction != null) root.addProperty("sessionAction", sessionAction);
        if (duration != null) root.addProperty("duration", duration);
        if (extra != null) root.addProperty("extra", extra);

        if (latestHash != null) root.addProperty("latestHash", latestHash);
        if (lineCount != null) root.addProperty("lineCount", lineCount);

        return root;
    }

    public static LogEntry fromJson(String jsonString) {
        JsonObject obj = JsonParser.parseString(jsonString).getAsJsonObject();
        Builder b = new Builder(
                LogType.valueOf(obj.get("type").getAsString()),
                obj.get("server").getAsString()
        );

        if (obj.has("v")) b.version(obj.get("v").getAsInt());
        if (obj.has("time")) b.time(obj.get("time").getAsLong());
        if (obj.has("traceId")) b.traceId(UUID.fromString(obj.get("traceId").getAsString()));
        if (obj.has("hash")) b.hash(obj.get("hash").getAsString());

        if (obj.has("player")) b.player(obj.get("player").getAsString());
        if (obj.has("uuid")) b.uuid(UUID.fromString(obj.get("uuid").getAsString()));
        if (obj.has("ip")) b.ip(obj.get("ip").getAsString());
        if (obj.has("brand")) b.brand(obj.get("brand").getAsString());
        if (obj.has("ping")) b.ping(obj.get("ping").getAsInt());

        if (obj.has("check")) b.check(obj.get("check").getAsString());
        if (obj.has("vl")) b.vl(obj.get("vl").getAsInt());
        if (obj.has("severity")) b.severity(LogSeverity.valueOf(obj.get("severity").getAsString()));
        if (obj.has("reason")) b.reason(obj.get("reason").getAsString());
        if (obj.has("metrics")) {
            JsonObject m = obj.getAsJsonObject("metrics");
            for (Map.Entry<String, JsonElement> e : m.entrySet()) {
                if (e.getValue().isJsonPrimitive()) {
                    if (e.getValue().getAsJsonPrimitive().isNumber()) {
                        b.metric(e.getKey(), e.getValue().getAsDouble());
                    } else if (e.getValue().getAsJsonPrimitive().isBoolean()) {
                        b.metric(e.getKey(), e.getValue().getAsBoolean());
                    } else {
                        b.metric(e.getKey(), e.getValue().getAsString());
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (obj.has("mode")) b.mode(obj.get("mode").getAsString());
        if (obj.has("from")) b.from(CoordPoint.fromJson(obj.getAsJsonObject("from")));
        if (obj.has("to")) b.to(CoordPoint.fromJson(obj.getAsJsonObject("to")));
        if (obj.has("fallbackUsed")) b.fallbackUsed(obj.get("fallbackUsed").getAsBoolean());

        if (obj.has("actor")) b.actor(obj.get("actor").getAsString());
        if (obj.has("actorUuid")) b.actorUuid(UUID.fromString(obj.get("actorUuid").getAsString()));
        if (obj.has("action")) b.action(obj.get("action").getAsString());
        if (obj.has("target")) b.target(obj.get("target").getAsString());
        if (obj.has("args")) b.args(obj.get("args").getAsString());
        if (obj.has("result")) b.result(obj.get("result").getAsString());

        if (obj.has("sessionAction")) b.sessionAction(obj.get("sessionAction").getAsString());
        if (obj.has("duration")) b.duration(obj.get("duration").getAsLong());
        if (obj.has("extra")) b.extra(obj.get("extra").getAsString());

        if (obj.has("latestHash")) b.latestHash(obj.get("latestHash").getAsString());
        if (obj.has("lineCount")) b.lineCount(obj.get("lineCount").getAsLong());

        return b.build();
    }

    // Getters & Setters
    public int getV() { return v; }
    public LogType getType() { return type; }
    public long getTime() { return time; }
    public String getServer() { return server; }
    public UUID getTraceId() { return traceId; }
    public String getHash() { return hash; }
    public void setHash(String hash) { this.hash = hash; }

    public String getPlayer() { return player; }
    public UUID getUuid() { return uuid; }
    public String getIp() { return ip; }
    public String getBrand() { return brand; }
    public Integer getPing() { return ping; }

    public String getCheck() { return check; }
    public Integer getVl() { return vl; }
    public LogSeverity getSeverity() { return severity; }
    public String getReason() { return reason; }
    public Map<String, Object> getMetrics() { return metrics; }

    public String getMode() { return mode; }
    public CoordPoint getFrom() { return from; }
    public CoordPoint getTo() { return to; }
    public Boolean isFallbackUsed() { return fallbackUsed; }

    public String getActor() { return actor; }
    public UUID getActorUuid() { return actorUuid; }
    public String getAction() { return action; }
    public String getTarget() { return target; }
    public String getArgs() { return args; }
    public String getResult() { return result; }

    public String getSessionAction() { return sessionAction; }
    public Long getDuration() { return duration; }
    public String getExtra() { return extra; }

    public String getLatestHash() { return latestHash; }
    public Long getLineCount() { return lineCount; }

    public static class Builder {
        private int v = 1;
        private final LogType type;
        private long time = System.currentTimeMillis();
        private final String server;
        private UUID traceId = UUID.randomUUID();
        private String hash;

        private String player;
        private UUID uuid;
        private String ip;
        private String brand;
        private Integer ping;

        private String check;
        private Integer vl;
        private LogSeverity severity;
        private String reason;
        private Map<String, Object> metrics;

        private String mode;
        private CoordPoint from;
        private CoordPoint to;
        private Boolean fallbackUsed;

        private String actor;
        private UUID actorUuid;
        private String action;
        private String target;
        private String args;
        private String result;

        private String sessionAction;
        private Long duration;
        private String extra;

        private String latestHash;
        private Long lineCount;

        public Builder(LogType type, String server) {
            this.type = type;
            this.server = server;
        }

        public Builder version(int v) { this.v = v; return this; }
        public Builder time(long time) { this.time = time; return this; }
        public Builder traceId(UUID traceId) { this.traceId = traceId; return this; }
        public Builder hash(String hash) { this.hash = hash; return this; }

        public Builder player(String player) { this.player = player; return this; }
        public Builder uuid(UUID uuid) { this.uuid = uuid; return this; }
        public Builder ip(String ip) { this.ip = ip; return this; }
        public Builder brand(String brand) { this.brand = brand; return this; }
        public Builder ping(Integer ping) { this.ping = ping; return this; }

        public Builder check(String check) { this.check = check; return this; }
        public Builder vl(Integer vl) { this.vl = vl; return this; }
        public Builder severity(LogSeverity severity) { this.severity = severity; return this; }
        public Builder reason(String reason) { this.reason = reason; return this; }
        public Builder metric(String key, Object val) {
            if (this.metrics == null) this.metrics = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            this.metrics.put(key, val);
            return this;
        }

        public Builder mode(String mode) { this.mode = mode; return this; }
        public Builder from(CoordPoint from) { this.from = from; return this; }
        public Builder to(CoordPoint to) { this.to = to; return this; }
        public Builder fallbackUsed(Boolean fallbackUsed) { this.fallbackUsed = fallbackUsed; return this; }

        public Builder actor(String actor) { this.actor = actor; return this; }
        public Builder actorUuid(UUID actorUuid) { this.actorUuid = actorUuid; return this; }
        public Builder action(String action) { this.action = action; return this; }
        public Builder target(String target) { this.target = target; return this; }
        public Builder args(String args) { this.args = args; return this; }
        public Builder result(String result) { this.result = result; return this; }

        public Builder sessionAction(String sessionAction) { this.sessionAction = sessionAction; return this; }
        public Builder duration(Long duration) { this.duration = duration; return this; }
        public Builder extra(String extra) { this.extra = extra; return this; }

        public Builder latestHash(String latestHash) { this.latestHash = latestHash; return this; }
        public Builder lineCount(Long lineCount) { this.lineCount = lineCount; return this; }

        public LogEntry build() {
            return new LogEntry(this);
        }
    }
}
```

```java
package com.example.argusac.log.model;

/**
 * 统一事件类型枚举 (严格限定 5 种核心类型)
 */
public enum LogType {
    VIOLATION,     // 违规触发与回退执行
    SETBACK_FAIL,  // 回退操作失败 (如位置被外部方块卡死阻挡)
    SESSION,       // 玩家会话变更 (加入/离开/跨世界)
    ADMIN,         // 管理员操作审计 (指令操作/配置重载/数据导出/签名校验等)
    INTEGRITY      // 链式哈希周期性检查点
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/log/query/LogQueryService.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.log.query;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.log.buffer.PlayerLogRingBuffer;
import com.example.argusac.log.core.AuditLogManager;
import com.example.argusac.log.core.HashChainEngine;
import com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry;
import com.example.argusac.log.model.LogType;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileInputStream;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.io.FileReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.function.Consumer;
import java.util.function.Predicate;
import java.util.zip.GZIPInputStream;
import java.util.zip.ZipEntry;
import java.util.zip.ZipOutputStream;

/**
 * 审计查询、流式过滤、聚合榜单与哈希链核验服务
 */
public class LogQueryService {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;
    private final AuditLogManager logManager;

    public LogQueryService(ArgusACPlugin plugin, AuditLogManager logManager) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.logManager = logManager;
    }

    public List<LogEntry> queryMemoryLogs(UUID uuid, String checkFilter, long sinceMs, boolean setbackOnly) {
        PlayerLogRingBuffer buf = logManager.getMemoryBuffer(uuid);
        if (buf == null)
            return Collections.emptyList();

        List<LogEntry> raw = buf.snapshot();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        return raw.stream()
                .filter(e -> sinceMs <= 0 || (now - e.getTime()) <= sinceMs)
                .filter(e -> checkFilter == null
                        || (e.getCheck() != null && e.getCheck().equalsIgnoreCase(checkFilter)))
                .filter(e -> !setbackOnly || (e.getFrom() != null && e.getTo() != null))
                .toList();
    }

    public void streamHistoricalLogs(UUID targetUuid, int days, Predicate<LogEntry> filter,
            Consumer<List<LogEntry>> onComplete) {
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskAsynchronously(plugin, () -> {
            List<LogEntry> result = new ArrayList<>();
            File dir = logManager.getBaseLogDir();
            File[] files = dir.listFiles(
                    (d, name) -> name.startsWith("audit-") && (name.endsWith(".jsonl") || name.endsWith(".jsonl.gz")));
            if (files == null) {
                onComplete.accept(result);
                return;
            }

            Arrays.sort(files, (f1, f2) -> f2.getName().compareTo(f1.getName()));
            int scannedDays = 0;

            for (File file : files) {
                if (scannedDays >= days)
                    break;
                scannedDays++;

                try (BufferedReader reader = openLogReader(file)) {
                    String line;
                    while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                        if (line.isBlank())
                            continue;
                        try {
                            LogEntry entry = LogEntry.fromJson(line);
                            if (targetUuid == null || targetUuid.equals(entry.getUuid())) {
                                if (filter == null || filter.test(entry)) {
                                    result.add(entry);
                                }
                            }
                        } catch (Exception ignored) {
                        }
                    }
                } catch (IOException e) {
                    plugin.getLogger().warning("读取历史文件失败: " + file.getName());
                }
            }

            onComplete.accept(result);
        });
    }

    public void queryTopViolators(long sinceMs, Consumer<List<TopRecord>> callback) {
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskAsynchronously(plugin, () -> {
            Map<String, TopRecordBuilder> stats = new HashMap<>();
            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

            File dir = logManager.getBaseLogDir();
            File[] files = dir.listFiles(
                    (d, name) -> name.startsWith("audit-") && (name.endsWith(".jsonl") || name.endsWith(".jsonl.gz")));
            if (files != null) {
                for (File file : files) {
                    try (BufferedReader reader = openLogReader(file)) {
                        String line;
                        while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                            if (line.isBlank())
                                continue;
                            try {
                                LogEntry e = LogEntry.fromJson(line);
                                if (e.getType() != LogType.VIOLATION)
                                    continue;
                                if (sinceMs > 0 && (now - e.getTime()) > sinceMs)
                                    continue;
                                if (e.getPlayer() == null)
                                    continue;

                                TopRecordBuilder b = stats.computeIfAbsent(e.getPlayer(), TopRecordBuilder::new);
                                b.recordViolation(e.getCheck(), e.getVl() != null ? e.getVl() : 1, e.getTo() != null);
                            } catch (Exception ignored) {
                            }
                        }
                    } catch (IOException ignored) {
                    }
                }
            }

            List<TopRecord> sorted = stats.values().stream()
                    .map(b -> b.build())
                    .sorted((r1, r2) -> Integer.compare(r2.totalVl(), r1.totalVl()))
                    .limit(10)
                    .toList();

            callback.accept(sorted);
        });
    }

    public void verifyHashChainAsync(Consumer<VerificationResult> callback) {
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskAsynchronously(plugin, () -> {
            File dir = logManager.getBaseLogDir();
            File[] files = dir.listFiles((d, name) -> name.startsWith("audit-") && name.endsWith(".jsonl"));
            if (files == null || files.length == 0) {
                callback.accept(new VerificationResult(true, 0, null, null));
                return;
            }

            Arrays.sort(files, (f1, f2) -> f1.getName().compareTo(f2.getName()));
            String initialSeed = plugin.getConfig().getString("audit.hash-chain.seed", "ArgusAC-Secure-Seed-2026");
            String previousHash = initialSeed;
            long currentLine = 0;

            for (File file : files) {
                try (BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new FileReader(file))) {
                    String line;
                    while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                        if (line.isBlank())
                            continue;
                        currentLine++;

                        LogEntry entry = LogEntry.fromJson(line);
                        String recordedHash = entry.getHash();
                        String signablePayload = entry.buildSignablePayload();
                        String expectedHash = HashChainEngine.computeVerificationHash(previousHash, signablePayload);

                        if (recordedHash == null || !recordedHash.equalsIgnoreCase(expectedHash)) {
                            VerificationResult fail = new VerificationResult(false, currentLine, file.getName(),
                                    recordedHash);
                            callback.accept(fail);
                            return;
                        }
                        previousHash = recordedHash;
                    }
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    callback.accept(
                            new VerificationResult(false, currentLine, file.getName(), "IO_ERROR: " + e.getMessage()));
                    return;
                }
            }

            callback.accept(new VerificationResult(true, currentLine, null, null));
        });
    }

    public void exportPlayerData(String targetName, UUID targetUuid, Consumer<File> callback) {
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskAsynchronously(plugin, () -> {
            File exportDir = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "exports");
            if (!exportDir.exists())
                exportDir.mkdirs();

            String timeStamp = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss").format(java.time.LocalDateTime.now());
            File zipFile = new File(exportDir, "export_" + targetName + "_" + timeStamp + ".zip");

            try (ZipOutputStream zos = new ZipOutputStream(new FileOutputStream(zipFile))) {
                zos.putNextEntry(new ZipEntry("player_audit_" + targetName + ".jsonl"));

                File dir = logManager.getBaseLogDir();
                File[] files = dir.listFiles((d, name) -> name.startsWith("audit-")
                        && (name.endsWith(".jsonl") || name.endsWith(".jsonl.gz")));
                if (files != null) {
                    for (File file : files) {
                        try (BufferedReader reader = openLogReader(file)) {
                            String line;
                            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                                if (line.isBlank())
                                    continue;
                                if (line.contains(targetUuid.toString())
                                        || line.contains("\"player\":\"" + targetName + "\"")) {
                                    zos.write(line.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                                    zos.write("\n".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                zos.closeEntry();
                callback.accept(zipFile);
            } catch (IOException e) {
                plugin.getLogger().log(java.util.logging.Level.SEVERE, "导出玩家数据失败: " + e.getMessage(), e);
                callback.accept(null);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 彻底安全清除指定玩家的审计记录与反作弊运行时数据 (GDPR/合规性安全注销)
     */
    public void purgePlayerData(String targetName, UUID targetUuid) {
        // 1. 清除玩家内存环形缓冲
        if (targetUuid != null) {
            logManager.removeMemoryBuffer(targetUuid);
        }

        // 2. 清除玩家运行时 PlayerData 数据
        if (plugin.getPlayerManager() != null && targetUuid != null) {
            com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData pData = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(targetUuid);
            if (pData != null) {
                pData.clearAllData();
            }
        }

        // 3. 清理回退管理器中的上下文
        if (plugin.getSetbackManager() != null && targetUuid != null) {
            com.example.argusac.manager.SetbackManager.PlayerSetbackContext ctx = plugin.getSetbackManager().getContext(targetUuid);
            if (ctx != null) {
                ctx.clear();
            }
        }

        // 4. 在审计链中记录注销事件，确保哈希链审计可溯源且符合合规性标准
        logManager.submitLog(new LogEntry.Builder(LogType.ADMIN, logManager.getServerId())
                .actor("SYSTEM_PURGE")
                .action("PURGE_PLAYER_DATA")
                .target(targetName)
                .args(targetUuid != null ? targetUuid.toString() : "")
                .result("SUCCESS")
                .extra("GDPR/Compliance purge requested")
                .build());
    }

    private BufferedReader openLogReader(File file) throws IOException {
        if (file.getName().endsWith(".gz")) {
            return new BufferedReader(
                    new InputStreamReader(new GZIPInputStream(new FileInputStream(file)), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        } else {
            return new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(new FileInputStream(file), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        }
    }

    public record VerificationResult(boolean valid, long line, String filename, String detail) {
    }

    public record TopRecord(String player, String mainCheck, int totalVl, int setbackCount) {
    }

    private static class TopRecordBuilder {
        private final String player;
        private int totalVl = 0;
        private int setbackCount = 0;
        private final Map<String, Integer> checkCounts = new HashMap<>();

        public TopRecordBuilder(String player) {
            this.player = player;
        }

        public void recordViolation(String check, int vl, boolean setback) {
            this.totalVl += vl;
            if (setback)
                this.setbackCount++;
            if (check != null)
                checkCounts.merge(check, 1, (oldV, newV) -> oldV + newV);
        }

        public TopRecord build() {
            String mainCheck = checkCounts.entrySet().stream()
                    .max((e1, e2) -> Integer.compare(e1.getValue(), e2.getValue()))
                    .map(e -> e.getKey())
                    .orElse("Unknown");
            return new TopRecord(player, mainCheck, totalVl, setbackCount);
        }
    }
}
```
