`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/log/replay/AsciiReplayRenderer.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.log.replay;

import com.example.argusac.log.model.CoordPoint;
import com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 纯字符 ASCII 轨迹与高度条轻量渲染器
 * 无需图形界面、零 Web 前端依赖、完全基于 Minecraft 聊天栏
 */
public class AsciiReplayRenderer {

    private static final int GRID_SIZE = 17; // 17x17 适中聊天框网格
    private static final char[] DENSITY_MAP = {' ', '.', ':', '-', '=', '+', '*', '#', '%'};

    public List<String> render(LogEntry violation, boolean sideView) {
        List<String> output = new ArrayList<>();
        if (violation == null || violation.getFrom() == null || violation.getTo() == null) {
            output.add("&c无有效轨迹坐标可回放。");
            return output;
        }

        CoordPoint from = violation.getFrom();
        CoordPoint to = violation.getTo();

        output.add("&e=== 移动轨迹 ASCII 取证回放: &b" + violation.getPlayer() + " &e(" + (sideView ? "XY 侧视图" : "XZ 俯视图") + ") ===");
        output.add(String.format("&7检测项: &c%s &8| &7严重度: &e%s &8| &7回退模式: &b%s",
                violation.getCheck(), violation.getSeverity(), violation.getMode()));
        output.add(String.format("&7起点(S): &a%s &8-> &7回退点(X): &c%s", from, to));

        char[][] grid = new char[GRID_SIZE][GRID_SIZE];
        for (char[] row : grid) Arrays.fill(row, '·');

        // 计算缩放映射
        double x1 = from.x();
        double z1 = sideView ? from.y() : from.z();
        double x2 = to.x();
        double z2 = sideView ? to.y() : to.z();

        double minX = Math.min(x1, x2) - 1.5;
        double maxX = Math.max(x1, x2) + 1.5;
        double minZ = Math.min(z1, z2) - 1.5;
        double maxZ = Math.max(z1, z2) + 1.5;

        int sx = mapCoord(x1, minX, maxX, GRID_SIZE);
        int sz = mapCoord(z1, minZ, maxZ, GRID_SIZE);
        int ex = mapCoord(x2, minX, maxX, GRID_SIZE);
        int ez = mapCoord(z2, minZ, maxZ, GRID_SIZE);

        drawTrajectory(grid, sx, sz, ex, ez);

        grid[sz][sx] = 'S';
        grid[ez][ex] = 'X';

        double yStart = from.y();
        double yEnd = to.y();
        double minY = Math.min(yStart, yEnd) - 1.0;
        double maxY = Math.max(yStart, yEnd) + 1.0;

        output.add("&8+-----------------+  &7[高度条]");
        for (int r = 0; r < GRID_SIZE; r++) {
            StringBuilder rowBuilder = new StringBuilder("&8|");
            for (int c = 0; c < GRID_SIZE; c++) {
                char ch = grid[r][c];
                if (ch == 'S') rowBuilder.append("&aS");
                else if (ch == 'X') rowBuilder.append("&cX");
                else if (ch == '*') rowBuilder.append("&e*");
                else rowBuilder.append("&8·");
            }
            rowBuilder.append("&8|  ");

            double rowYRatio = 1.0 - ((double) r / (GRID_SIZE - 1));
            int densityIdx = (int) Math.round(rowYRatio * (DENSITY_MAP.length - 1));
            char densityChar = DENSITY_MAP[Math.max(0, Math.min(densityIdx, DENSITY_MAP.length - 1))];
            rowBuilder.append("&b").append(densityChar).append(" &7Y: ").append(String.format("%.1f", minY + (maxY - minY) * rowYRatio));

            output.add(rowBuilder.toString());
        }
        output.add("&8+-----------------+");
        output.add("&7图例: &aS&7=最后合法起点, &e*&7=异常越界位移, &cX&7=强制回退点");

        return output;
    }

    private int mapCoord(double val, double min, double max, int size) {
        if (Math.abs(max - min) < 1e-4) return size / 2;
        double ratio = (val - min) / (max - min);
        int mapped = (int) Math.round(ratio * (size - 1));
        return Math.max(0, Math.min(mapped, size - 1));
    }

    private void drawTrajectory(char[][] grid, int x0, int y0, int x1, int y1) {
        int dx = Math.abs(x1 - x0);
        int dy = Math.abs(y1 - y0);
        int sx = x0 < x1 ? 1 : -1;
        int sy = y0 < y1 ? 1 : -1;
        int err = dx - dy;

        while (true) {
            grid[y0][x0] = '*';
            if (x0 == x1 && y0 == y1) break;
            int e2 = 2 * err;
            if (e2 > -dy) {
                err -= dy;
                x0 += sx;
            }
            if (e2 < dx) {
                err += dx;
                y0 += sy;
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/manager/AlertManager.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.manager;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.i18n.I18n;
import com.example.argusac.util.ColorUtil;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.event.ClickEvent;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.event.HoverEvent;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Sound;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * 警报通知管理器 (AlertManager)
 * 负责向在线管理员分发违规告警消息，全面集成 i18n 客户端多语言自适应与 Adventure 文本交互
 */
public class AlertManager {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;

    private boolean enabled;
    private boolean soundEnabled;
    private Sound soundType;
    private float soundVolume;
    private float soundPitch;
    private long cooldownMs;

    // 针对每个玩家+检测项的告警冷却映射 (Key: "PlayerName:CheckName")
    private final Map<String, Long> alertCooldowns = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final java.util.Set<java.util.UUID> mutedPlayers = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

    public AlertManager(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.reloadConfig();
    }

    public void reloadConfig() {
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfig();
        this.enabled = config.getBoolean("alerts.enabled", true);
        this.cooldownMs = config.getLong("alerts.cooldown-ms", 1000L);

        this.soundEnabled = config.getBoolean("alerts.sound.enabled", true);
        String soundName = config.getString("alerts.sound.type", "BLOCK_NOTE_BLOCK_BASS");
        try {
            this.soundType = Sound.valueOf(soundName);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            this.soundType = Sound.BLOCK_NOTE_BLOCK_BASS;
        }
        this.soundVolume = (float) config.getDouble("alerts.sound.volume", 1.0);
        this.soundPitch = (float) config.getDouble("alerts.sound.pitch", 1.2);
    }

    /**
     * 发送检测违规警报给所有拥有 ArgusAC.alert 权限的管理员 (全面接入 i18n 客户端自适应)
     */
    public void sendAlert(Player cheater, String checkName, int currentVl, int maxVl, String details) {
        if (!enabled || cheater == null) {
            return;
        }

        // 检查冷却
        String coolKey = cheater.getName() + ":" + checkName;
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        Long lastTime = alertCooldowns.get(coolKey);
        if (lastTime != null && now - lastTime < cooldownMs) {
            return;
        }
        alertCooldowns.put(coolKey, now);

        int ping = com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().getPing(cheater);
        int displayPing = Math.max(0, ping);
        double[] tpsArr = com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().getTPS();
        String tpsStr = (tpsArr != null && tpsArr.length > 0) ? String.format("%.1f", Math.min(20.0, tpsArr[0])) : "20.0";
        var checkObj = plugin.getCheckManager().getCheck(checkName);
        String checkType = (checkObj != null) ? checkObj.getType().name() : "OTHER";

        for (Player admin : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
            if ((admin.hasPermission("argusac.alert") || admin.hasPermission("ArgusAC.alert")) && !isMuted(admin)) {
                // 基于每个管理员的客户端语言偏好渲染警报文本
                Component baseComponent = I18n.component(admin, "alert.format",
                        "player", cheater.getName(),
                        "check", checkName,
                        "type", checkType,
                        "vl", currentVl,
                        "max_vl", maxVl,
                        "ping", displayPing,
                        "tps", tpsStr,
                        "detail", details);

                Component hoverComponent = I18n.component(admin, "alert.hover-details",
                        "player", cheater.getName(),
                        "detail", details);

                Component finalComponent = baseComponent
                        .hoverEvent(HoverEvent.showText(hoverComponent))
                        .clickEvent(ClickEvent.runCommand("/tp " + cheater.getName()));

                admin.sendMessage(finalComponent);

                if (soundEnabled && soundType != null) {
                    admin.playSound(admin.getLocation(), soundType, soundVolume, soundPitch);
                }
            }
        }

        // 发送给控制台 (使用服务端基准语言)
        Component consoleComp = I18n.component(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), "alert.format",
                "player", cheater.getName(),
                "check", checkName,
                "type", checkType,
                "vl", currentVl,
                "max_vl", maxVl,
                "ping", displayPing,
                "tps", tpsStr,
                "detail", details);
        Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(consoleComp);
    }

    /**
     * 向管理员广播系统信息
     */
    public void broadcastAdmin(String message) {
        Component comp = ColorUtil.toComponent(message);
        for (Player admin : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
            if ((admin.hasPermission("argusac.alert") || admin.hasPermission("ArgusAC.alert")) && !isMuted(admin)) {
                admin.sendMessage(comp);
            }
        }
        Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(comp);
    }

    public boolean isMuted(Player player) {
        return player != null && mutedPlayers.contains(player.getUniqueId());
    }

    public void setMuted(Player player, boolean muted) {
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }
        if (muted) {
            mutedPlayers.add(player.getUniqueId());
        } else {
            mutedPlayers.remove(player.getUniqueId());
        }
    }

    /**
     * 玩家退出时清除会话状态与警报冷却记录
     */
    public void clearCooldowns(String playerName) {
        if (playerName == null) {
            return;
        }
        String prefix = playerName.toLowerCase() + ":";
        alertCooldowns.keySet().removeIf(k -> k.toLowerCase().startsWith(prefix));
    }

    public void clearSessionState(Player player) {
        if (player != null) {
            mutedPlayers.remove(player.getUniqueId());
            clearCooldowns(player.getName());
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/manager/PunishmentManager.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.manager;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.Check;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;

import java.util.*;
import java.util.logging.Level;

/**
 * 惩罚调度管理器 (PunishmentManager)
 * <p>
 * 【惩罚分级体系】：
 * 1. 默认处罚手段 (Tier 1 Default Punishment)：回退 (Setback) 机制。
 *    作为反作弊系统第一道防线，默认对所有确认违规立即执行回退与动量撤销，阻止任何非法收益。
 * 2. 升级惩罚手段 (Tier 2 Escalated Punishment)：控制台指令 (Kick/Ban)。
 *    当玩家在经历默认回退处罚后仍恶意持续作弊、VL 达到高阈值时，自动升级执行指令惩戒。
 */
public class PunishmentManager {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;
    private boolean enabled;
    private boolean setbackAsDefault;
    private int defaultActionMinVl;
    private final List<PunishmentRule> rules = new ArrayList<>();

    public PunishmentManager(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.reloadConfig();
    }

    public void reloadConfig() {
        rules.clear();
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfig();
        this.enabled = config.getBoolean("punishments.enabled", true);
        this.setbackAsDefault = config.getBoolean("punishments.default-action.setback-as-default", true);
        this.defaultActionMinVl = config.getInt("punishments.default-action.min-vl", 1);

        List<Map<?, ?>> actionList = config.getMapList("punishments.actions");
        for (Map<?, ?> item : actionList) {
            try {
                Object vlObj = item.get("vl");
                Object cmdObj = item.get("command");
                if (vlObj != null && cmdObj != null) {
                    int vl = Integer.parseInt(vlObj.toString());
                    String cmd = cmdObj.toString();
                    rules.add(new PunishmentRule(vl, cmd));
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                plugin.getLogger().warning("解析惩罚规则失败: " + item);
            }
        }
        // 按 VL 从小到大排序
        rules.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(r -> r.getVlThreshold()));
    }

    /**
     * 执行默认处罚手段 (Default Punishment: Setback 回退机制)
     *
     * @param player 违规玩家
     * @param data 玩家运行时数据
     * @param check 触发的检测项
     * @param currentVl 当前违规等级
     * @param details 违规详情
     * @param moveEvent 移动事件 (可为 null)
     * @return 是否成功触发并执行了默认回退处罚
     */
    public boolean executeDefaultPunishment(Player player, PlayerData data, Check check,
                                           int currentVl, String details, PlayerMoveEvent moveEvent) {
        if (!setbackAsDefault || player == null || check == null) {
            return false;
        }

        SetbackManager setbackManager = plugin.getSetbackManager();
        if (setbackManager.shouldSetback(player, check, currentVl)) {
            return setbackManager.mitigate(player, check, currentVl, details, moveEvent);
        }
        return false;
    }

    /**
     * 校验玩家违规等级，并执行对应阶段的升级惩罚命令 (如 Kick/Ban)
     */
    public void checkAndPunish(Player player, String checkName, int currentVl) {
        if (!enabled || player == null || !player.isOnline()) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }

        for (PunishmentRule rule : rules) {
            // 使用 >= 避免单次违规加分跳过临界值 (例如 19 -> 21) 导致惩罚失效
            if (currentVl >= rule.getVlThreshold() && data.getLastPunishedVl() < rule.getVlThreshold()) {
                data.setLastPunishedVl(rule.getVlThreshold());
                String cmd = rule.getCommand()
                        .replace("{player}", player.getName())
                        .replace("{check}", checkName)
                        .replace("{vl}", String.valueOf(currentVl));

                if (!Bukkit.isPrimaryThread()) {
                    Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> executeCommand(cmd, player.getName()));
                } else {
                    executeCommand(cmd, player.getName());
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private void executeCommand(String command, String playerName) {
        try {
            plugin.getLogger().log(Level.INFO, "[Punishment] 正在对玩家 {0} 执行自动升级惩罚命令: {1}",
                    new Object[]{playerName, command});
            Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), command);
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            plugin.getLogger().log(Level.SEVERE, "执行惩罚指令失败: " + command, ex);
        }
    }

    public boolean isSetbackAsDefault() {
        return setbackAsDefault;
    }

    public int getDefaultActionMinVl() {
        return defaultActionMinVl;
    }

    private static class PunishmentRule {
        private final int vlThreshold;
        private final String command;

        public PunishmentRule(int vlThreshold, String command) {
            this.vlThreshold = vlThreshold;
            this.command = command;
        }

        public int getVlThreshold() {
            return vlThreshold;
        }

        public String getCommand() {
            return command;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/manager/SetbackManager.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.manager;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.Check;
import com.example.argusac.api.CheckType;
import com.example.argusac.model.LegitimateLocation;
import com.example.argusac.model.SetbackFailureLog;
import com.example.argusac.model.SetbackOverride;
import com.example.argusac.model.ViolationLog;
import com.example.argusac.util.ColorUtil;
import com.example.argusac.util.MathUtil;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.PlayerDeathEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.*;
import org.bukkit.event.vehicle.VehicleEnterEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.vehicle.VehicleExitEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedDeque;
import java.util.logging.Level;

/**
 * 状态回退全能集中管理器 (SetbackManager)
 * <p>
 * 【架构设计与集中化原则】：
 * 1. 严格将回退约束于移动类检测 (CheckType.MOVEMENT)，战斗与方块交互违规通过取消事件处理，不执行传送；
 * 2. 移除 HARD 传送后对玩家步行/飞行速度的强制覆盖重置，保留服务器与技能配置；
 * 3. 维护短窗口已验证移动快照，选择同世界、时效有效且距离有界的目标；超出边界时安全降级取消当前移动；
 * 4. 采用跨版本玩家物理碰撞箱检测目标空间通行性；
 * 5. 将网络校正追踪与检测剥离，收窄自免疫窗口，避免检测盲区；
 * 6. 支持按检测模块独立节流冷却，并在冷却或失败时优先取消当前非法移动。
 */
public class SetbackManager implements Listener {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;

    // ==================== 配置参数 ====================
    private boolean globalEnabled;
    private String defaultMode; // "SOFT" 或 "HARD"
    private long cooldownMs;
    private int globalMinVl;
    private boolean kickWhenBothBlocked;
    private String kickMessage;
    private final Set<String> exemptedWorlds = new HashSet<>();

    // 边界与快照配置
    private double maxSetbackDistance;
    private long maxSnapshotAgeMs;
    private int maxSnapshotCapacity;

    // 网络校正与自免疫参数
    private long correctionTimeoutMs;
    private long minImmunityMs;
    private long immunityExtraMs;

    // 提示通知参数
    private String notifyPlayerChannel; // "ACTIONBAR", "CHAT", "NONE"
    private long notifyPlayerCooldownMs;
    private boolean notifyAdminEnabled;

    // ==================== 集中化状态存储 ====================
    private final Map<UUID, PlayerSetbackContext> contextMap = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Set<UUID> selfTeleportingPlayers = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

    public SetbackManager(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.reloadConfig();
    }

    /**
     * 重载回退配置文件
     */
    public void reloadConfig() {
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfig();

        this.globalEnabled = config.getBoolean("setback.global-enabled", true);
        this.defaultMode = config.getString("setback.default-mode", "HARD").toUpperCase();
        this.cooldownMs = config.getLong("setback.cooldown-ms", 350L);
        this.globalMinVl = config.getInt("setback.default-min-vl", 1);
        this.kickWhenBothBlocked = config.getBoolean("setback.boundary.kick-when-both-blocked", false);
        this.kickMessage = config.getString("setback.boundary.kick-message",
                "&c[ArgusAC] 移动异常且历史位置已被阻挡，已安全断开连接。");

        this.exemptedWorlds.clear();
        for (String w : config.getStringList("setback.exempted-worlds")) {
            this.exemptedWorlds.add(w.toLowerCase());
        }

        this.maxSetbackDistance = config.getDouble("setback.boundary.max-distance", 8.0);
        this.maxSnapshotAgeMs = config.getLong("setback.snapshot.max-age-ms", 2500L);
        this.maxSnapshotCapacity = config.getInt("setback.snapshot.max-capacity", 30);

        this.correctionTimeoutMs = config.getLong("setback.correction.timeout-ms", 600L);
        this.minImmunityMs = config.getLong("setback.immunity.min-duration-ms", 400L);
        this.immunityExtraMs = config.getLong("setback.immunity.extra-buffer-ms", 150L);

        this.notifyPlayerChannel = config.getString("setback.notifications.player.channel", "ACTIONBAR").toUpperCase();
        this.notifyPlayerCooldownMs = config.getLong("setback.notifications.player.cooldown-ms", 1000L);

        this.notifyAdminEnabled = config.getBoolean("setback.notifications.admin.enabled", true);
    }

    // ==============================================================================
    // 自包含生命周期与移动监听 (Event Listeners)
    // ==============================================================================

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onPlayerJoin(PlayerJoinEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerSetbackContext context = getContext(player.getUniqueId());
        context.reset(player.getLocation(), player.isInsideVehicle(),
                player.getVehicle() != null ? player.getVehicle().getUniqueId() : null);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onPlayerQuit(PlayerQuitEvent event) {
        UUID uid = event.getPlayer().getUniqueId();
        contextMap.remove(uid);
        selfTeleportingPlayers.remove(uid);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onPlayerDeath(PlayerDeathEvent event) {
        PlayerSetbackContext context = contextMap.get(event.getEntity().getUniqueId());
        if (context != null) {
            context.clear();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onPlayerRespawn(PlayerRespawnEvent event) {
        PlayerSetbackContext context = getContext(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId());
        context.reset(event.getRespawnLocation(), false, null);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onPlayerChangedWorld(PlayerChangedWorldEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerSetbackContext context = getContext(player.getUniqueId());
        UUID vehicleId = (player.isInsideVehicle() && player.getVehicle() != null)
                ? player.getVehicle().getUniqueId() : null;
        context.reset(player.getLocation(), player.isInsideVehicle(), vehicleId);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlayerTeleport(PlayerTeleportEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        UUID uid = player.getUniqueId();

        // 识别本反作弊自身发起的内部回退传送，跳过重置
        if (selfTeleportingPlayers.contains(uid)) {
            return;
        }

        Location to = event.getTo();
        if (to != null) {
            PlayerSetbackContext context = getContext(uid);
            UUID vehicleId = (player.isInsideVehicle() && player.getVehicle() != null)
                    ? player.getVehicle().getUniqueId() : null;
            context.reset(to, player.isInsideVehicle(), vehicleId);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onVehicleEnter(VehicleEnterEvent event) {
        if (event.getEntered() instanceof Player player) {
            PlayerSetbackContext context = getContext(player.getUniqueId());
            context.resetPreviousOnly();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onVehicleExit(VehicleExitEvent event) {
        if (event.getExited() instanceof Player player) {
            PlayerSetbackContext context = getContext(player.getUniqueId());
            context.resetPreviousOnly();
        }
    }

    /**
     * 在 MONITOR 优先级监听移动事件：
     * 仅当移动事件已通过所有检测模块 (HIGHEST 优先级)、未被取消且坐标确实改变时，采样维护短时快照
     */
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlayerMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (plugin.isPlayerExempt(player)) {
            return;
        }

        Location to = event.getTo();
        Location from = event.getFrom();
        if (to == null || (from.getX() == to.getX() && from.getY() == to.getY() && from.getZ() == to.getZ())) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerSetbackContext context = getContext(player.getUniqueId());

        // 客户端坐标校正匹配消费
        if (context.hasPendingCorrection() && context.matchesCorrection(to)) {
            context.clearPendingCorrection();
        }

        // 若处于自免疫期，不采样可能残留过渡抖动的坐标
        if (context.isImmune()) {
            return;
        }

        UUID vehicleId = (player.isInsideVehicle() && player.getVehicle() != null)
                ? player.getVehicle().getUniqueId() : null;

        LegitimateLocation verified = LegitimateLocation.from(
                to,
                player.getVelocity(),
                to.clone().subtract(0, 0.1, 0).getBlock().getType().isSolid(),
                player.isInsideVehicle(),
                vehicleId
        );

        if (verified != null) {
            context.update(verified, maxSnapshotCapacity, maxSnapshotAgeMs);
        }
    }

    // ==============================================================================
    // 核心处罚决策与执行入口 (Mitigation API)
    // ==============================================================================

    /**
     * 判断某个检测模块和玩家当前是否应该执行回退 (P0: 仅限移动检测，按检测模块独立冷却)
     */
    public boolean shouldSetback(Player player, Check check, int currentVl) {
        if (player == null || check == null) {
            return false;
        }

        if (plugin.isPlayerExempt(player)) {
            return false;
        }

        // P0 改法：回退机制严格仅针对移动类违规！战斗与方块交互通过取消事件处理，绝不传送
        if (check.getType() != CheckType.MOVEMENT) {
            return false;
        }

        if (player.getWorld() != null && exemptedWorlds.contains(player.getWorld().getName().toLowerCase())) {
            return false;
        }

        PlayerSetbackContext context = getContext(player.getUniqueId());

        // 一级裁决：单人覆写
        SetbackOverride pOverride = context.getOverride();
        if (pOverride == SetbackOverride.DISABLED) {
            return false;
        }

        // 二级与三级裁决：单人默认时受全局和模块开关管控
        if (pOverride == SetbackOverride.DEFAULT) {
            if (!globalEnabled || !check.isSetbackEnabled()) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        // P2: 按检测模块独立冷却检查，避免不同检测连续触发时互相压制
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long lastCheckSetback = context.getLastSetbackTimestamp(check.getName());
        if (now - lastCheckSetback < cooldownMs) {
            return false;
        }

        // 违规等级门槛检查
        int requiredVl = check.getSetbackMinVl() > 0 ? check.getSetbackMinVl() : globalMinVl;
        return currentVl >= requiredVl;
    }

    public boolean shouldSetback(Player player, com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData data, Check check, int currentVl) {
        return shouldSetback(player, check, currentVl);
    }

    /**
     * 查询指定玩家的特定检测模块是否正处于回退冷却中
     */
    public boolean isCheckOnCooldown(UUID uuid, String checkName) {
        PlayerSetbackContext context = contextMap.get(uuid);
        if (context == null) return false;
        return (System.currentTimeMillis() - context.getLastSetbackTimestamp(checkName)) < cooldownMs;
    }

    /**
     * 判断某次移动是否是由服务端回退引发的客户端网络坐标校正
     */
    public boolean isCorrectionMove(Player player, Location from, Location to) {
        if (player == null || to == null) return false;
        PlayerSetbackContext context = contextMap.get(player.getUniqueId());
        if (context == null) return false;
        return context.matchesCorrection(to);
    }

    /**
     * 默认反作弊惩戒统一执行入口 (Mitigate / Setback)
     *
     * @param player 违规玩家
     * @param check 触发的检测模块
     * @param currentVl 违规等级
     * @param reason 违规详情
     * @param moveEvent 若在 PlayerMoveEvent 内调用可传入；可为 null
     * @return 是否成功处置违规
     */
    public boolean mitigate(Player player, Check check, int currentVl, String reason, PlayerMoveEvent moveEvent) {
        if (player == null || !player.isOnline() || check == null) {
            return false;
        }

        // 严格限制回退仅用于移动违规
        if (check.getType() != CheckType.MOVEMENT) {
            return false;
        }

        // 若非主线程调用且无移动事件，安全调度回主线程执行
        if (!Bukkit.isPrimaryThread() && moveEvent == null) {
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> mitigate(player, check, currentVl, reason, null));
            return true;
        }

        PlayerSetbackContext context = getContext(player.getUniqueId());
        Location curLoc = player.getLocation();
        String fromLocStr = String.format("%.2f,%.2f,%.2f", curLoc.getX(), curLoc.getY(), curLoc.getZ());
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // P0 改法：InventoryMove 等优先原地取消本次移动，不进行远距离传送
        if ("InventoryMove".equalsIgnoreCase(check.getName()) && moveEvent != null) {
            moveEvent.setTo(moveEvent.getFrom());
            context.setLastSetbackTimestamp(check.getName(), now);
            postSetbackSuccess(player, context, check.getName(), currentVl, reason, "CANCEL_MOVE", fromLocStr, fromLocStr, false);
            return true;
        }

        // P1 改法：在短时间窗口内检索同世界、未超时且距离有界的通行快照
        UUID curWorldUid = (curLoc.getWorld() != null) ? curLoc.getWorld().getUID() : null;
        LegitimateLocation target = context.findBoundedLegitimateLocation(
                curWorldUid,
                curLoc,
                maxSetbackDistance,
                maxSnapshotAgeMs
        );
        boolean usedFallback = false;

        // 若未找到符合有界且可通行的快照：执行安全降级策略
        if (target == null) {
            // 降级 1: 若当前存在移动事件，直接将终点设为起点 from，安全取消本次位移，不盲目跨区大传送
            if (moveEvent != null && moveEvent.getFrom() != null) {
                moveEvent.setTo(moveEvent.getFrom());
                context.setLastSetbackTimestamp(check.getName(), now);
                postSetbackSuccess(player, context, check.getName(), currentVl, reason, "SAFE_FALLBACK_CANCEL", fromLocStr, fromLocStr, true);
                return true;
            }

            // 降级 2: 无移动事件时，在同世界尝试寻找距离最近的可通行快照 (放宽至 2 倍最大距离)
            LegitimateLocation relaxedTarget = context.findNearestPassableLocation(curWorldUid, curLoc, maxSetbackDistance * 2.0);
            if (relaxedTarget != null) {
                target = relaxedTarget;
                usedFallback = true;
            } else {
                recordFailure(player, context, check.getName(), "UNBOUNDED_OR_OCCLUDED", fromLocStr);
                if (kickWhenBothBlocked) {
                    dispatchKick(player, kickMessage);
                }
                return false;
            }
        }

        Location targetLocation = target.toLocation();
        if (targetLocation == null || targetLocation.getWorld() == null) {
            recordFailure(player, context, check.getName(), "WORLD_UNAVAILABLE", target.toString());
            return false;
        }

        // 对齐 Grim 等主流反作弊核心机制：严格保留玩家当前的水平与垂直朝向 (Yaw/Pitch)
        // 彻底杜绝回退时由于恢复历史朝向导致的视角瞬间扭转 (Snap View / 视角扯头眩晕)
        float currentYaw = (moveEvent != null && moveEvent.getTo() != null) ? moveEvent.getTo().getYaw() : curLoc.getYaw();
        float currentPitch = (moveEvent != null && moveEvent.getTo() != null) ? moveEvent.getTo().getPitch() : curLoc.getPitch();
        targetLocation.setYaw(currentYaw);
        targetLocation.setPitch(currentPitch);

        // 确定执行模式
        String effectiveMode = determineEffectiveMode(context.getOverride(), check.getSetbackMode());
        String toLocStr = String.format("%.2f,%.2f,%.2f", targetLocation.getX(), targetLocation.getY(), targetLocation.getZ());

        // 记录预期网络校正目标与时效
        int ping = Math.max(0, com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().getPing(player));
        long correctionTimeout = Math.min(correctionTimeoutMs, 2L * ping + immunityExtraMs);
        context.setPendingCorrection(new PendingCorrection(
                targetLocation.getX(), targetLocation.getY(), targetLocation.getZ(),
                targetLocation.getWorld().getUID(),
                now + correctionTimeout
        ));
        context.setLastSetbackTimestamp(check.getName(), now);

        // 执行具体模式 (若玩家在载具中，必须走 HARD 模式脱离载具传送)
        final LegitimateLocation finalTarget = target;
        final boolean finalUsedFallback = usedFallback;

        if ("SOFT".equalsIgnoreCase(effectiveMode) && moveEvent != null && !player.isInsideVehicle()) {
            // SOFT 模式：直接在事件内修正终点
            moveEvent.setTo(targetLocation);
            context.setLastLegitimateLocation(finalTarget);
            postSetbackSuccess(player, context, check.getName(), currentVl, reason, "SOFT", fromLocStr, toLocStr, finalUsedFallback);
            return true;
        } else {
            // HARD 模式：主线程执行强制传送与动量清空
            if (!Bukkit.isPrimaryThread()) {
                Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () ->
                        executeHardSetbackOnMainThread(player, context, targetLocation, finalTarget, check.getName(), currentVl, reason, fromLocStr, toLocStr, finalUsedFallback)
                );
            } else {
                executeHardSetbackOnMainThread(player, context, targetLocation, finalTarget, check.getName(), currentVl, reason, fromLocStr, toLocStr, finalUsedFallback);
            }
            return true;
        }
    }

    public boolean execute(Player player, com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData data, Check check,
                           int currentVl, String reason, PlayerMoveEvent moveEvent) {
        return mitigate(player, check, currentVl, reason, moveEvent);
    }

    public boolean execute(Player player, Check check, int currentVl, String reason, PlayerMoveEvent moveEvent) {
        return mitigate(player, check, currentVl, reason, moveEvent);
    }

    /**
     * HARD 模式在主线程执行传送与动量重置 (P0: 移除速度默认值重置，保留服务器设置)
     */
    private void executeHardSetbackOnMainThread(Player player, PlayerSetbackContext context,
                                                Location targetLocation, LegitimateLocation target,
                                                String checkName, int currentVl, String reason,
                                                String fromLocStr, String toLocStr, boolean usedFallback) {
        if (!player.isOnline()) {
            recordFailure(player, context, checkName, "PLAYER_OFFLINE", toLocStr);
            return;
        }

        try {
            selfTeleportingPlayers.add(player.getUniqueId());

            if (player.isInsideVehicle()) {
                player.leaveVehicle();
            }

            boolean teleportSuccess = player.teleport(targetLocation, PlayerTeleportEvent.TeleportCause.PLUGIN);

            if (teleportSuccess) {
                // P0 改法：清除异常动量与坠落距离，绝不重置 walkSpeed / flySpeed，保留服务器与技能配置
                player.setVelocity(new Vector(0, 0, 0));
                
                // 对齐 Grim 等主流反作弊免摔保护策略：
                // 若目标合法点在地面，安全重置坠落距离；若在空中，保留合理高度，防止作弊者利用回退当免摔刷伤害
                if (target != null && target.isOnGround()) {
                    player.setFallDistance(0.0f);
                }

                // 同步更新最后合法位置为回退落点
                if (target != null) {
                    context.setLastLegitimateLocation(target);
                }

                postSetbackSuccess(player, context, checkName, currentVl, reason, "HARD", fromLocStr, toLocStr, usedFallback);
            } else {
                recordFailure(player, context, checkName, "TELEPORT_CANCELLED", toLocStr);
            }

        } catch (Exception ex) {
            plugin.getLogger().log(Level.WARNING, "执行玩家 " + player.getName() + " HARD 回退时异常", ex);
            recordFailure(player, context, checkName, "EXCEPTION_" + ex.getMessage(), toLocStr);
        } finally {
            selfTeleportingPlayers.remove(player.getUniqueId());
        }
    }

    /**
     * 回退成功后的统一后续处理
     */
    private void postSetbackSuccess(Player player, PlayerSetbackContext context, String checkName, int currentVl,
                                    String details, String mode, String fromLocStr, String toLocStr, boolean usedFallback) {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // 1. 写入收窄后的自免疫截止时间戳 (P1: 由 1500ms 大幅收窄为与 ping 自适应的短时间窗口)
        int ping = Math.max(0, com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().getPing(player));
        long immunityDuration = Math.max(minImmunityMs, 2L * ping + immunityExtraMs);
        context.setImmunityUntil(now + immunityDuration);
        context.setLastSetbackTimestamp(now);

        // 2. 玩家端提示 (带独立冷却，走 i18n 多语言)
        sendPlayerNotification(player, context);

        // 3. 管理员端广播 (全面接入 i18n 多语言)
        if (notifyAdminEnabled) {
            com.example.argusac.i18n.I18n.broadcastStaff(
                    "punishment.setback-admin",
                    "player", player.getName(),
                    "check", checkName,
                    "mode", mode,
                    "vl", currentVl,
                    "old_loc", fromLocStr,
                    "new_loc", toLocStr
            );
        }

        // 4. 内部审计日志记录
        ViolationLog log = new ViolationLog(
                player.getName(),
                player.getUniqueId(),
                checkName,
                currentVl,
                details,
                true,
                mode,
                fromLocStr,
                toLocStr,
                usedFallback
        );
        int maxLogs = plugin.getConfig().getInt("settings.logs.max-per-player", 50);
        context.addLog(log, maxLogs);

        com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData pData = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (pData != null) {
            pData.addLog(log, maxLogs);
        }

        // 5. 提交统一结构化日志至 AuditLogManager
        com.example.argusac.log.model.CoordPoint fromPt = com.example.argusac.log.model.CoordPoint.fromLocation(player.getLocation(), false);
        LegitimateLocation targetLegit = context.getLastLegitimateLocation();
        com.example.argusac.log.model.CoordPoint toPt = targetLegit != null ? com.example.argusac.log.model.CoordPoint.fromLocation(targetLegit.toLocation(), false) : null;
        com.example.argusac.log.model.LogSeverity severity = (currentVl >= 15)
                ? com.example.argusac.log.model.LogSeverity.HIGH
                : (currentVl >= 5 ? com.example.argusac.log.model.LogSeverity.MEDIUM : com.example.argusac.log.model.LogSeverity.LOW);

        com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry entry = new com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry.Builder(
                com.example.argusac.log.model.LogType.VIOLATION,
                plugin.getAuditLogManager().getServerId()
        )
                .player(player.getName())
                .uuid(player.getUniqueId())
                .check(checkName)
                .vl(currentVl)
                .severity(severity)
                .reason(details)
                .mode(mode)
                .from(fromPt)
                .to(toPt)
                .fallbackUsed(usedFallback)
                .build();
        plugin.getAuditLogManager().submitLog(entry);
    }

    private void sendPlayerNotification(Player player, PlayerSetbackContext context) {
        if ("NONE".equalsIgnoreCase(notifyPlayerChannel)) {
            return;
        }

        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (now - context.getLastNotifyTimestamp() < notifyPlayerCooldownMs) {
            return;
        }
        context.setLastNotifyTimestamp(now);

        // 全面接入 i18n 国际化多语言系统
        if ("CHAT".equalsIgnoreCase(notifyPlayerChannel)) {
            com.example.argusac.i18n.I18n.sendPrefixed(player, "punishment.setback-notify");
        } else {
            com.example.argusac.i18n.I18n.sendActionBar(player, "punishment.setback-notify");
        }
    }

    public void recordFailure(Player player, PlayerSetbackContext context, String checkName, String reason, String origLoc) {
        SetbackFailureLog failLog = new SetbackFailureLog(
                player.getName(),
                player.getUniqueId(),
                checkName,
                reason,
                origLoc
        );
        context.addFailureLog(failLog, 20);

        com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData pData = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (pData != null) {
            pData.addFailureLog(failLog, 20);
        }

        if (plugin.getConfig().getBoolean("settings.debug", false)) {
            plugin.getLogger().warning(failLog.toString());
        }

        com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry failEntry = new com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry.Builder(
                com.example.argusac.log.model.LogType.SETBACK_FAIL,
                plugin.getAuditLogManager().getServerId()
        )
                .player(player.getName())
                .uuid(player.getUniqueId())
                .check(checkName)
                .reason(reason)
                .from(com.example.argusac.log.model.CoordPoint.fromLocation(player.getLocation(), false))
                .build();
        plugin.getAuditLogManager().submitLog(failEntry);
    }

    private void dispatchKick(Player player, String rawMessage) {
        Component kickReason = com.example.argusac.i18n.I18n.component(player, "punishment.kick-reason", "check", "Setback", "code", "OCCLUDED");
        if (kickReason == null) {
            kickReason = ColorUtil.toComponent(rawMessage);
        }
        final Component finalKick = kickReason;
        if (!Bukkit.isPrimaryThread()) {
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> player.kick(finalKick));
        } else {
            player.kick(finalKick);
        }
    }

    private String determineEffectiveMode(SetbackOverride pOverride, String checkMode) {
        if (pOverride == SetbackOverride.SOFT_ONLY) {
            return "SOFT";
        }
        if (pOverride == SetbackOverride.HARD_ONLY) {
            return "HARD";
        }
        if (checkMode != null && !checkMode.isEmpty()) {
            return checkMode;
        }
        return defaultMode;
    }

    // ==============================================================================
    // 集中上下文访问与诊断运维 API
    // ==============================================================================

    public PlayerSetbackContext getContext(UUID uuid) {
        return contextMap.computeIfAbsent(uuid, k -> new PlayerSetbackContext());
    }

    public boolean isImmune(UUID uuid) {
        PlayerSetbackContext context = contextMap.get(uuid);
        return context != null && context.isImmune();
    }

    public long getImmunityRemaining(UUID uuid) {
        PlayerSetbackContext context = contextMap.get(uuid);
        if (context == null) return 0L;
        return Math.max(0L, context.getImmunityUntil() - System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    public SetbackOverride getOverride(UUID uuid) {
        PlayerSetbackContext context = contextMap.get(uuid);
        return context != null ? context.getOverride() : SetbackOverride.DEFAULT;
    }

    public void setOverride(UUID uuid, SetbackOverride override) {
        getContext(uuid).setOverride(override);
    }

    public LegitimateLocation getLastLocation(UUID uuid) {
        PlayerSetbackContext context = contextMap.get(uuid);
        return context != null ? context.getLastLegitimateLocation() : null;
    }

    public LegitimateLocation getPreviousLocation(UUID uuid) {
        PlayerSetbackContext context = contextMap.get(uuid);
        return context != null ? context.getPreviousLegitimateLocation() : null;
    }

    public List<SetbackFailureLog> getFailureLogs(UUID uuid) {
        PlayerSetbackContext context = contextMap.get(uuid);
        return context != null ? context.getFailureLogs() : Collections.emptyList();
    }

    public List<ViolationLog> getSetbackLogs(UUID uuid) {
        PlayerSetbackContext context = contextMap.get(uuid);
        return context != null ? context.getLogs() : Collections.emptyList();
    }

    public boolean isSelfTeleport(UUID playerUuid) {
        return playerUuid != null && selfTeleportingPlayers.contains(playerUuid);
    }

    public boolean isGlobalEnabled() {
        return globalEnabled;
    }

    public void setGlobalEnabled(boolean globalEnabled) {
        this.globalEnabled = globalEnabled;
        plugin.getConfig().set("setback.global-enabled", globalEnabled);
        plugin.saveConfig();
    }

    public String getDefaultMode() {
        return defaultMode;
    }

    public void clearPlayerCooldown(UUID uuid) {
        PlayerSetbackContext context = contextMap.get(uuid);
        if (context != null) {
            context.setLastNotifyTimestamp(0L);
        }
    }

    public void cleanup() {
        contextMap.clear();
        selfTeleportingPlayers.clear();
    }

    // ==============================================================================
    // 待同步网络校正数据模型 (PendingCorrection)
    // ==============================================================================

    public static class PendingCorrection {
        private final double targetX;
        private final double targetY;
        private final double targetZ;
        private final UUID worldUid;
        private final long expireTime;

        public PendingCorrection(double targetX, double targetY, double targetZ, UUID worldUid, long expireTime) {
            this.targetX = targetX;
            this.targetY = targetY;
            this.targetZ = targetZ;
            this.worldUid = worldUid;
            this.expireTime = expireTime;
        }

        public boolean isExpired() {
            return System.currentTimeMillis() > expireTime;
        }

        public boolean matchesCorrection(Location loc) {
            if (loc == null || loc.getWorld() == null || isExpired()) {
                return false;
            }
            if (!loc.getWorld().getUID().equals(worldUid)) {
                return false;
            }
            double dx = loc.getX() - targetX;
            double dy = loc.getY() - targetY;
            double dz = loc.getZ() - targetZ;
            return (dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) <= 0.25; // 距离落点在 0.5 格内视为校正到位
        }
    }

    // ==============================================================================
    // 集中化玩家回退状态模型 (PlayerSetbackContext)
    // ==============================================================================

    public static class PlayerSetbackContext {
        private volatile LegitimateLocation lastLegitimateLocation;
        private volatile LegitimateLocation previousLegitimateLocation;
        private volatile SetbackOverride override = SetbackOverride.DEFAULT;
        private volatile long lastSetbackTimestamp = 0L;
        private volatile long immunityUntil = 0L;
        private volatile long lastNotifyTimestamp = 0L;

        // P1: 短时验证移动快照队列
        private final Deque<LegitimateLocation> snapshotHistory = new ConcurrentLinkedDeque<>();

        // P2: 按检测名称细分的冷却时间戳映射
        private final Map<String, Long> lastSetbackByCheck = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

        // P1: 待确认网络坐标校正
        private volatile PendingCorrection pendingCorrection = null;

        private final Deque<ViolationLog> logs = new ConcurrentLinkedDeque<>();
        private final Deque<SetbackFailureLog> failureLogs = new ConcurrentLinkedDeque<>();

        public void update(LegitimateLocation location, int maxCapacity, long maxAgeMs) {
            this.previousLegitimateLocation = this.lastLegitimateLocation;
            this.lastLegitimateLocation = location;

            snapshotHistory.addFirst(location);
            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
            while (snapshotHistory.size() > maxCapacity) {
                snapshotHistory.removeLast();
            }
            while (!snapshotHistory.isEmpty() && (now - snapshotHistory.peekLast().getTimestamp() > maxAgeMs * 2)) {
                snapshotHistory.removeLast();
            }
        }

        public void update(LegitimateLocation location) {
            update(location, 30, 2500L);
        }

        public void setLastLegitimateLocation(LegitimateLocation location) {
            this.lastLegitimateLocation = location;
            if (location != null) {
                snapshotHistory.addFirst(location);
            }
        }

        /**
         * P1: 从快照队列检索同世界、时效有效、距离有界且可通行的最优合法快照
         * 对齐 Grim 机制：地面稳定态优先策略 (Ground Preference)，优先让玩家安全落地方块表面，避免悬空下滑
         */
        public LegitimateLocation findBoundedLegitimateLocation(UUID worldUid, Location currentLoc, double maxDistance, long maxAgeMs) {
            if (worldUid == null || currentLoc == null) {
                return null;
            }
            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
            LegitimateLocation firstPassable = null;

            for (LegitimateLocation snap : snapshotHistory) {
                if (snap.getWorldUid() == null || !snap.getWorldUid().equals(worldUid)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (now - snap.getTimestamp() > maxAgeMs) {
                    continue;
                }
                Location snapLoc = snap.toLocation();
                if (snapLoc == null) {
                    continue;
                }
                double dist = MathUtil.getHorizontalDistance(snapLoc, currentLoc);
                if (dist > maxDistance) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (snap.isPassable()) {
                    // 若处于稳固地面，作为第一优先级立即采纳返回
                    if (snap.isOnGround()) {
                        return snap;
                    }
                    if (firstPassable == null) {
                        firstPassable = snap;
                    }
                }
            }
            return firstPassable;
        }

        /**
         * 安全降级：在同世界且限定距离内查找最近的可通行快照
         */
        public LegitimateLocation findNearestPassableLocation(UUID worldUid, Location currentLoc, double maxDistance) {
            if (worldUid == null || currentLoc == null) {
                return null;
            }
            LegitimateLocation best = null;
            double bestDist = Double.MAX_VALUE;

            for (LegitimateLocation snap : snapshotHistory) {
                if (snap.getWorldUid() == null || !snap.getWorldUid().equals(worldUid)) {
                    continue;
                }
                Location snapLoc = snap.toLocation();
                if (snapLoc == null) {
                    continue;
                }
                double dist = MathUtil.getHorizontalDistance(snapLoc, currentLoc);
                if (dist <= maxDistance && dist < bestDist && snap.isPassable()) {
                    best = snap;
                    bestDist = dist;
                }
            }
            return best;
        }

        public void reset(Location loc, boolean inVehicle, UUID vehicleUid) {
            LegitimateLocation reset = LegitimateLocation.from(loc, new Vector(0, 0, 0), true, inVehicle, vehicleUid);
            this.lastLegitimateLocation = reset;
            this.previousLegitimateLocation = reset;
            this.snapshotHistory.clear();
            if (reset != null) {
                this.snapshotHistory.addFirst(reset);
            }
            this.pendingCorrection = null;
        }

        public void resetPreviousOnly() {
            this.previousLegitimateLocation = null;
        }

        public void clear() {
            this.lastLegitimateLocation = null;
            this.previousLegitimateLocation = null;
            this.snapshotHistory.clear();
            this.pendingCorrection = null;
        }

        public boolean isImmune() {
            return System.currentTimeMillis() < immunityUntil;
        }

        public LegitimateLocation getLastLegitimateLocation() {
            return lastLegitimateLocation;
        }

        public LegitimateLocation getPreviousLegitimateLocation() {
            return previousLegitimateLocation;
        }

        public SetbackOverride getOverride() {
            return override;
        }

        public void setOverride(SetbackOverride override) {
            this.override = (override != null) ? override : SetbackOverride.DEFAULT;
        }

        public long getLastSetbackTimestamp() {
            return lastSetbackTimestamp;
        }

        public void setLastSetbackTimestamp(long lastSetbackTimestamp) {
            this.lastSetbackTimestamp = lastSetbackTimestamp;
        }

        public long getLastSetbackTimestamp(String checkName) {
            return lastSetbackByCheck.getOrDefault(checkName, 0L);
        }

        public void setLastSetbackTimestamp(String checkName, long timestamp) {
            this.lastSetbackByCheck.put(checkName, timestamp);
            this.lastSetbackTimestamp = timestamp;
        }

        public long getImmunityUntil() {
            return immunityUntil;
        }

        public void setImmunityUntil(long immunityUntil) {
            this.immunityUntil = immunityUntil;
        }

        public long getLastNotifyTimestamp() {
            return lastNotifyTimestamp;
        }

        public void setLastNotifyTimestamp(long lastNotifyTimestamp) {
            this.lastNotifyTimestamp = lastNotifyTimestamp;
        }

        public PendingCorrection getPendingCorrection() {
            return pendingCorrection;
        }

        public void setPendingCorrection(PendingCorrection pendingCorrection) {
            this.pendingCorrection = pendingCorrection;
        }

        public void clearPendingCorrection() {
            this.pendingCorrection = null;
        }

        public boolean hasPendingCorrection() {
            PendingCorrection pc = this.pendingCorrection;
            return pc != null && !pc.isExpired();
        }

        public boolean matchesCorrection(Location loc) {
            PendingCorrection pc = this.pendingCorrection;
            return pc != null && pc.matchesCorrection(loc);
        }

        public void addLog(ViolationLog log, int maxLogs) {
            logs.addLast(log);
            while (logs.size() > maxLogs) {
                logs.pollFirst();
            }
        }

        public List<ViolationLog> getLogs() {
            return new ArrayList<>(logs);
        }

        public void addFailureLog(SetbackFailureLog log, int maxLogs) {
            failureLogs.addLast(log);
            while (failureLogs.size() > maxLogs) {
                failureLogs.pollFirst();
            }
        }

        public List<SetbackFailureLog> getFailureLogs() {
            return new ArrayList<>(failureLogs);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/manager/ViolationManager.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.manager;

import com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin;
import com.example.argusac.api.Check;
import com.example.argusac.data.PlayerData;
import com.example.argusac.model.ViolationLog;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.scheduler.BukkitTask;

/**
 * 违规事务综合调度管理器 (ViolationManager)
 * 统一处理违规判定、VL增加、衰减任务、回退执行、警报分发及惩罚执行
 */
public class ViolationManager {

    private final ArgusACPlugin plugin;
    private BukkitTask decayTask;

    public ViolationManager(ArgusACPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.startDecayTask();
    }

    /**
     * 常规违规处理入口 (无 MoveEvent 上下文)
     */
    public void handleViolation(Player player, Check check, int vlAmount, String details) {
        handleViolation(player, check, vlAmount, details, null);
    }

    /**
     * 核心违规处理方法 (支持传入 PlayerMoveEvent 用于 SOFT 模式精准取消)
     *
     * @param player 违规玩家
     * @param check 触发的检测模块
     * @param vlAmount 增加的 VL 点数
     * @param details 违规细节数据
     * @param moveEvent 移动事件 (可为 null)
     */
    public void handleViolation(Player player, Check check, int vlAmount, String details, PlayerMoveEvent moveEvent) {
        if (player == null || check == null) {
            return;
        }

        // 检查 bypass 权限及创造模式全局豁免
        if (plugin.isPlayerExempt(player)) {
            return;
        }

        PlayerData data = plugin.getPlayerManager().getPlayerData(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }

        // 1. 拆分回退网络校正豁免与全局检测盲区：仅在移动检测项处于回退坐标校正时豁免位移累加，并记录豁免日志
        if (check.getType() == com.example.argusac.api.CheckType.MOVEMENT && data.hasLegalVelocity(com.example.argusac.model.VelocitySourceType.SETBACK_CORRECTION)) {
            com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry graceEntry = new com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry.Builder(
                    com.example.argusac.log.model.LogType.VIOLATION,
                    plugin.getAuditLogManager().getServerId()
            )
                    .player(player.getName())
                    .uuid(player.getUniqueId())
                    .check(check.getName())
                    .vl(data.getVl(check.getName()))
                    .severity(com.example.argusac.log.model.LogSeverity.LOW)
                    .reason("[GRACE_EXEMPTED] " + details)
                    .mode("GRACE_EXEMPTED")
                    .from(com.example.argusac.log.model.CoordPoint.fromLocation(player.getLocation(), false))
                    .build();
            plugin.getAuditLogManager().submitLog(graceEntry);
            return;
        }

        // 2. 累加违规等级
        int currentVl = data.addVl(check.getName(), vlAmount);

        // 3. 区分检测类别进行处置：
        // 移动违规 (MOVEMENT)：调用 SetbackManager 执行回退或原地取消
        // 战斗/方块违规 (COMBAT/WORLD)：由各 Check 取消非法交互事件，此处仅记录日志并触发升级惩戒，绝不传送
        boolean defaultPunishmentTriggered = false;
        String logMode = "LOG_ONLY";

        if (check.getType() == com.example.argusac.api.CheckType.MOVEMENT) {
            if (plugin.getSetbackManager().shouldSetback(player, check, currentVl)) {
                defaultPunishmentTriggered = plugin.getSetbackManager().mitigate(
                        player, check, currentVl, details, moveEvent
                );
            } else if (plugin.getSetbackManager().isCheckOnCooldown(player.getUniqueId(), check.getName())) {
                // P2 改法：处于回退冷却期间连续违规，若存在移动事件，优先取消当前非法移动，防止作弊者借冷却缝隙位移
                if (moveEvent != null && moveEvent.getFrom() != null) {
                    moveEvent.setTo(moveEvent.getFrom());
                    defaultPunishmentTriggered = true;
                    logMode = "COOLDOWN_CANCEL";
                } else {
                    logMode = "COOLDOWN_SKIP";
                }
            }
        } else {
            // 战斗、方块及数据包类违规，处置方式为取消非法交互并记录日志
            logMode = "CANCEL_INTERACTION";
        }

        // 4. 若未被 SetbackManager 内部日志覆盖，在此记录统一结构化违规日志
        if (!defaultPunishmentTriggered) {
            com.example.argusac.log.model.LogSeverity severity = (currentVl >= check.getMaxVl())
                    ? com.example.argusac.log.model.LogSeverity.HIGH
                    : (currentVl > check.getMaxVl() / 2 ? com.example.argusac.log.model.LogSeverity.MEDIUM : com.example.argusac.log.model.LogSeverity.LOW);

            com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry entry = new com.example.argusac.log.model.LogEntry.Builder(
                    com.example.argusac.log.model.LogType.VIOLATION,
                    plugin.getAuditLogManager().getServerId()
            )
                    .player(player.getName())
                    .uuid(player.getUniqueId())
                    .check(check.getName())
                    .vl(currentVl)
                    .severity(severity)
                    .reason(details)
                    .mode(logMode)
                    .from(com.example.argusac.log.model.CoordPoint.fromLocation(player.getLocation(), false))
                    .build();

            plugin.getAuditLogManager().submitLog(entry);

            // 兼顾向后兼容内存缓存
            int maxLogs = plugin.getConfig().getInt("settings.logs.max-per-player", 50);
            ViolationLog log = new ViolationLog(
                    player.getName(),
                    player.getUniqueId(),
                    check.getName(),
                    currentVl,
                    details,
                    false,
                    logMode,
                    "-",
                    "-",
                    false
            );
            data.addLog(log, maxLogs);
        }

        // 若开启临时模块调试，写入独立调试日志
        plugin.getCheckDebugLogger().log(check.getName(), player.getName(), "VL:" + currentVl + " " + details);

        // 4. 发送管理员警报
        plugin.getAlertManager().sendAlert(player, check.getName(), currentVl, check.getMaxVl(), details);

        // 5. 校验执行二级升级惩罚 (Tier 2 Escalated Punishment: 严重恶意作弊指令惩罚，如 Kick/Ban)
        plugin.getPunishmentManager().checkAndPunish(player, check.getName(), currentVl);
    }

    /**
     * 启动 VL 周期性自动衰减任务
     */
    public void startDecayTask() {
        if (decayTask != null) {
            decayTask.cancel();
        }

        int intervalSeconds = plugin.getConfig().getInt("settings.vl-decay.interval-seconds", 15);
        int amount = plugin.getConfig().getInt("settings.vl-decay.amount", 1);
        long intervalTicks = Math.max(20L, intervalSeconds * 20L);

        decayTask = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimerAsynchronously(plugin, () -> {
            for (PlayerData data : plugin.getPlayerManager().getAllData()) {
                data.decayVl(amount);
            }
        }, intervalTicks, intervalTicks);
    }

    public void stopDecayTask() {
        if (decayTask != null) {
            decayTask.cancel();
            decayTask = null;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/model/LegitimateLocation.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.model;

import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * 玩家最后合法位置数据模型 (LegitimateLocation)
 * <p>
 * 【核心设计原则】：
 * 回退目标是玩家最后一个通过验证的位置，不是安全位置。
 * 安全位置是复活点或传送落点的概念，不属于反作弊回退。
 * <p>
 * 本类只记录玩家通过反作弊移动验证时的瞬时合法物理状态，
 * 采用不可变数据模型设计，严格避免长期持有 org.bukkit.World 强引用。
 */
public final class LegitimateLocation {

    private final double x;
    private final double y;
    private final double z;
    private final float yaw;
    private final float pitch;
    private final double velocityX;
    private final double velocityY;
    private final double velocityZ;
    private final boolean onGround;
    private final UUID worldUid;
    private final String worldName;
    private final long timestamp;
    private final boolean inVehicle;
    private final UUID vehicleUid;

    public LegitimateLocation(double x, double y, double z,
                              float yaw, float pitch,
                              double velocityX, double velocityY, double velocityZ,
                              boolean onGround,
                              UUID worldUid, String worldName,
                              long timestamp,
                              boolean inVehicle, UUID vehicleUid) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.z = z;
        this.yaw = yaw;
        this.pitch = pitch;
        this.velocityX = velocityX;
        this.velocityY = velocityY;
        this.velocityZ = velocityZ;
        this.onGround = onGround;
        this.worldUid = worldUid;
        this.worldName = worldName != null ? worldName : "";
        this.timestamp = timestamp;
        this.inVehicle = inVehicle;
        this.vehicleUid = vehicleUid;
    }

    /**
     * 快捷静态构造方法：从 Bukkit 实体状态创建合法位置
     */
    public static LegitimateLocation from(Location loc, Vector velocity, boolean onGround,
                                          boolean inVehicle, UUID vehicleUid) {
        if (loc == null || loc.getWorld() == null) {
            return null;
        }
        double vx = (velocity != null) ? velocity.getX() : 0.0;
        double vy = (velocity != null) ? velocity.getY() : 0.0;
        double vz = (velocity != null) ? velocity.getZ() : 0.0;

        return new LegitimateLocation(
                loc.getX(), loc.getY(), loc.getZ(),
                loc.getYaw(), loc.getPitch(),
                vx, vy, vz,
                onGround,
                loc.getWorld().getUID(),
                loc.getWorld().getName(),
                System.currentTimeMillis(),
                inVehicle,
                vehicleUid
        );
    }

    /**
     * 还原为 Bukkit Location 实例
     */
    public Location toLocation() {
        World world = null;
        if (worldUid != null) {
            world = Bukkit.getWorld(worldUid);
        }
        if (world == null && !worldName.isEmpty()) {
            world = Bukkit.getWorld(worldName);
        }
        if (world == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return new Location(world, x, y, z, yaw, pitch);
    }

    /**
     * 还原为运动速度向量
     */
    public Vector toVelocity() {
        return new Vector(velocityX, velocityY, velocityZ);
    }

    /**
     * 边界可用性检测：使用跨版本兼容的完整玩家碰撞箱 (0.6x1.8) 检查目标空间是否可通行
     * 替代粗糙的 isOccluding 判定，正确识别栅栏、铁栅栏、台阶、楼梯、门等有碰撞箱的非 occluding 方块
     *
     * @return 若未被外部实体方块阻挡/窒息则返回 true
     */
    public boolean isPassable() {
        Location loc = toLocation();
        if (loc == null || loc.getWorld() == null) {
            return false;
        }
        World world = loc.getWorld();
        int chunkX = loc.getBlockX() >> 4;
        int chunkZ = loc.getBlockZ() >> 4;
        if (!world.isChunkLoaded(chunkX, chunkZ)) {
            return false;
        }

        // 构建标准玩家物理碰撞体 (宽度 0.6，高度 1.8，内缩 0.01 避免贴地/贴墙浮点误差)
        com.example.argusac.compat.CompatBoundingBox playerBox = new com.example.argusac.compat.CompatBoundingBox(
                x - 0.29, y + 0.01, z - 0.29,
                x + 0.29, y + 1.79, z + 0.29
        );

        return com.example.argusac.compat.VersionAdapterFactory.getAdapter().isSpacePassable(world, playerBox);
    }

    public double getX() {
        return x;
    }

    public double getY() {
        return y;
    }

    public double getZ() {
        return z;
    }

    public float getYaw() {
        return yaw;
    }

    public float getPitch() {
        return pitch;
    }

    public double getVelocityX() {
        return velocityX;
    }

    public double getVelocityY() {
        return velocityY;
    }

    public double getVelocityZ() {
        return velocityZ;
    }

    public boolean isOnGround() {
        return onGround;
    }

    public UUID getWorldUid() {
        return worldUid;
    }

    public String getWorldName() {
        return worldName;
    }

    public long getTimestamp() {
        return timestamp;
    }

    public boolean isInVehicle() {
        return inVehicle;
    }

    public UUID getVehicleUid() {
        return vehicleUid;
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return String.format("[%s] %.2f, %.2f, %.2f (yaw:%.1f, pitch:%.1f)", worldName, x, y, z, yaw, pitch);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/model/SetbackFailureLog.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.model;

import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * 回退失败审计日志模型 (SetbackFailureLog)
 */
public class SetbackFailureLog {

    private static final SimpleDateFormat TIME_FORMAT = new SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm:ss");

    private final String playerName;
    private final UUID playerUuid;
    private final String checkName;
    private final String failureReason;
    private final String originalLocation;
    private final long timestamp;

    public SetbackFailureLog(String playerName, UUID playerUuid, String checkName,
                             String failureReason, String originalLocation) {
        this.playerName = playerName;
        this.playerUuid = playerUuid;
        this.checkName = checkName;
        this.failureReason = failureReason;
        this.originalLocation = originalLocation;
        this.timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    public String getPlayerName() {
        return playerName;
    }

    public UUID getPlayerUuid() {
        return playerUuid;
    }

    public String getCheckName() {
        return checkName;
    }

    public String getFailureReason() {
        return failureReason;
    }

    public String getOriginalLocation() {
        return originalLocation;
    }

    public long getTimestamp() {
        return timestamp;
    }

    public String getFormattedTime() {
        return TIME_FORMAT.format(new Date(timestamp));
    }

    public String toJson() {
        return String.format(
                "{\"type\":\"SETBACK_FAILURE\",\"time\":%d,\"player\":\"%s\",\"uuid\":\"%s\",\"check\":\"%s\",\"reason\":\"%s\",\"origLoc\":\"%s\"}",
                timestamp, playerName, playerUuid.toString(), checkName, failureReason, originalLocation
        );
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return String.format("[%s] [FAIL] %s 触发 %s 回退失败 - 原因: %s (原目标: %s)",
                getFormattedTime(), playerName, checkName, failureReason, originalLocation);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/model/ViolationLog.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.model;

import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * 违规与回退审计记录模型 (ViolationLog)
 * 记录违规者、触发检查项、违规等级、回退模式、前后坐标与是否启用兜底位置
 */
public class ViolationLog {

    private static final SimpleDateFormat DATE_FORMAT = new SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm:ss");

    private final String playerName;
    private final UUID playerUuid;
    private final String checkName;
    private final int vl;
    private final String details;
    private final boolean setbackExecuted;
    private final String setbackMode;
    private final String fromLocation;
    private final String toLocation;
    private final boolean usedFallback;
    private final long timestamp;

    public ViolationLog(String playerName, UUID playerUuid, String checkName, int vl, String details,
                        boolean setbackExecuted, String setbackMode,
                        String fromLocation, String toLocation, boolean usedFallback) {
        this.playerName = playerName;
        this.playerUuid = playerUuid;
        this.checkName = checkName;
        this.vl = vl;
        this.details = details;
        this.setbackExecuted = setbackExecuted;
        this.setbackMode = setbackMode != null ? setbackMode : "NONE";
        this.fromLocation = fromLocation != null ? fromLocation : "-";
        this.toLocation = toLocation != null ? toLocation : "-";
        this.usedFallback = usedFallback;
        this.timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    public String getPlayerName() {
        return playerName;
    }

    public UUID getPlayerUuid() {
        return playerUuid;
    }

    public String getCheckName() {
        return checkName;
    }

    public int getVl() {
        return vl;
    }

    public String getDetails() {
        return details;
    }

    public boolean isSetbackExecuted() {
        return setbackExecuted;
    }

    public String getSetbackMode() {
        return setbackMode;
    }

    public String getFromLocation() {
        return fromLocation;
    }

    public String getToLocation() {
        return toLocation;
    }

    public boolean isUsedFallback() {
        return usedFallback;
    }

    public long getTimestamp() {
        return timestamp;
    }

    public String getFormattedTime() {
        return DATE_FORMAT.format(new Date(timestamp));
    }

    /**
     * 生成供落盘使用的标准 JSON 字符串
     */
    public String toJson() {
        return String.format(
                "{\"type\":\"VIOLATION\",\"time\":%d,\"player\":\"%s\",\"uuid\":\"%s\",\"check\":\"%s\",\"vl\":%d,\"details\":\"%s\",\"setback\":%b,\"mode\":\"%s\",\"from\":\"%s\",\"to\":\"%s\",\"usedFallback\":%b}",
                timestamp, playerName, playerUuid != null ? playerUuid.toString() : "",
                checkName, vl, details.replace("\"", "\\\""),
                setbackExecuted, setbackMode, fromLocation, toLocation, usedFallback
        );
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        String setbackInfo = "";
        if (setbackExecuted) {
            String fallbackTag = usedFallback ? " (已启用前一位置兜底)" : "";
            setbackInfo = String.format(" [回退(%s): %s -> %s%s]", setbackMode, fromLocation, toLocation, fallbackTag);
        }
        return String.format("[%s] %s 触发 %s (VL: %d) - %s%s",
                getFormattedTime(), playerName, checkName, vl, details, setbackInfo);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/util/ColorUtil.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.util;

import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.serializer.legacy.LegacyComponentSerializer;

/**
 * 颜色与文本工具类
 * 兼容经典 Minecraft 颜色代码以及 Paper Adventure Component API
 */
public final class ColorUtil {

    private ColorUtil() {}

    /**
     * 将带有 & 颜色代码的字符串转换为标准颜色字符串
     */
    public static String colorize(String message) {
        if (message == null) {
            return "";
        }
        // 使用 Adventure LegacyComponentSerializer 转换，避免依赖已废弃的 ChatColor
        return LegacyComponentSerializer.legacyAmpersand()
                .serialize(LegacyComponentSerializer.legacyAmpersand().deserialize(message));
    }

    /**
     * 将经典颜色代码字符串转换为 Paper Adventure Component
     */
    public static Component toComponent(String message) {
        if (message == null) {
            return Component.empty();
        }
        return LegacyComponentSerializer.legacyAmpersand().deserialize(message);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/com/example/argusac/util/MathUtil.java`:

```java
package com.example.argusac.util;

import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.Collection;

/**
 * 高性能数学计算工具类
 * 提供距离、角度、方差等计算，规避不必要的开方运算以提升性能
 */
public final class MathUtil {

    private MathUtil() {}

    /**
     * 计算水平方向 (X, Z) 的欧氏距离
     */
    public static double getHorizontalDistance(Location from, Location to) {
        if (from == null || to == null || from.getWorld() != to.getWorld()) {
            return 0.0;
        }
        double dx = to.getX() - from.getX();
        double dz = to.getZ() - from.getZ();
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
    }

    /**
     * 计算水平方向平方距离，避免 Math.sqrt 降低 CPU 开销
     */
    public static double getHorizontalDistanceSquared(Location from, Location to) {
        if (from == null || to == null || from.getWorld() != to.getWorld()) {
            return 0.0;
        }
        double dx = to.getX() - from.getX();
        double dz = to.getZ() - from.getZ();
        return dx * dx + dz * dz;
    }

    /**
     * 计算三维欧氏距离平方
     */
    public static double getDistance3DSquared(Location from, Location to) {
        if (from == null || to == null || from.getWorld() != to.getWorld()) {
            return 0.0;
        }
        return from.distanceSquared(to);
    }

    /**
     * 计算玩家视线向量与目标朝向向量之间的夹角（度数，零对象分配高频优化）
     */
    public static double getAngleBetweenVectors(Vector eyeDir, Vector targetDir) {
        double lenSq1 = eyeDir.lengthSquared();
        double lenSq2 = targetDir.lengthSquared();
        if (lenSq1 == 0.0 || lenSq2 == 0.0) {
            return 0.0;
        }
        double dot = eyeDir.dot(targetDir) / (Math.sqrt(lenSq1) * Math.sqrt(lenSq2));
        // 限制在 [-1.0, 1.0] 防止精度误差产生 NaN
        dot = Math.max(-1.0, Math.min(1.0, dot));
        return Math.toDegrees(Math.acos(dot));
    }

    /**
     * 计算数值集合的样本标准差（用于 AutoClicker 检测连点器机械一致性）
     */
    public static double getStandardDeviation(Collection<? extends Number> values) {
        if (values == null || values.size() <= 1) {
            return 999.0;
        }
        double sum = 0.0;
        for (Number val : values) {
            sum += val.doubleValue();
        }
        double mean = sum / values.size();
        double varianceSum = 0.0;
        for (Number val : values) {
            double diff = val.doubleValue() - mean;
            varianceSum += diff * diff;
        }
        return Math.sqrt(varianceSum / (values.size() - 1));
    }
}

```

```yml
# ==============================================================================
# ArgusAC - Core Configuration File
# Supports Spigot / Paper 1.12.2 ~ 1.21.x Multi-Version Architecture, Powered by Java 17
# ==============================================================================

# ==============================================================================
# Language & Internationalization (i18n) Configuration
# ==============================================================================
language:
  # Default language code used for server console output, system logs, and global broadcasts.
  # Corresponds to filenames in the lang/ directory (lowercase without .yml extension, e.g. zh_cn).
  server-language: "en_us"

  # Client fallback language:
  # Used when a player's client locale cannot be detected, or when their language file is missing (e.g. en_us, zh_cn).
  client-default-language: "en_us"

  # Whether to automatically adapt messages based on each player's client language (true / false).
  # If false, all players receive messages in server-language.
  auto-client-language: true

# ==============================================================================
# Cross-Version Adaptive Compatibility Matrix
# ==============================================================================
compatibility-matrix:
  # Minimum allowed server version (plugin safely disables below this version to protect server stability)
  minimum-allowed-version: "1.12.2"

  # Degradation policy when legacy server APIs lack certain modern features:
  # true  = Graceful degradation (missing features fall back to mathematical/geometric approximation)
  # false = Strict mode (directly disables checks that require native modern APIs)
  allow-feature-degradation: true

  # Whether to conspicuously notify admins in console and in /Argus status when modules are degraded
  notify-admins-on-degradation: true

  # Module-level version overrides and compatibility switches
  module-overrides:
    elytra-fly:
      auto-disable-below-1-9: true

# ==============================================================================

  bypass-creative: true
```
