    interval-seconds: 15     # Decay interval in seconds
    amount: 1                # VL deducted per decay cycle
  # Violation log storage settings
  logs:
    max-per-player: 50       # Maximum log entries retained per player

# ==============================================================================
# Alert Notification System
# ==============================================================================
alerts:
  enabled: true
  # Alert format: {player}=Player, {check}=Check, {vl}=VL, {max_vl}=Threshold, {details}=Data, {ping}=Ping
  format: "&c[!] &e{player} &7failed &b{check} &8(VL: &c{vl}&8/&7{max_vl}&8) &7- &f{details} &8[&7{ping}ms&8]"
  # Play alert sound to staff with ArgusAC.alert permission
  sound:
    enabled: true
    type: "BLOCK_NOTE_BLOCK_BASS"
    volume: 1.0
    pitch: 1.2
  # Alert cooldown (ms) to prevent message spam from rapid violations
  cooldown-ms: 1000

# ==============================================================================
# Core Mechanism: Last Known Valid Location Setback System
# [Default Mitigation]: As the first line of defense, setbacks are enabled by default!
# Reverses invalid movement and pulls cheating players back to their last safe location.
# ==============================================================================
setback:
  # Master toggle (true by default as the primary active mitigation mechanism)
  global-enabled: true

  # Default setback execution mode:
  # SOFT: Smoothly overrides movement destination inside PlayerMoveEvent without jarring rubberbands
  # HARD: Schedules synchronous teleport on main thread, canceling velocity and fall distance
  default-mode: "HARD"

  # Cooldown between setbacks (ms) to prevent high-frequency jitter or network overload
  cooldown-ms: 350

  # Default minimum Violation Level (VL) required to trigger a setback (1 = immediate setback on violation)
  default-min-vl: 1

  # World exemption list (players in these worlds will not be set back)
  exempted-worlds: []

  # Boundary safety & bounded setback distance (prevents unconstrained cross-region teleportation)
  boundary:
    # Maximum horizontal distance allowed for a valid setback target snapshot (blocks)
    # If all verified snapshots exceed this distance, safe fallback cancels the current movement rather than teleporting far away
    max-distance: 8.0
    # Whether to disconnect the player if both current and fallback safe positions are blocked by solid blocks
    kick-when-both-blocked: false
    kick-message: "&c[ArgusAC] Irregular movement detected and previous location obstructed. Disconnected safely."

  # Verified movement snapshot buffer (short sliding window for bounded, same-world setback selection)
  snapshot:
    # Maximum lifespan (ms) of a verified movement snapshot
    max-age-ms: 2500
    # Maximum number of verified snapshots cached per player
    max-capacity: 30

  # Network correction synchronization window (differentiates server setback correction from normal checks)
  correction:
    # Timeout in ms for the client to acknowledge and correct to the server-issued setback position
    timeout-ms: 600

  # Post-setback temporary immunity window (tightened to prevent false flags while eliminating 1.5s blind spots)
  immunity:
    # Base immunity duration in milliseconds (actual immunity = max(min-duration, 2 * ping + extra-buffer))
    min-duration-ms: 400
    extra-buffer-ms: 150

  # Notification settings
  notifications:
    player:
      # Notification channel: ACTIONBAR, CHAT, or NONE
      channel: "ACTIONBAR"
      message: "&c[ArgusAC] Irregular movement detected, your position has been setback!"
      # Notification cooldown (ms) to avoid spam
      cooldown-ms: 1000
    admin:
      enabled: true
      format: "&6[Setback] &e{player} &7set back by &c{check} &8(&f{old_loc} &8-> &a{new_loc}&8) &7[&b{mode}&7]"

  # Asynchronous disk audit logging (persisted as JSON Lines, one JSON object per line)
  disk-logging:
    enabled: true
    path: "plugins/ArgusAC/logs/setbacks.jsonl"

  # Per-check setback overrides (differentiated by check categories):
  # Movement checks: Trigger positional setback (HARD / SOFT) or in-place move cancellation
  # Combat & World checks: Handled by cancelling interactions & escalating punishments (never teleporting)
  per-check:
    # --- Movement Checks (Setback Enabled) ---
    Speed:
      enabled: true
      min-vl: 1
      mode: "HARD"
    Fly:
      enabled: true
      min-vl: 1
      mode: "HARD"
    NoFall:
      enabled: true
      min-vl: 1
      mode: "HARD"
    Velocity:
      enabled: true
      min-vl: 1
      mode: "HARD"
    InventoryMove:
      enabled: true
      min-vl: 1
      mode: "SOFT" # Prioritizes canceling the current invalid move in-place
    ElytraFly:
      enabled: true
      min-vl: 3
      mode: "HARD"

    # --- Combat Checks (Interaction Cancellation Only, No Setback Teleport) ---
    Reach:
      enabled: false
      min-vl: 1
      mode: "HARD"
    KillAura:
      enabled: false
      min-vl: 1
      mode: "HARD"
    AutoClicker:
      enabled: false
      min-vl: 1
      mode: "HARD"

    # --- World & Block Checks (Block Event Cancellation Only, No Setback Teleport) ---
    FastBreak:
      enabled: false
      min-vl: 1
      mode: "HARD"
    Nuker:
      enabled: false
      min-vl: 1
      mode: "HARD"
    Scaffold:
      enabled: false
      min-vl: 1
      mode: "HARD"

    # --- Packet Checks (Packet Interception Only) ---
    BadPackets:
      enabled: false
      min-vl: 1
      mode: "HARD"

# ==============================================================================
# Punishment System
# Architecture: Two-Tier Punishment Mechanism
# 1. Tier 1 Default Punishment: Setback Mechanism!
#    Serves as the primary active defense line. Once violation is confirmed (VL >= 1),
#    immediately pulls the player back and resets motion momentum.
# 2. Tier 2 Escalated Punishment: Console Command Actions.
#    Triggers automated console commands (e.g. kick/ban) only when a player persists in
#    cheating and accumulates a high VL threshold.
# ==============================================================================
punishments:
  enabled: true

  # Default mitigation configuration: using Setback as first-line action
  default-action:
    setback-as-default: true
    min-vl: 1

  # Escalated console punishment commands (triggered only at high VL thresholds)
  # Supported variables: {player}=Player name, {check}=Check name, {vl}=Current VL
  actions:
    - vl: 25
      command: "kick {player} [ArgusAC] Continuous irregular client behavior detected."
    # Example ban rule (uncomment to enable):
    # - vl: 50
    #   command: "ban {player} 7d [ArgusAC] Persistent cheating behavior detected"

# ==============================================================================
# Detection Module Threshold Configurations
# ==============================================================================
checks:
  # 1. Speed Check
  Speed:
    enabled: true
    max-vl: 15
    # Maximum allowed horizontal distance per tick (sprinting/jumping is ~0.62, includes potion & ice tolerances)
    max-horizontal-speed: 0.65
    # Additional velocity multiplier for ice/packed ice friction
    ice-modifier: 1.45
    # Speed potion bonus multiplier per potion amplifier level
    speed-potion-multiplier: 0.20

  # 2. Fly Check
  Fly:
    enabled: true
    max-vl: 12
    # Maximum allowed air tick count without collision, elytra, or flight ability
    max-air-ticks: 25
    # Illegal upwards ascent threshold without jump boost effects
    vertical-ascent-threshold: 0.42

  # 3. NoFall Check (Spoofed onGround packets)
  NoFall:
    enabled: true
    max-vl: 15
    # Tolerance distance when client declares onGround=true but no solid blocks are beneath
    ground-check-tolerance: 0.001

  # 4. Reach Check (Combat distance)
  Reach:
    enabled: true
    max-vl: 15
    # Maximum base reach distance in blocks for Survival mode
    max-reach: 3.10
    # Maximum reach threshold with ping compensation and knockback margins
    max-ping-reach: 3.50

  # 5. AutoClicker Check (Click speed and regularity)
  AutoClicker:
    enabled: true
    max-vl: 15
    # Maximum allowed clicks per second (CPS)
    max-cps: 18
    # Minimum standard deviation threshold (detects mechanical uniformity from macro/autoclickers)
    min-std-deviation: 4.5

  # 6. KillAura Check (Combat angles and raytracing)
  KillAura:
    enabled: true
    max-vl: 18
    # Maximum allowed field-of-view angle (degrees) away from target when attacking
    max-angle: 68.0
    # Check for solid block obstruction between attacker eyes and target eyes
    raytrace-blocks: true
    # Maximum target-switching interval in milliseconds for rapid aura attacks
    target-switch-ms: 80

  # 7. Velocity Check (Knockback modification / Anti-KB)
  Velocity:
    enabled: true
    max-vl: 15
    # Expected vertical knockback ratio (values below this threshold indicate anti-knockback)
    vertical-ratio: 0.85
    # Expected horizontal knockback ratio
    horizontal-ratio: 0.80

  # 8. FastBreak Check
  FastBreak:
    enabled: true
    max-vl: 15
    # Percentage threshold for digging faster than theoretical duration (0.20 = 20% faster)
    time-leniency: 0.20

  # 9. Nuker Check (Mass block breaking)
  Nuker:
    enabled: true
    max-vl: 12
    # Maximum blocks broken per tick (vanilla clients break at most 1 per tick)
    max-breaks-per-tick: 2
    # Maximum blocks broken per second without Haste II
    max-breaks-per-second: 15

  # 10. Scaffold Check (Automated bridging)
  Scaffold:
    enabled: true
    max-vl: 15
    # Minimum downward pitch angle when placing blocks beneath feet
    min-pitch: 70.0
    # Tolerance delay (ms) for rapid consecutive downward backwards placement
    fast-placement-delay-ms: 120

  # 11. InventoryMove Check
  InventoryMove:
    enabled: true
    max-vl: 10
    # Maximum allowed distance moved while viewing inventory or container screens
    max-distance-while-open: 0.15

  # 12. BadPackets Check
  BadPackets:
    enabled: true
    max-vl: 10
    # Strict pitch constraint: pitch must remain within [-90.0, 90.0] (prevents Derp / HeadRoll)
    strict-pitch: true
    # Check for duplicate movement packets while ascending
    check-duplicate-move: true

  # 13. ElytraFly Check
  # Analyzes elytra aerodynamics to detect hovering, unauthorized propulsion, timer, and no-fall exploits
  ElytraFly:
    enabled: true
    max-vl: 15

    # Exemption and physical environment parameters
    exemptions:
      # Firework rocket boost grace window (ms)
      firework-boost-duration-ms: 1500
      # Riptide trident grace window (ms)
      riptide-duration-ms: 2000
      # External velocity grace window for explosions, pistons, and wind charges (ms)
      external-velocity-duration-ms: 1200
      # Takeoff grace duration during initial jump glide (ms)
      takeoff-grace-duration-ms: 1000
      # Minimum server TPS threshold before relaxing strict checks
      min-server-tps: 17.5

    # Sub-Check 1: Gliding Speed Limit (ElytraSpeed)
    speed:
      enabled: true
      # Maximum horizontal speed in unboosted level flight (blocks/tick, vanilla is ~1.35)
      max-unboosted-speed: 1.45
      # Maximum allowed horizontal speed during rocket propulsion (blocks/tick)
      max-boosted-speed: 2.60
      # Dive speed multiplier based on downward viewing angle (pitch > 0)
      pitch-dive-multiplier: 0.55
      min-vl: 3
      setback-mode: "HARD"

    # Sub-Check 2: Unpropelled Ascent (ElytraAscend)
    ascend:
      enabled: true
      # Maximum vertical ascent speed per tick without fireworks, riptide, or bubble columns
      max-vertical-ascent: 0.15
      # Consecutive ascent tick threshold (tolerates 1-2 ticks of network interpolation jitter)
      ascend-ticks-threshold: 4
      min-vl: 3
      setback-mode: "HARD"

    # Sub-Check 3: Mid-Air Hovering (ElytraHover)
    hover:
      enabled: true
      # Maximum horizontal speed threshold for hovering (blocks/tick)
      max-horizontal-speed: 0.08
      # Maximum vertical speed threshold for hovering (blocks/tick)
      max-vertical-speed: 0.05
      # Consecutive hovering ticks required to trigger violation
      hover-ticks-threshold: 15
      min-vl: 3
      setback-mode: "HARD"

    # Sub-Check 4: Firework Acceleration Alignment (ElytraFirework)
    firework:
      enabled: true
      # Minimum unauthorized forward acceleration without firework rocket consumption
      min-unpropelled-accel: 0.12
      # Consecutive ticks sustaining theoretical maximum velocity without fireworks
      continuous-high-speed-ticks: 50
      min-vl: 3
      setback-mode: "HARD"

    # Sub-Check 5: Glide State Consistency (ElytraGlideState)
    glide-state:
      enabled: true
      # Validate whether chestplate elytra has depleted durability (durability 0 cannot glide)
      check-chestplate-durability: true
      min-vl: 1
      setback-mode: "HARD"

    # Sub-Check 6: Tick Rate Timer Anomaly (ElytraTimer)
    timer:
      enabled: true
      # Sliding window sample size (average interval across last N move packets)
      sample-window-size: 20
      # Minimum average interval lower bound (ms, normal is ~50ms per tick)
      min-average-interval-ms: 44.0
      min-vl: 3
      setback-mode: "SOFT"

    # Sub-Check 7: Rapid State Toggling (ElytraFlip)
    flip:
      enabled: true
      # Sliding statistical window in milliseconds
      window-ms: 1000
      # Maximum allowed glide state toggles within the statistical window
      max-toggles: 4
      min-vl: 3
      setback-mode: "SOFT"

    # Sub-Check 8: Ground Proximity Fall Damage Exploit (ElytraNoFall)
    nofall:
      enabled: true
      # Fall distance threshold prior to touching ground (blocks)
      min-fall-distance: 3.0
      # Proximity threshold to solid ground beneath feet (blocks)
      ground-proximity-distance: 1.8
      min-vl: 3
      setback-mode: "HARD"

`src/main/resources/plugin.yml`:

```yml
name: ArgusAC
version: '${project.version}'
main: com.example.argusac.ArgusACPlugin
api-version: '1.13'
author: haha
description: Industrial-grade high-performance cross-version anti-cheat plugin (ArgusAC), supporting 1.12.2 - 1.21.x
softdepend:
  - ProtocolLib

commands:
  argus:
    description: ArgusAC main command
    aliases:
      - ac
      - argusac
      - aegis
    usage: /<command> [help|reload|vl|logs|toggle|setback|alerts]

permissions:
  argusac.admin:
    description: Allows the use of all ArgusAC admin commands
    default: op
  argusac.alert:
    description: Allows receiving anti-cheat alerts and managing session alert preferences
    default: op
  argusac.bypass:
    description: Bypasses anti-cheat checks and setbacks
    default: false

  # Legacy aliases for backwards compatibility
  aegisac.admin:
    description: Legacy permission alias for argusac.admin
    default: op
  aegisac.alert:
    description: Legacy permission alias for argusac.alert
    default: op
  aegisac.bypass:
    description: Legacy permission alias for argusac.bypass
    default: false
```
